Extensive experiments across challenging spatial reasoning benchmarks show that PEPS consistently improves reasoning accuracy while substantially reducing refinement rounds, API cost, and end-to-end latency over strong tool-integrated baselines. These results demonstrate that explicit requirement commitment can make tool-integrated spatial reasoning not only more reliable, but also more execution-efficient.

</details>


### [37] [DUGO: Dual-Scale Gaussian Memory for Online Temporal Action Localization in Egocentric Procedural Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41402)
*Mohammed Aman Bhuiyan,Md. Sazzad Hossain Adib,Samiul Basir Bhuiyan,Mir Faiyaz Hossain,Syed Mohammed Shamsul Islam,Md Zakir Hossain,Shafin Rahman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DUGO提出双尺度时序编码器（深度可分离局部表示+全局自注意力）与高斯记忆库（输入条件化的随机高斯原型+门控交叉注意力），用于在线时序动作定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线时序动作定位（OnTAL）需在无未来帧、不可回溯修正的严格实时约束下检测动作，现有方法仅依赖短时锚点上下文或单尺度历史帧记忆，难以刻画多时间尺度展开的动作与长程推理。

Method: 双尺度时序编码器：深度可分离卷积式局部表征与全局自注意力并行融合；高斯记忆库：将动作记忆建模为随机、输入条件化的高斯原型，并在编码器与锚点查询两个阶段施加门控交叉注意力以读写记忆。

Result: 在EGTEA、EPIC-Kitchens-100等程序性第一人称数据集上达到SOTA，并在非第一人称基准Muses上有明显提升。

Conclusion: 双尺度编码+概率式记忆是提升在线流式动作定位长程建模能力的有效方案，且具备跨数据集域的迁移性。

Abstract: Online Temporal Action Localization (OnTAL) aims to detect and localize action instances from untrimmed streaming videos in real time. Unlike offline approaches, OnTAL models have no access to future frames and cannot go back and refine earlier predictions, which makes the task considerably harder given the strict real-time constraints and narrow temporal window available. Most existing OnTAL methods lean on either brief anchor-based context or a single-scale memory of past frames, which limits how well they can capture actions unfolding across multiple time scales or requiring long-range reasoning in Procedural Videos. To address these gaps, we present DUGO (Dual-Scale Encoding and Gaussian Memory Online), a new OnTAL framework that jointly models local motion dynamics and long-range temporal dependencies. At its core, DUGO uses a Dual-Scale Temporal Encoder, pairing depth-wise local representations with global self-attention so it can pick up both fine-grained temporal patterns and broader contextual cues. We also introduce a Gaussian Memory Bank, which represents action memory as stochastic, input-conditioned Gaussian prototypes, allowing for richer temporal memory through gated cross-attention applied at both the encoder and anchor-query stages. Extensive experiments on procedural egocentric datasets (EGTEA, EPIC-Kitchens-100) demonstrate that DUGO achieves state-of-the-art performance while also providing substantial improvements on non-egocentric benchmarks such as Muses. The project page is available at: this https URL

</details>


### [38] [SACQ: Structure-Aware Codebook Quantization for Ultra-Low-Bit Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41408)
*Haesung Lee,Insu Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SACQ：面向VLA模型超低比特仅权重量化的向量码本框架，结合结构感知码本拟合、动作感知混合比特分配与两阶段量化后精修，在GR00T N1.7上以1.5bit实现LIBERO平均93.85%成功率、内存降4.83倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型内存占用大，难以部署在资源受限机器人平台；从LLM继承的PTQ配方在超低比特下失效，且现有低比特方法未显式建模下游算子结构，也未考虑量化对策略动作输出的影响，导致精度分配不优。

Method: 三项组合：(1) 结构感知码本拟合，采用按角色区分的二次度量，反映输入统计特性与下游敏感度；(2) 动作感知的混合比特分配，依据逐层压缩引起的最终动作误差增量决定各层比特；(3) 两阶段量化后精修——以二阶误差补偿重分配码字索引，并针对解码后动作校准码本与行组缩放。

Result: GR00T N1.7在四个LIBERO套件上，语言/动作权重1.5bit平均成功率93.85%，内存降低4.83倍；叠加INT8激活量化与低精度注意力后保持93.55%，较BF16加速1.51倍。SimplER上2bit下Visual Matching 63.76%、Variant Aggregation 52.27%（BF16为58.90%/53.45%）。

Conclusion: 量化VLA权重时显式引入策略动作误差与算子结构约束，可在1.5–2bit极低比特下基本保持甚至部分场景超越BF16的策略性能，为机器人端侧部署提供可行压缩路径；但以仿真基准评测为主，尚缺真实机器人平台验证。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models enable capable robot behavior, but their large memory footprint limits deployment on resource-constrained robotic platforms. Weight quantization is a natural remedy, yet post-training quantization (PTQ) recipes inherited from large language models degrade when applied to VLAs at ultra-low precision. Existing low-bit PTQ methods often fail to explicitly account for the structure of downstream operations and allocate precision without considering its effect on the policy actions. We introduce SACQ, an ultra- low-bit weight-only vector-codebook quantization framework for VLAs. SACQ combines (1) structure-aware codebook fitting with role-specific quadratic metrics that reflect input statistics and downstream sensitivity; (2) action-aware mixed-bit allocation based on the increase in final-action error as individual layers are compressed; and (3) two-stage post-quantization refinement that reassigns codeword indices with second-order error compensation and calibrates codebooks and row-group scales against the decoded action. Using GR00T N1.7, SACQ achieves 93.85% average success across four LIBERO suites at 1.5 bits per language/action weight while reducing model memory by 4.83x. With INT8 activation quantization and low-precision attention, it retains 93.55% success while running 1.51x faster than BF16. On SimplER, SACQ further achieves 63.76% and 52.27% success under Visual Matching and Variant Aggregation at 2 bits per language/action weight, compared to 58.90% and 53.45% for BF16, respectively.

</details>


### [39] [FlexReader: Towards Adaptive Decoding Paths for Efficient Arbitrary-Length Scene Text Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41426)
*Zhibin Ma,Pengwen Dai,Zhigang Yao,Xugong Qin,Wenqi Ren,Xiaochun Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlexReader 提出一种支持多解码步长的场景文本识别模型，通过子串感知模块与并行解码块实现任意步长解码，并引入自适应前缀解码按置信度动态选择步长，兼顾精度与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 STR 模型的训练范式将其与特定解码策略绑定，部署时无法在不同识别场景间灵活切换精度-效率权衡，缺乏灵活性。

Method: 1) Sub-string Perception 模块从视觉特征中感知目标子串；2) 多个并行解码块独立解码子串中每个字符，支持子串范围内任意步长解码；3) Adaptive Prefix Decoding 按每步字符置信度自适应选择解码步长，寻找每个输入的最优解码路径。

Result: 在长文本、弯曲文本、遮挡等多类基准上表现稳健，识别精度持续优于现有解码策略，相比自回归解码最高获得 6 倍解码加速。

Conclusion: 单一 STR 模型即可通过可变解码步长实现跨数据集的精度-效率最优权衡，为部署灵活性提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Existing Scene Text Recognition (STR) models are inherently coupled with a specific decoding strategy by their training paradigms, limiting their flexibility in deployment across diverse recognition scenarios. In this paper, we propose FlexReader, an STR model that supports multiple decoding strides, enabling a single model to achieve an optimal accuracy-efficiency trade-off across different datasets. Specifically, we first introduce a Sub-string Perception module to perceive the target sub-string from visual features, followed by multiple parallel decoding blocks that independently decode each character in the sub-string. During inference, FlexReader supports decoding with arbitrary strides within the perceived sub-string range. Furthermore, we propose Adaptive Prefix Decoding, which adaptively selects the decoding stride at each step based on the model's confidence in the decoded characters, thereby identifying the optimal decoding path for each input. Experimental results demonstrate that FlexReader exhibits robust recognition capability across a diverse range of benchmarks, including those featuring long text, curved text, and occlusions. It consistently outperforms existing decoding strategies in recognition accuracy while achieving up to 6-flod decoding speedup over autoregressive decoding.

</details>


### [40] [Beyond Trial-and-Error: A Geospatial-Guided Active Reasoning Framework for Robust Aerial Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41446)
*Jiawei Ge,Wenkang Gong,Jiuxin Cao,Bo Liu,Lei Cui,Haoran Wang,Xuelin Zhu,Biwei Cao,Chang Liu,Chen Feng,Ioannis Patras*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoReasoner 将空中视觉语言导航从"反应式试错"转为"地理空间引导的主动推理"，用预搜索地理先验约束目标假设空间，再以假设驱动探索主动采集判别性证据，并以一致性感知验证防止过早/错误确认目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中 VLN 中目标小而视觉歧义大，现有方法沿用室内 VLN 的反应式范式：仅靠局部观测被动累积证据更新状态，忽视了导航中稳定的地理空间结构，导致视觉相似干扰物使错误目标假设被反复自我强化，定位不可靠。

Method: 三阶段框架：(1) 预搜索地理空间推理，生成区域级先验并约束目标假设空间；(2) 假设驱动探索，主动获取判别性证据以精化竞争的候选目标假设；(3) 一致性感知目标验证，在停止前对抗式评估互补证据源，抑制过早或错误确认。

Result: 在真实场景 CityNav 基准上取得零样本空中 VLN 方法的新 SOTA，并可与有监督方法相竞争。

Conclusion: 显式利用稳定的地理空间先验，并把搜索过程重构为假设检验式的主动证据获取，可有效缓解空中 VLN 中的感知歧义与错误自强化问题。

Abstract: Aerial Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) requires UAVs to locate language-specified goals in large-scale urban environments, where objects often appear small and visually ambiguous from high-altitude viewpoints. Existing methods largely follow a reactive paradigm common in indoor VLN, repeatedly updating navigation states from local observations while underutilizing the geospatial structure that remains stable during navigation. As a result, visually similar distractors can cause a reactive goal search to repeatedly reinforce locally plausible goal hypotheses based on passively accumulated evidence, leading to unreliable localization. To address this, we propose GeoReasoner, a robust framework that shifts the paradigm of Aerial VLN from reactive trial-and-error to geospatial-guided active reasoning. Specifically, GeoReasoner first performs pre-search geospatial reasoning to derive region-level priors and constrain the goal hypothesis space. Building on these, it further conducts hypothesis-driven exploration to actively acquire discriminative evidence and refine competing goal hypotheses. Finally, consistency-aware goal verification adversarially assesses complementary evidence sources before stopping, hence reducing premature or erroneous goal confirmations. Experiments on the real-world CityNav benchmark demonstrate that GeoReasoner sets a new SOTA among zero-shot Aerial VLN methods while competing favorably with supervised approaches.

</details>


### [41] [BDNet: Fast-slow thinking is all you need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41485)
*Chenzhi Zheng,Zhuoran Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BDNet 提出层级分类主干：用低秩投影+通道缩放构成的基线形变算子(BDO)作用于特征相对阶段参考的残差，复杂度线性于 token 数，四个冻结参考缓冲累积训练期统计量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索"慢参考"统计机制能否在保持简单张量实现的前提下提升紧凑表示的鲁棒性，并明确区分其与输入相关注意力的差异。

Method: 四阶段金字塔(depth 2,2,6,2；width 96,192,384,768)，20.70M 参数、224×224 输入；BDO 对特征偏离阶段特定参考的偏差做低秩投影与学习通道缩放；参考缓冲训练中累积 detach 统计、推理期冻结；无需成对 token 相似度计算。

Result: 在 ImageNet-1K 上开展干净分类、八项受控消融，以及高斯噪声、对比度、亮度扰动实验；摘要未给出具体精度数值。

Conclusion: 将训练期统计参考机制与输入依赖注意力区分开，作为紧凑分类主干的一种鲁棒性改进思路，但缺乏定量结果与代码/开源信息。

Abstract: We present BDNet, a hierarchical image-classification backbone built around fast image features and slow statistical references. Its baseline deformation operator (BDO) transforms the deviation of a feature from a stage-specific reference through a low-rank projection and a learned channel scale. Rather than computing pairwise token similarity, the branch operates directly on baseline-relative residuals with complexity linear in the token count. Each of four reference buffers accumulates detached stage statistics during training and remains frozen at inference. A four-stage pyramid with depths $(2,2,6,2)$ and widths $(96,192,384,768)$ yields a 20.70M-parameter classifier at $224\times224$ resolution. We organize an ImageNet-1K study around clean classification, eight controlled ablations, and Gaussian-noise, contrast, and brightness perturbations. The central hypothesis is that a slow reference can improve the robustness of a compact representation while retaining a simple tensor implementation. We distinguish this training-time statistical mechanism from input-dependent attention and make its linear inference behavior explicit.

</details>


### [42] [VariaFlow: Uncertainty-Aware Flow Matching for Unsupervised Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41494)
*Arnela Hadzic,Martin Urschler,Cosmin I. Bercea*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VariaFlow 用无条件 flow matching 先验下的掩码引导修复集成来估计逐位置预测方差，并用残差除以该方差进行归一化，从而区分真实病灶与健康解剖变异，实现跨域脑 MRI 异常检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重建式异常检测的瓶颈已从重建质量转移到残差打分：扫描与伪健康重建的残差同时包含真实病灶和健康个体间的解剖变异，二者不可区分。对单一重建打分会把两者都当作异常，在解剖变异大的区域产生大量假阳性，同时小病灶被淹没在重建误差中。作者指出缺失的关键量是逐位置的预测方差，即健康重建在该位置可能的变化幅度。

Method: 在有监督掩码引导的修复框架下，采用无条件 flow matching 先验，用不同噪声初始化生成多次修复结果构成集成，估计每个位置的预测标准差；再以该标准差归一化残差得到异常分数。使用少步 flow matching 控制集成的计算成本，并在两个空间尺度上做修复以覆盖不同大小的病灶。仅在健康解剖上训练一次。

Result: 在三个脑 MRI 数据集（卒中与肿瘤）上，单次训练即跨域泛化，AUROC 分别达到 0.94、0.80、0.78（均为最高），在两个跨域数据集上取得最高 AUPRC，比最强基线分别高出 37% 和 50%。在域偏移下，该基线 AUPRC 最多下降 52%，而 VariaFlow 更稳定；三个数据集上小病灶 Dice 均为最佳。

Conclusion: 将以集成方式估计的预测方差引入重建残差归一化，是一种简洁有效的异常打分方式，可提升跨域泛化与小病灶灵敏度，并缓解解剖变异导致的假阳性问题。

Abstract: Reconstruction-based anomaly detection no longer fails at reconstruction. Generative models produce high-quality pseudo-healthy reconstructions, and the remaining difficulty is the step of scoring residuals. The residual between a scan and its reconstruction has two indistinguishable sources, genuine pathology and the anatomical variation that separates one healthy brain from another. Scoring against a single reconstruction treats both as anomalous, inflating false positives where healthy anatomy varies most while small or subtle lesions are lost in the surrounding reconstruction error. We identify the missing quantity as the predictive variance, how much a healthy reconstruction can vary at each location. We introduce VariaFlow, which estimates this variance from an ensemble of mask-guided restorations under an unconditional flow matching prior, each started from a different noise initialization, and which normalizes the residual by the resulting standard deviation. Few-step flow matching keeps the ensemble computationally affordable, and restoring at two spatial scales captures lesions of different size. Across three brain MRI datasets spanning stroke and tumor, VariaFlow is trained once on healthy anatomy and generalizes across all three, attaining the highest AUROC (0.94, 0.80, 0.78) on each and the highest AUPRC on both cross-domain datasets, exceeding the strongest baseline by 37% and 50%. Under domain shift, the same baseline drops by up to 52% in AUPRC, whereas VariaFlow stays more stable and achieves the best small-lesion Dice on all three datasets.

</details>


### [43] [VoxLution: Evolving Voxels for High-Fidelity 3D Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41501)
*Daoyi Gao,Xinyi Zhang,Alexey Bokhovkin,Lei Li,Angela Dai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VoxLution：基于Transformer的由粗到细分块稀疏体素序列建模，实现高分辨率3D室内场景生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式3D场景模型几何过度平滑、分辨率低，难以满足VR、空间计算与机器人仿真的沉浸式细节需求。

Method: 先合成低分辨率空间布局，再基于该结构逐块局部细化几何；核心是把3D超分表述为多分辨率稀疏体素网格上的序列建模问题，提出免训练、分辨率不变的tokenization（将8个八分体几何编码为单个十进制数），序列包含多分辨率层级以让粗布局引导细部生成，并将相邻块边界信息直接写入序列以保证块间连贯。

Result: 在比现有基线更细的体素分辨率下生成场景；3D-FRONT上FPD降低59.6%、FID降低51.1%；ScanNet++上FPD降低70.5%、FID降低73.8%。

Conclusion: 多分辨率稀疏体素序列建模可有效突破既有生成模型的分辨率与平滑性瓶颈，为高保真室内场景生成提供可扩展范式。

Abstract: High-resolution 3D indoor scene generation is crucial for immersive applications such as virtual reality, spatial computing, and robotics simulation. We introduce VoxLution, a coarse-to-fine transformer-based approach that generates high-fidelity indoor scenes with rich details, overcoming the overly smooth geometry typical of existing generative models. First, we generate a low-resolution spatial layout; then, based on this structural information, we progressively refine the scene geometry locally in chunks. Key to our approach is formulating 3D super-resolution as a sequence modeling problem over multi-resolution sparse voxel grids. To achieve this, we introduce a training-free, resolution-invariant tokenization scheme that compactly encodes the geometric information of eight octants into a single decimal number. We formulate the transformer sequence to include multiple resolutions, allowing the coarse layout to guide the generation of fine local details. To ensure coherence across local chunks, we incorporate adjacent chunk boundary information directly into the transformer sequencing. VoxLution achieves scene generation at a finer voxel resolution than existing baselines, demonstrating state-of-the-art performance on standard benchmarks with 59.6% lower FPD and 51.1% lower FID on 3D-FRONT, and 70.5% lower FPD and 73.8% lower FID on Scannet++.

</details>


### [44] [Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning of Transformer Encoders for Audio-Image-Symbolic Music Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41512)
*Eliseo Fuentes-Martinez,Antonio Hidalgo-Centeno,Jorge Calvo-Zaragoza,Antonio Javier Gallego*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文系统评估参数高效微调（PEFT，LoRA/QLoRA）在音频、乐谱图像、符号谱三类预训练音乐编码器上的效果，并检验单模态增益能否转化为多模态检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于打分的多模态音乐检索要求表征既具备模态内判别性、又具备跨模态兼容性；现有工作缺乏对"PEFT是否既能省钱又能提升多模态组织"的系统性证据。

Method: 在TriScore与Musical Theme Dataset上，比较全量微调、LoRA、QLoRA，覆盖9个预训练音频/图像/符号谱编码器；先用冻结编码器线性探测作参照做单模态监督分类筛选，再设计2×2受控实验（编码器适配×任务特定共享投影），对比投影前后的冻结与适配表征，并进行跨模态排除与邻域分析。

Result: 18个模态-任务组合中14个选择LoRA/QLoRA，常可匹敌或超越全量微调且参数量显著更少；但单模态增益向多模态分类的迁移不一致：TriScore作曲家任务中适配与投影互补，TriScore乐器及MTD乐器/合奏任务中大体冗余，MTD作曲家任务中部分干扰。适配+投影后的标题表征实现曲目级检索，Top-5/Top-10平均准确率55.32/64.68。

Conclusion: PEFT是替代全量微调的高效方案，但单模态判别性提升并不能保证多模态表征组织更优，需针对任务验证适配与投影的交互效应。

Abstract: Score-based multimodal music retrieval requires representations that are both discriminative within each modality and compatible across audio recordings, sheet music images, and symbolic scores. This paper investigates the effectiveness of parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) for adapting pretrained music encoders and examines whether modality-specific improvements translate into better multimodal representations. We compare full fine-tuning, LoRA, and QLoRA across nine pretrained audio, image, and symbolic score encoders on TriScore and the Musical Theme Dataset. Encoders are first evaluated and selected through supervised unimodal classification, using frozen-encoder linear probing as a reference. We then introduce a controlled 2x2 design crossing encoder adaptation with task-specific shared projection, comparing frozen and adapted representations before and after projection. LoRA or QLoRA is selected in 14 of 18 modality-target combinations, frequently matching or outperforming full fine-tuning while optimizing substantially fewer parameters. However, these unimodal gains do not transfer uniformly to multimodal classification. Adaptation and projection are complementary for composer classification on TriScore, largely redundant for TriScore instrument and the Musical Theme Dataset instrument and ensemble tasks, and partially interfering for composer classification on the Musical Theme Dataset. The final adapted-and-projected title representation on TriScore supports piece-level retrieval, reaching average Top-5 and Top-10 accuracies of 55.32 and 64.68, respectively. Cross-modal exclusion and neighborhood analyses further reveal strong modality-specific local structure in the projected spaces. Overall, PEFT provides an effective and computationally efficient alternative to full fine-tuning, but improved unimodal discrimination alone does not guarantee better multimodal organization.

</details>


### [45] [TACO: Trajectory-Aware Curvature Observation for Memorization Localization and Mitigation in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41520)
*Ruxi Gu,Zilei Wang,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TACO 沿扩散去噪后期轨迹聚合 prompt 相关的曲率响应并做潜空间引导的空间细化，得到记忆化定位图，作为缓解控制信号驱动空间重加权与局部引导修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像扩散模型会在局部区域复现训练记忆内容，精确定位是定向缓解的前提；但单步去噪状态的曲率观测含瞬态响应，而空间均匀的缓解又会破坏与 prompt 相关的内容。

Method: 实证发现记忆相关曲率在去噪后期显著，并在相邻状态间保持空间位置一致性。据此对晚期去噪轨迹上的 prompt 依赖曲率响应做聚合，再用潜空间引导的空间细化提升空间连贯性；所得记忆图同时作为定位信号与缓解的空间控制信号，通过空间重加权与局部化引导修正来引导现有推理时干预方法。

Result: 大量实验中 TACO 一致优于现有定位方法，并改善了记忆抑制、语义对齐与感知质量三者间的 Pareto 权衡。

Conclusion: 轨迹感知的空间分析可作为连接记忆定位与靶向缓解的有效桥梁。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models can reproduce memorized training content within localized image regions, making precise localization essential for targeted mitigation. However, curvature observations at a single denoising state can contain transient responses, while spatially uniform mitigation may disrupt prompt-relevant content. We present Trajectory-Aware Curvature Observation (TACO), a unified framework for local memorization detection and mitigation in diffusion models. Our empirical analysis reveals that memorization-related curvature becomes pronounced during late-stage denoising and persists at consistent spatial locations across neighboring states. Motivated by these observations, TACO aggregates prompt-dependent curvature responses along the late denoising trajectory and applies latent-guided spatial refinement to improve spatial coherence. The resulting memorization map serves both as a localization signal and as a spatial control signal for mitigation. Specifically, TACO uses this map to steer existing inference-time interventions toward memorization-related regions through spatial reweighting and localized guidance correction. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TACO consistently outperforms competing localization methods, and improves the Pareto trade-off between memorization suppression, semantic alignment, and perceptual quality. These results establish trajectory-aware spatial analysis as an effective link between memorization localization and targeted mitigation.

</details>


### [46] [Scout-Refine-Correct: Teaching Flow Models to Refine Their Own Samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41598)
*Sergey Karpukhin,Aibek Alanov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SRC（Scout-Refine-Correct）是一种训练配方：在采样每步让模型沿自身轨迹前瞻K步得到全样本估计，并将其回灌为自条件信号，可重复R轮；配合两个在实际rollout状态上施加的校正损失，使流匹配生成器获得真正的推理时精炼能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散/流匹配生成质量基本由训练算力决定，推理时唯一可调的是采样步数，且增益很快饱和。作者希望让生成器在推理阶段具备真正的迭代精炼能力，而非依赖外部验证器或奖励信号做best-of-N筛选。

Method: scout前瞻：每步沿自身轨迹向前rollout K步，外推全样本估计，作为自条件信号回灌后再走真实一步，可重复R轮精炼。训练时施加两个校正损失，且施加位置为模型自身rollout真正到达的状态而非仅真值路径上，以教会模型用该信号探索新样本、避免坍缩到次优解。消融显示近乎不增加参数量，无外部验证器与奖励信号。

Result: ImageNet-256上LightningDiT-B在NFE≈550时FID从14.5降至7.26，匹敌使用oracle FID验证器的特权best-of-N基线；与best-of-N组合后在NFE≈4400达FID≈3.81，说明两轴互补。消融覆盖scout信号的架构通路、前瞻视界、各校正损失与训练调度。

Conclusion: 推理时自精炼可通过训练配方内化到模型中，无需外部验证器或奖励信号，且参数开销可忽略；自精炼与外部采样筛选两类提升手段互补。为流匹配/扩散生成提供了新的性能调控维度。

Abstract: The quality of image generation models trained with diffusion or flow matching is largely fixed by the training budget: at inference, the only available lever is the number of sampling steps, and additional steps improve quality only up to a point. We present a training recipe, Scout-Refine-Correct (SRC), that instead gives a flow-matching generator a genuine inference-time refinement capability. At each sampling step the model takes a scout rollout -- $K$ steps ahead along its own trajectory -- extrapolates a full-sample estimate, and feeds that estimate back to itself as a self-conditioning signal before taking the real step. This can be repeated for $R$ refinement rounds. Two correction losses, applied during training at states the model's own rollouts actually reach rather than only on the ground-truth path, teach the model to use this signal to reach new samples instead of collapsing to a suboptimal one. On ImageNet-256, SRC improves LightningDiT-B from FID~14.5 to FID~7.26 at NFE~550, matching a privileged best-of-$N$ baseline that uses an oracle FID verifier to choose among samples. SRC uses no external verifier, no reward signal, and, after ablation, almost no extra parameters over the base architecture. Combining SRC with best-of-$N$ improves the result further, to FID~3.81 at NFE~4400, showing that the two axes are complementary. We ablate the main design choices -- the architectural pathway for the scout signal, the scout horizon, each correction loss, and the training schedule to demonstrate key working components of training.

</details>


### [47] [DrawSplat: Atlanta World-Guided Implicit Gaussian SLAM for Indoor Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41602)
*Fan Zhu,Zhisong Xu,Ziyu Chen,Zhenjun Zhao,Takayuki Okatani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DrawSplat: 以Atlanta World重力先验引导的点线联合跟踪 + 锚基隐式神经高斯表示，将结构平面转为对高斯中心/法向的可微正则，实现室内RGB-D SLAM的紧凑、高保真重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高斯SLAM仅用光度/深度/局部帧到模型约束优化高斯基元，在弱纹理、大平面、深度噪声、长轨迹的室内场景下易出现位姿漂移、表面厚度伪影、法向噪声与结构边界模糊。

Method: 模仿"绘画"流程：先用显式点线几何勾勒结构布局，再用隐式神经高斯填充外观与几何细节。点线联合跟踪估计相机位姿、稀疏几何与重力方向；利用Atlanta World先验的共享重力分量从跟踪线推断结构平面，并转化为对高斯中心与法向的可微正则项；结合锚基隐式神经高斯表示与自适应结构采样，将高斯容量分配给结构信息丰富区域。

Result: 多数据集实验显示，在基于高斯的SLAM系统中实现稳健跟踪、高保真渲染与较强几何重建，并在质量、效率与模型大小间取得实用平衡。

Conclusion: 结构先验（Atlanta World + 点线）与隐式神经高斯表示结合可有效抑制位姿漂移与表面伪影，并提升紧凑性；属室内RGB-D SLAM的增量改进。

Abstract: RGB-D SLAM requires accurate online tracking, high-quality rendering, and geometrically consistent reconstruction. Existing Gaussian SLAM methods mainly optimize Gaussian primitives with photometric, depth, or local frame-to-model constraints, which are often insufficient in indoor scenes with weak textures, large planar structures, noisy depth, and long trajectories. The resulting maps may suffer from pose drift, surface thickness artifacts, noisy normals, and blurred structural boundaries. We propose DrawSplat, an Atlanta World-guided implicit Gaussian SLAM framework for indoor reconstruction. DrawSplat follows a drawing-like process: explicit point-line geometry first sketches the structural layout, and implicit neural Gaussians then fill in high-fidelity appearance and continuous geometric details. It estimates camera poses, sparse geometry, and gravity direction through point-line joint tracking, infers structural planes from tracked lines using the shared-gravity component of the Atlanta World prior, and converts them into differentiable regularization terms on Gaussian centers and normals. To improve compactness and efficiency, DrawSplat further combines an anchor-based implicit neural Gaussian representation with adaptive structural sampling, which allocates Gaussian capacity to structurally informative regions. Experiments on multiple datasets show that DrawSplat achieves robust tracking, high-fidelity rendering, and strong geometric reconstruction among Gaussian-based SLAM systems, while maintaining a practical balance among quality, efficiency, and model size.

</details>


### [48] [Attention Maps Know When to Think: Training-Free Difficulty-Aware Chain-of-Thought for Reasoning Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41618)
*Chanseul Cho,Jaesung Jun,Dongheon Lee,Youngmin Ro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DA-CoT：用注意力图熵衡量输入难度，按需触发CoT，并用双向反思机制在失败时翻转决策，在推理分割上达到SOTA且提升效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT提示在MLLM中已成标准做法，但无差别使用会引入"过度思考"（overthinking），反而损害推理分割中查询意图与视觉定位的对齐。

Method: 训练免调的自适应框架：(1) 以模型注意力图熵作为难度信号——低熵表示定位确定，高熵表示不确定、值得启用CoT——决定是否触发CoT；(2) 引入双向反思（Bidirectional Rethinking）机制，初始输出失败时自动翻转推理决策，缓解过思与欠思。

Result: 在Reasoning Segmentation基准上取得SOTA，同时显著提升推理效率。

Conclusion: 自适应、按难度触发CoT可在不训练的前提下兼顾精度与效率，熵作为难度代理信号简洁有效；但仅涉及2D视觉语言推理分割，无具身/3D/机器人实证，属相邻方法性启发。

Abstract: Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting has become a standard reasoning strategy in Multi-modal Large Language Models (MLLMs), showing strong performance in complex vision-language tasks such as reasoning segmentation. However, our analysis reveals that indiscriminate CoT usage can lead to degraded performance by inducing unnecessary reasoning, a phenomenon we term overthinking. In reasoning segmentation, overthinking often leads to misalignment between the query's intent and visual grounding. To address this, we propose Difficulty-Aware Chain-of-Thought (DA-CoT), a training-free adaptive framework that selectively triggers CoT based on the input's intrinsic difficulty. We find that the entropy of the model's attention maps reliably reflects task difficulty: low entropy indicates confident, localized grounding, while high entropy suggests uncertainty and potential benefit from CoT. DA-CoT uses this entropy signal to determine whether to apply CoT prompting. Additionally, we introduce a Bidirectional Rethinking mechanism that enables the model to automatically flip its reasoning decision when the initial output fails, enhancing robustness by recovering from failures induced by overthinking and underthinking. DA-CoT demonstrates its effectiveness by achieving state-of-the-art performance on the Reasoning Segmentation benchmarks while significantly improving inference efficiency.

</details>


### [49] [GRIT: Gradient Masking for Visually Misgrounded Tokens in Text-Rich Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41629)
*Chanseul Cho,Noori Bae,Dongheon Lee,Youngmin Ro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出RLVR在多模态文本密集推理中的失效原因：模型常"答对但视觉定位错误"（CVMR），并提出GRIT框架，将视觉定位信号注入RLVR训练，同时发布GRIT-Bench基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR已被推广到视觉语言模型，但在富文本推理任务上效果有限。作者认为关键原因之一是实现标准答案的路径中视觉感知常常是错误的，而仅奖励最终答案正确性的RLVR无法区分"视觉对齐正确"与"视觉错配"的推理，从而同等强化两者。

Method: 提出CVMR现象的形式化定义；通过token级注意力分析发现CVMR token与良基（well-grounded）token呈现系统性不同的注意力模式；据此设计GRIT训练框架，在RLVR奖励/训练中引入视觉定位（grounding）信号以抑制CVMR；并构建GRIT-Bench以跨文本长度与推理深度系统评测富文本推理。

Result: 实验显示GRIT在推理准确率与定位保真度两方面均显著优于现有RLVR方法；token级注意力分析支持CVMR假设；GRIT-Bench覆盖不同文本长度与推理深度的评测维度。

Conclusion: 最终答案正确性不足以作为可靠的强化信号，显式建模视觉定位可提升VLM富文本推理的可靠性与可解释性；CVMR是可诊断、可缓解的失效模式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has recently been extended to vision-language models (VLMs), yet its effectiveness remains limited for text-rich reasoning tasks. We highlight a key factor contributing to this limitation: models frequently arrive at correct final answers despite misperceiving visual information during intermediate steps. We define this phenomenon as Correct-but-Visually-Misgrounded Reasoning (CVMR). Because standard RLVR rewards only final-answer correctness, it fails to differentiate between properly grounded and misgrounded reasoning, reinforcing both equally. Through token-level analysis, we reveal that CVMR tokens exhibit distinct attention patterns that differ systematically from well-grounded ones. Based on this observation, we propose GRIT, a training framework that incorporates visual grounding into RLVR to mitigate the impact of CVMR. Furthermore, to address the lack of text-rich reasoning benchmarks that allow for systematic evaluation across text length and reasoning depth, we introduce GRIT-Bench. Experiments demonstrate that GRIT significantly improves both reasoning accuracy and grounding fidelity over existing RLVR methods.

</details>


### [50] [TADP: Dual-Branch Temporally Adaptive Differential Privacy for Expression-Aware Video Anonymization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41642)
*Feng Xu,Xun Li,Lars Petersson,Yulei Sui,David Ahmedt-Aristizabal,Dadong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TADP：面向固定长度片段替换邻接的视频生成推理期隐私保护框架，双分支提取身份/表情表示，用时间自适应高斯机制加噪（噪声尺度依赖同片段内历史发布并有历史均匀校准下界），经自适应RDP组合转为(ε,δ)-DP，量化与时序处理作为后处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成在推理阶段会泄露人脸身份等敏感信息，需在固定长度片段替换邻接设定下提供形式化差分隐私保证，同时尽量保持表情识别效用与时序稳定。

Method: 双分支身份/表情表示提取；时间自适应高斯机制（噪声尺度仅依赖同片段先前已发布结果，并设历史均匀校准的最小下界）；自适应Rényi DP组合处理联合发布；将片段级RDP保证转为(ε,δ)-DP；软化量化与时序处理置于发布后作为DP后处理。

Result: 在面部表情数据集上，评估攻击下身份泄露减少，感知质量与时序稳定性可用，表情识别效用相对隐私保护基线较高；形式上满足片段级DP，经验上呈现有利的隐私-效用权衡。

Conclusion: TADP为推理期视频生成提供了可行的片段级DP方案，在评估威胁模型下实现较好的隐私-效用平衡；但缺乏真实机器人/物理AI场景验证，属通用隐私保护生成方向。

Abstract: We present TADP, a temporally adaptive differential privacy (DP) framework for inference-time privacy-preserving video generation under fixed-length clip-replacement adjacency. TADP uses a dual-branch design to extract identity and expression representations, clips and privatizes both branches with temporally adaptive Gaussian mechanisms whose noise scales depend only on preceding privatized releases within the same clip and are bounded below by history-uniform calibrated minima, and accounts for their joint release via adaptive Rényi Differential Privacy (RDP) composition. The clip-level RDP guarantee is converted to $(\varepsilon,\delta)$-differential privacy, while soft quantization and subsequent temporal processing are applied only after privatization as DP post-processing. Experiments on facial expression datasets show reduced identity leakage under evaluated attacks, usable perceptual quality and temporal stability, and high facial expression recognition utility relative to the evaluated privacy-preserving baselines. TADP formally satisfies clip-level DP and empirically provides a favorable privacy--utility trade-off under the evaluated threat models and datasets.

</details>


### [51] [When Depth Lies: Benchmarking Vision-Language Models on Mirror-Induced RGB-D Ambiguity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41651)
*Hao Yin,Tianchen Guo,Heming Du,Yan Ke,Yanbin Liu,Xin Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出镜像场景RGB-D数据集与MIRA-Bench基准（1,011张RGB-D图像、14,140 QA、8子任务/3认知层级），系统评测VLM在反射场景下的空间推理，发现深度信息反而降低性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLMs的多模态空间推理普遍假设深度图忠实反映物理距离，而镜面会扭曲深度读数却复现物体外观，使反射物体与真实物体难以区分；现有基准几乎不覆盖此类反射场景，VLM在此类场景中的能力未知。

Method: 采集525个真实室内外场景的1,011张RGB-D图像，覆盖6类反射面、昼夜两时段，标注反射面区域掩码及直接观测/镜面反射物体的掩码；据此构建MIRA-Bench，含8个子任务、14,140个QA对，组织为反射感知、空间推理、场景级决策三个认知层级，并对SOTA VLM进行评测。

Result: SOTA VLM在各任务上一致失败：无法判断物体是直接观测还是反射、无法将反射实例与真实对应物匹配、产生错误的空间推断；且提供深度数据反而使推理性能下降，说明模型无法识别镜面场景中的错误深度。

Conclusion: 镜面反射构成现有RGB-D空间推理范式的系统性盲区，单纯引入深度并非充分甚至有害；需在感知层面显式建模反射几何与深度有效性判定。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have made significant progress in spatial reasoning, particularly by integrating RGB images with depth data, which is often assumed to faithfully reflect physical distances. However, mirrors violate this assumption: they distort depth readings while reproducing object appearances, making reflections difficult to distinguish from real objects. As existing benchmarks rarely consider such reflective scenarios, VLM performance in mirror scenes remains largely unexplored. In this work, we introduce a mirror-centric RGB-D dataset from reflective scenes to evaluate the spatial reasoning capabilities of state-of-the-art VLMs in these scenarios. Specifically, we collect 1,011 RGB-D images from 525 real-world scenes, spanning diverse indoor and outdoor environments, six reflective surface types, and both daytime and nighttime conditions. All images are annotated with reflective surface region masks and mask annotations for both directly observed and mirror-reflected objects. Then, we curate a benchmark named MIRA-Bench (Mirror-Induced Reasoning Ambiguity Benchmark), comprising 14,140 QA pairs across eight sub-tasks organized into three cognitive levels: Reflection-aware Perception, Spatial Reasoning, and Scene-level Decision-making. We evaluate state-of-the-art VLMs on MIRA-Bench and find consistent failures across tasks: models misclassify whether objects are directly observed or reflected, fail to match reflected instances to their real-world counterparts, and produce erroneous spatial inferences. Remarkably, providing depth data actually degrades reasoning performance, implying that VLMs cannot identify incorrect depth in mirror scenes. The code and data are available at this https URL

</details>


### [52] [A Self-Supervised Pose Estimation Approach for Construction Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41673)
*Alaa Alshubbak,Daniel Görges*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出三阶段自监督（SimCLR 对比学习）框架：预训练→迁移学习→下游微调，用于挖掘机等重型工程机械的骨架姿态估计，无需第一阶段标注数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 姿态估计多依赖大规模标注数据，而工程机械领域标注成本高、数据稀缺；作者希望利用大量无标注图像（ACID）预训练，再迁移到小规模仿真数据与真实图像上。

Method: 三阶段模型：1）用 SimCLR 对比学习在 ACID 无标注数据集上自监督预训练；2）在 NVIDIA Isaac 与 MATLAB Simscape 仿真器生成的小数据集上做迁移学习微调；3）在下游任务（真实图像姿态估计）上微调；分别以 ResNet-50 与 HRNet-W32 为骨干。

Result: 相对普通自监督基线，真实图像上重型机械姿态估计精度分别提升 11%（ResNet-50）与 13%（HRNet-W32）；并验证了仅用原始 ACID 3.5% 数据即可完成知识迁移。

Conclusion: 对比学习预训练结合仿真数据迁移可显著缓解工程机械姿态估计的标注瓶颈，为少标签工业视觉任务提供可行方案。

Abstract: Pose estimation is a computer vision task used to estimate a skeleton of dynamic systems to predict future movements. Most of the research in this direction is based on a supervised learning approach which requires a massive amount of labeled datasets. In this paper, a self-supervised three-stage model based on a contrastive learning approach is introduced for estimating a skeleton of dynamic construction machines; such as excavators without using any labeled images for the first stage. The whole model structure is divided into three stages: the pre-train stage using the SimCLR contrastive approach, and two fine-tuning stages for the transfer learning and the downstream task. The model can leverage the features and learn from a huge unlabeled dataset called ACID to two small datasets generated from NVIDIA Isaac and MATLAB Simscape simulators as well as transfer the knowledge to a smaller dataset with a ratio of 3.5% from the original ACID dataset. The results show that the proposed approach can improve the accuracy of pose estimation for heavy construction machines in real images by 11% and 13% in comparison to the normal self-supervised approach with two backbones ResNet-50 and HRNet-W32, respectively.

</details>


### [53] [OT-CAL: Optimal-Transport-Guided Adaptive Sample Selection for Efficient Visual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41677)
*Suryansh Talukdar,Jin Soung Yoo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OT-CAL：用五个信息量特征（难度、可学习性、不确定性等）表示样本，以课程向量描述当前偏好，用最优传输匹配分配选择权重，并据此更新课程向量形成自适应反馈回路，在每批选50%样本时比AdaSelection高1.3–2.1点，训练提速1.18–1.24倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练中并非所有样本贡献相同，仅对信息量高的样本做反向传播可节省算力；但样本价值随训练阶段变化（早期难样本有用、中期"变好"的样本重要、后期类别多样性重要），现有方法依赖固定选择规则或昂贵的逐样本梯度计算。

Method: 将每个样本表征为五个信息量特征（含难度、可学习性、不确定性）；用课程向量刻画当前偏好的样本特性；通过最优传输（OT）匹配按样本与该目标的接近程度赋予选择权重；再用被选样本反向更新课程向量，形成无需手工调度(manual schedule)的自适应闭环。

Result: 在三个标准视觉学习基准上，每mini-batch选择50%样本时，性能较AdaSelection提升1.3–2.1个点，训练速度达到全数据训练的1.18–1.24倍。

Conclusion: 基于特征表示+最优传输的课程式数据选择可自适应地替代固定规则与昂贵的梯度计算，在显著降低反向计算量的同时优于强基线，具备实用的加速与精度双重收益。

Abstract: Deep neural networks for image recognition are typically trained using mini-batches, with a forward pass to compute predictions and losses followed by a more computationally expensive backward pass to update model parameters. However, not all training samples contribute equally to learning, suggesting that computation can be reduced by performing backward passes only on informative samples. The challenge is that sample informativeness changes throughout training: difficult samples may be valuable early on, improving samples become important later, and class diversity becomes increasingly relevant toward the end. Existing methods often rely on fixed selection rules or computationally expensive per-sample gradient calculations. We propose OT-CAL, an adaptive data selection method that automatically adjusts its sampling preferences during training. OT-CAL represents each sample using five informativeness features, including difficulty, learnability, and uncertainty. A curriculum vector describes the currently preferred sample characteristics, and optimal-transport-based matching assigns selection weights according to how closely samples match this target. The curriculum vector is then updated using the selected samples, forming a feedback loop that adapts without a manually defined schedule. Experiments on three standard visual learning benchmarks show that, when selecting 50% of each mini-batch, OT-CAL improves performance by 1.3--2.1 points over AdaSelection while achieving 1.18--1.24 times faster training than full-data training.

</details>


### [54] [Retrieval-Augmented Initialization with Dual-Guided Inversion for Flow-Based Inverse Problem Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41695)
*Hansam Cho,Seoung Bum Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 RA-DGI：用检索到的代理干净图像做双引导反演来构造更好的初始噪声，在固定下游 flow-based 逆问题求解器的前提下系统评估初始化策略，显著提升重建保真度与感知质量，且比推理期扩展开销更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 flow-based 逆问题求解器几乎都用随机高斯噪声初始化，而图像生成领域已表明初始噪声显著影响生成结果；但“初始化”这一设计维度在逆问题求解中仍未被系统研究。

Method: 控制变量实验框架：固定下游 flow 基求解器，仅替换噪声初始化策略。提出 Retrieval-Augmented Initialization with Dual-Guided Inversion（RA-DGI）：从检索库取代理干净图像，经双引导反演得到更优初始噪声。

Result: 在多个逆问题上的实验显示初始噪声质量大幅影响重建保真度与感知指标；RA-DGI 优于随机初始化，并优于推理期扩展（inference-time scaling）策略，同时计算开销更低。

Conclusion: 初始化是 flow-based 逆问题求解中被长期忽视却关键的因素，检索增强 + 双引导反演是一种高效可行的初始化替代方案。

Abstract: Flow models have recently emerged as powerful generative priors for solving inverse problems. Despite their success, most existing flow-based inverse problem solvers initialize the reconstruction process with random Gaussian noise. However, recent studies in image generation have shown that the choice of initial noise can significantly influence generation outcomes. The role of initialization as a distinct design choice in flow-based inverse problem solving remains comparatively underexplored. In this work, we systematically evaluate noise initialization strategies while holding the downstream flow-based inverse solver fixed. We propose Retrieval-Augmented Initialization with Dual-Guided Inversion (RA-DGI), a method that constructs improved initial noise by leveraging retrieved proxy clean images and dual-guided inversion. Extensive experiments across multiple inverse problems demonstrate that the quality of the initial noise substantially affects reconstruction fidelity and perceptual performance. RA-DGI outperforms standard random initialization and achieves superior results compared to inference-time scaling strategies, while requiring less computational overhead. These findings highlight initialization as a critical yet previously overlooked factor in flow-based inverse problem solvers.

</details>


### [55] [Nereus: A Large-Scale Underwater Dataset for Fine-Grained Attribute Understanding and Grounded Counting Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41705)
*Jingtao Deng,Zihao Jin,Yuehe Chen,Feixiang He,Shuiwang Li,Dan Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Nereus：面向水下细粒度感知的大规模数据集，含88K图像、2.68M问答对，覆盖2,690物种/1,515属的136K生物个体标注、65K点标注与67K grounded counting QA，联合支持细粒度属性理解与基于定位的计数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水下数据集仅提供粗类别标签、泛化描述或总量计数监督，无法支撑个体级细粒度性状识别与基于空间证据的生态推理。

Method: 构建多任务标注体系：物种/属级细粒度分类、个体属性问答、点标注定位计数及grounded counting QA；并用代表性多模态大模型进行微调与评测对比。

Result: 在代表性多模态模型上，Nereus相对现有水下域数据的下游适配效果更优，验证了细粒度个体级标注对模型能力提升的作用。

Conclusion: Nereus为细粒度海洋生物感知与生态推理提供数据基础，但属于数据集/基准贡献，未提出新方法或具身/3D相关技术洞见。

Abstract: Underwater imagery records rich ecological information and supports applications such as science communication, fishery monitoring, ecological conservation, and oceanographic research. Beyond general scene understanding, these applications require models to recognize fine-grained biological traits and reason over spatially grounded evidence. However, existing underwater datasets often provide coarse category labels, generic captions, or total-count supervision, which limits their use for organism-level analysis and ecological reasoning. We introduce Nereus, a large-scale dataset for Underwater Fine-grained Perception that jointly supports Fine-Grained Object Attribute Understanding and Grounded Counting Perception. Nereus contains 88K images and 2.68M question-answer pairs, including annotations for 136K marine organism objects across 2,690 species and 1,515 genera, as well as 65K point annotations and 67K grounded counting QA pairs. Experiments with representative multimodal models show that Nereus improves downstream adaptation over existing underwater-domain data and provides a foundation for future research on fine-grained marine perception.

</details>


### [56] [SAR-TS-BERT: Physics-Guided Self-Supervised Pre-Training With Polarimetric Features for SAR Time-Series Crop Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41728)
*Jieqiu Wang,Yuhan Zhang,Bo Zheng,Shaoming Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAR-TS-BERT：面向多时相SAR作物分类的物理引导BERT式自监督预训练框架，将VV/VH/VV-VH后向散射与双极化Cloude-Pottier分解(H/α/A)组成六波段输入，采用基于时序后向散射变化率的SpanPhysics跨度掩码与双分支跨模态交叉注意力编码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多时相SAR可提供连续无云观测，但高精度分类依赖大量标注；现有遥感自监督预训练多面向光学影像或通用SAR空间场景，未充分利用时序后向散射动态与极化散射机理。

Method: 双核心设计：1) SpanPhysics物理引导跨度掩码，依据时序后向散射变化率自适应选择连续掩码片段以聚焦关键物候转折；2) 双分支跨模态融合编码器，分别用Transformer建模后向散射与极化分解特征，并经双向交叉注意力融合。六波段输入融合强度与极化比。

Result: 爱沙尼亚EuroCropsML总体精度96.78%，美国加州CDL 87.29%，崇明岛月均OA达99.37%；爱沙尼亚基准上全监督下较从零训练OA提升1.17、Macro F1提升1.59个百分点，仅用1%标注时提升2.58与5.19个百分点。

Conclusion: 将SAR散射物理引入时序自监督预训练，可有效提升作物分类精度、地块级制图一致性与少样本迁移能力。

Abstract: Multitemporal synthetic aperture radar (SAR) imagery provides continuous, cloud-free observations of crop phenology for agricultural monitoring in cloudy and rainy regions, yet high-accuracy classification typically requires extensive labeled samples. Although self-supervised pre-training mitigates label dependency in remote sensing, existing frameworks primarily target optical imagery or generic spatial SAR scenes, underutilizing temporal backscatter dynamics and polarimetric scattering mechanisms. We propose SAR-TS-BERT, a physics-guided BERT-style self-supervised pre-training framework for SAR time-series crop classification. SAR-TS-BERT integrates backscatter intensities and polarimetric ratio features (VV, VH, and VV/VH) with dual-polarimetric Cloude-Pottier decomposition parameters ($H/\alpha/A$) into a six-band input representation through two core designs: 1) a physics-guided span masking strategy (SpanPhysics) that adaptively selects contiguous masked spans based on temporal backscatter change rates to prioritize key phenological transitions; and 2) a dual-branch cross-modal fusion encoder that models backscatter and polarimetric decomposition features through separate Transformer branches followed by bidirectional cross-attention fusion. On independent test sets in Estonia (EuroCropsML) and the USA (California CDL), SAR-TS-BERT achieves overall accuracies (OAs) of 96.78% and 87.29%, respectively, and attains a mean monthly OA of 99.37% on Chongming Island, Shanghai. On the Estonia benchmark, pre-training improves OA and Macro F1 by 1.17 and 1.59 percentage points over training from scratch under full supervision, and by 2.58 and 5.19 percentage points using only 1% labeled data. Incorporating SAR scattering physics into temporal self-supervised pre-training effectively enhances crop classification accuracy, parcel-level mapping consistency, and few-shot transferability.

</details>


### [57] [Measuring What Matters: A Causal Reliance Score for Histopathology Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41763)
*Samuel Thomas,Daniel Tweneboah Anyimadu,Ahmed Karam Eldaly,Somaia Elsheikh,Mohammed M. Abdelsamea*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于因果启发的干预式评估框架与 Causal Reliance Score（CRS），量化分割模型对前景组织扰动与背景/域变化的相对敏感性，并配套反事实式数据增强训练策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学病理分割模型易依赖虚假的背景相关性而非病理相关结构，仅靠改进架构不足以保证可靠性；现有 Dice/IoU 等指标无法揭示这种依赖差异。

Method: 构建受控干预评估框架：分别对前景组织与背景/域因素施加扰动，提出 CRS 指标衡量两者相对敏感性；训练阶段设计增强策略充当隐式反事实干预，抑制冗余因子、保持形态不变；实例化于自监督 Transformer（CAUSE-ViT）。

Result: 干预揭示了 Dice/IoU 无法捕捉的模型行为差异，表明不同模型对前景与背景的依赖程度存在显著区别。

Conclusion: 该工作主要贡献是原理化的干预式方法论与审计指标而非新架构；基于反事实干预的行为评估对可靠、临床鲁棒的医学分割系统是必要的。

Abstract: Reliable histopathology segmentation requires models that rely on pathology-relevant structures rather than spurious background correlations. In this work, we argue that improving architectures alone is insufficient if models continue to rely on spurious correlations rather than pathology-relevant features. We introduce a causality-inspired evaluation framework based on controlled interventions that explicitly probes model sensitivity to foreground tissue structure and background/domain variation. Building on this, we propose the Causal Reliance Score (CRS), a metric that quantifies the relative sensitivity of model predictions to foreground perturbation versus background/domain variation under controlled interventions. To support this analysis, we design augmentation strategies that act as implicit counterfactual interventions during training, encouraging invariance to nuisance factors while preserving morphology. We demonstrate how these interventions reveal behavioural differences between models that are not captured by standard metrics such as Dice or IoU. While we instantiate our framework using a self-supervised transformer (CAUSE-ViT), the primary contribution of this work is not a new architecture, but a principled intervention-based methodology and evaluation metric for auditing model behaviour in medical image segmentation. Our results suggest that evaluating model behaviour through counterfactual interventions is essential for developing reliable and clinically robust medical image segmentation systems.

</details>


### [58] [WorkIng: A Real-World and Synthetic Highway Work-Zone Benchmark Dataset for Autonomous Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41772)
*Xujun Xie,Dominik Rößle,Adithya Mohan,Daniel Cremers,Torsten Schön*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WorkIng: 面向自动驾驶施工区的双域基准（真实360°多模态 + CARLA程序化监管感知仿真），含12000+帧、227399个3D框与24个场景


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高速公路施工区是AV关键边缘案例：几何特异、法规地域化，现有基准无法覆盖——真实数据集缺乏360°多模态全场景3D标注，单一实地无法涵盖多样布局与法规变体

Method: WorkIng-Highway：128线LiDAR+6路RGB+RTK-GPS，32km、12000+标注帧、227399个跟踪3D框；WorkIng-CARLA：程序化生成24个监管感知场景、6类规范拓扑、6种国际交通标准、100+自建3D资产；在两域上评测代表性3D检测与端到端规划架构

Result: 真实数据仍是性能主要驱动；按场景分层的合成增强在2D识别、3D检测与轨迹规划上均有提升

Conclusion: 提供双域施工区基准与训练资源，验证场景分层合成增强对跨域泛化的价值

Abstract: Highway work zones are critical edge cases for autonomous vehicles (AVs), combining atypical geometries and jurisdiction-specific regulations that existing benchmarks fail to capture: real-world datasets lack 360° multi-modal, full-scene 3D work zone annotation, and no single recorded site can span the layouts and regulatory variants needed for generalizable models. We present WorkIng, a dual-domain benchmark and training resource. WorkIng-Highway provides a 360° multi-modal work zone benchmark with synchronized 128-channel LiDAR, six RGB cameras, and RTK-GPS across 32 km, yielding 12,000+ annotated frames and 227,399 tracked 3D bounding boxes. WorkIng-CARLA complements this with procedural simulation spanning 24 regulation-aware scenarios across six canonical topologies and six international traffic standards, populated by 100+ custom 3D assets. Benchmarking representative 3D detection and end-to-end planning architectures shows that real-world data remains the primary performance driver, and that scenario-stratified synthetic augmentation improves architectures across 2D recognition, 3D detection, and trajectory planning. Resources: this https URL

</details>


### [59] [Hard Sample-Conditioned Decorrelated Attention for Robust Face Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41789)
*Tahar Chettaoui,Guray Ozgur,Eduarda Caldeira,Naser Damer,Fadi Boutros*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ViT-HarDis 提出按样本难度调节注意力头解耦强度的正则化：对与负类中心余弦相似度高的难样本施加更强解耦，并发现只对最后几层 Transformer 施加解耦效果最佳，在不改结构、不增推理开销下提升人脸识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ViT 多头注意力缺乏显式正则时各头易学到冗余模式；现有基于相关性的头部解耦惩罚对所有样本一视同仁，而人脸识别中靠近竞争身份的"难样本"才是优化与泛化的关键。

Method: 设计样本难度条件化的头部解耦损失：以样本嵌入与负类中心的余弦相似度衡量难度，作为权重调制头间注意力去相关的正则强度，使身份歧义最大处强制头部专门化；实验上比较了全网络与仅最后若干 Transformer 层施加解耦的效果。

Result: 多数基准上优于基线与均匀解耦方法；分析显示难样本与竞争类中心相似度下降、头间注意力相似度降低；仅末层解耦优于全网络正则；WebFace4M 训练下在 IJB-B、IJB-C、TinyFace Rank-1 上取得 ViT 类方法 SOTA，MS1MV3 训练下取得最佳 IJB-B 与 TinyFace 结果。

Conclusion: 将正则强度与样本难度耦合是一种低成本、无架构改动的有效注意力头专化手段，且解耦只需施加于浅层之外的末段层即可获益，对人脸识别精度与泛化均有提升。

Abstract: Multi-head attention in Vision Transformers (ViTs) allows independent attention patterns across heads, but without explicit regularization, heads may learn redundant patterns rather than complementary roles. Correlation-based penalties encourage head disentanglement, yet apply uniform regularization across all training samples. In face recognition (FR), hard samples whose embeddings lie close to competing identities are critical for optimization and generalization. Motivated by this, we propose ViT-HarDis, which conditions attention head disentanglement on sample difficulty: samples with high cosine similarity to negative class centers receive stronger head decorrelation, enforcing specialization where identity ambiguity is highest. Experiments show improvements over both the baseline and uniform disentanglement on most benchmarks. Our analysis shows that ViT-HarDis reduces the similarity of hard samples to competing class centers while decreasing inter-head attention similarity, and that applying disentanglement only to the final transformer layers outperforms regularizing the entire network. Trained on WebFace4M, ViT-HarDis achieves SOTA results among ViT-based methods on IJB-B, IJB-C, and TinyFace Rank-1, while achieving the best IJB-B and TinyFace results when trained on MS1MV3, improving FR without architectural modifications or additional inference cost. Code: this https URL .

</details>


### [60] [Different Models, Shared Failures: Auditing Frozen-Encoder Pools for Medical Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41790)
*Eunseob Choi,Kyeonghun Kim,Hyuk-Jae Lee,Nam-Joon Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoFailMed audits correlated failures across 11 (expanded to 17) public pretrained encoders on five 2D medical segmentation tasks using frozen features with 1×1 readouts; shared-checkpoint pairs are most correlated, four-member cross-encoder pools have effective sizes of 1.56–2.92 at a pooled 33rd-percentile Dice threshold, and richer readouts/fine-tuning shrink the between-group correlation gap without reliably raising effective pool size.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Average segmentation accuracy and nominal model counts in ensembles/pools implicitly assume error independence, which is rarely verified; the authors ask whether pretrained medical encoders actually fail on the same cases.

Method: Frozen-feature transfer evaluation of 11 public pretrained encoders across five 2D medical segmentation tasks, each with separately trained 1×1 readouts and four seeds; per-case Dice correlation analysis, expanded 17-encoder comparison across encoder families, and effective-pool-size estimation from failure correlations at a pooled 33rd-percentile Dice threshold; ablations with richer readouts and fine-tuning, with thresholds recomputed per training recipe.

Result: Shared-checkpoint pairs show the highest mean per-case Dice correlation; same-family checkpoints remain more correlated than cross-family pairs; four-member cross-encoder pools yield mean effective sizes of 1.56–2.92; richer readouts and fine-tuning narrow the correlation gap between same-checkpoint/same-release pairs and others, yet inter-encoder dependence can increase and effective pool size does not consistently grow.

Conclusion: Reduced between-group correlation gaps can coexist with higher absolute dependence, so pool evaluation should report absolute dependence and threshold-specific effective size alongside accuracy and nominal pool size; aligned per-case traces, scripts and study code are released for reproduction.

Abstract: Average segmentation accuracy and model count do not reveal whether models fail on the same cases. We present CoFailMed, an audit of correlated failures in pretrained-encoder pools for medical segmentation. Across five 2D tasks, we evaluate 11 public pretrained encoders (a controlled sample, not a census) using frozen features and separately trained $1\times 1$ readouts with four seeds per encoder. Shared-checkpoint pairs have the highest mean per-case Dice correlation. An expanded 17-encoder analysis shows that distinct checkpoints within the same encoder family remain more correlated than cross-family pairs. At the pooled 33rd-percentile Dice threshold, four-member pools drawn from distinct encoders have mean effective sizes of 1.56-2.92, estimated from failure correlations in pools with enough failures and passes per member. In the primary pool, richer readouts and fine-tuning narrow the Dice-correlation gap between pairs sharing a checkpoint (or the DINOv2-B/S release) and all other pairs. Yet dependence between different encoders can increase, and fine-tuning does not consistently increase effective pool size at 33rd-percentile thresholds recomputed for each training recipe. A smaller between-group gap can coexist with greater dependence. Pool evaluation should therefore report absolute dependence and threshold-specific effective size alongside accuracy and nominal pool size. The CoFailMed release provides aligned per-case traces, analysis scripts and study code to reproduce the central audit results.

</details>


### [61] [A Patch is More Than a Token](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41814)
*Zixiong Wang,Haiping Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 PMTT 表示框架，把 ViT 单个 patch token 扩展为“以规范状态为中心的表示状态族”（Patch-Level Local Feature Space），在冻结 DINOv3 上无需神经后训练即可获得更强的 patch 级结构化表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ViT 用一个固定观测条件下产生的规范 token 表示图像 patch，这只是该 patch 众多可能状态中的一种实现，隐含丢失了 patch 在身份保持变化下的大量信息。作者希望验证：预训练视觉骨干内部是否蕴含比单 token 接口更丰富的 patch 信息，以及这些信息能否被显式组织成可用表示。

Method: PMTT 由上到下分四部分：(1) Patch Information Acquisition + Dense Orbital Views，采样同一 patch 在合法变换下的多种状态；(2) Visible-to-Hidden 预测任务，检验可见状态能否揭示未观测状态；(3) 内在几何分析，刻画低维、能量集中、patch 特异且可预测的结构；(4) 表示组织，区分“获取更多信息”与“组织信息”，以解析构造的 Manifold 作为核心结构化表示。全程在冻结的 DINOv3 S/B/L/H+ 上实现，不做表示层面的神经后训练。

Result: 在两个独立的 4096 锚点队列上，Manifold 可解释 70.8–79.6% 的 Hidden 变化，直接 Observed 状态为 55.0–72.3%；各尺度下超过 95% 的锚点上 Manifold 占优。有效秩由约 2.5 降至 2.0，主能量集中度随骨干规模上升。六项视觉能力 × 四尺度共 24 个条件中，Observed 优于 Center 21 次，Manifold 优于 Center 23 次，Manifold 优于 Observed 16 次；最强结果为物理对应任务，MegaDepth 上各尺度均满足 Center < Observed < Manifold。代码与实验资产已开源。

Conclusion: 预训练视觉骨干包含远超标准单 token 接口所直接暴露的 patch 级信息；把这些 lawful 状态组织成显式结构表示（而非单纯增加采样）才带来增益。该工作为 patch 级表示提供了新的对象定义与分析范式，但评估以冻结特征分析和代理任务为主，缺少下游空间智能/操作任务的端到端验证。

Abstract: Vision Transformers represent each image patch through a canonical token produced under one observation condition. That token is a powerful interface, but it is still one realized state of the patch. We introduce PMTT, which establishes two foundations: a patch representation object can extend from one canonical state to a canonical-centered Representation State Family, and identity-preserving variation opens Patch-Level Local Feature Space. We develop four parts of this representation program. Patch Information Acquisition and Dense Orbital Views expose lawful same-patch states. Visible-to-Hidden prediction tests whether observed states reveal structure beyond themselves. Intrinsic analysis identifies low-dimensional, energy-concentrated, patch-specific, predictive geometry. Representation Organization separates acquiring more information from organizing that information, with an analytic Manifold as the central structured representation. We realize PMTT on frozen DINOv3 S/B/L/H+ backbones without neural post-training of the representation. Across two independent 4,096-anchor cohorts, Manifold explains 70.8-79.6% of Hidden variation, compared with 55.0-72.3% for directly Observed states, and wins on more than 95% of anchors at every scale. Effective rank falls from about 2.5 to 2.0 while dominant energy concentration rises with backbone scale. Across six visual capabilities and four scales, Observed exceeds the canonical Center in 21 of 24 conditions, Manifold exceeds Center in 23, and Manifold exceeds Observed in 16. The strongest current consequence appears in physical correspondence, where MegaDepth satisfies Center < Observed < Manifold at every scale. These results show that pretrained visual backbones contain richer patch information than the standard single-token interface directly exposes. Code and experimental assets are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [62] [PGGT-Loc: Primitive-Grounded Geometry Transformer for Feed-Forward Camera Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41833)
*Hanqiao Ye,Yuzhou Liu,Mengqi Rong,Jian Liu,Yangdong Liu,Shuhan Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PGGT（Primitive-Grounded Geometry Transformer），将单张查询图像与一组平面地图基元（平面图元）直接对齐，前馈回归6-DoF相机位姿，在稠密表面地图与极简布局模型上都刷新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有相机定位多为多阶段流水线，依赖构建、存储与长期维护成本高昂的专用复杂地图；而结构化、以紧凑且富有表现力的基元组织的场景地图（含丰富几何与语义线索、人类可解读）往往现成可得。但因地图基元与视觉观测间存在显著模态差异，其用于直接定位的潜力几乎未被挖掘。

Method: 以“观测—基元”grounding为定位新范式，并在平面基元场景下实例化：输入单张查询图像与一组平面地图基元，用Transformer网络预测多个定位目标，前馈式输出6-DoF位姿；架构简洁可扩展，训练后可直接泛化到未见环境（无需逐场景重训）。

Result: 在稠密表面地图与极简layout模型两类地图上均达到优于现有方法的定位性能，且可泛化至未见场景。

Conclusion: 将结构化地图基元作为定位的“一等公民”，用端到端前馈回归跨越模态鸿沟，可绕开昂贵专用地图流水线，为轻量、可维护的相机定位提供新路线。

Abstract: Camera localization methods are mostly multi-stage pipelines relying on dedicated and complex maps that are costly to construct, store, and maintain over time. Alternatively, well-structured maps that organize scenes with compact yet expressive primitives are often readily available, providing rich structural and semantic cues that humans can interpret. However, their potential for direct camera localization remains largely unexplored due to the significant modality gap between map primitives and visual observations. This motivates a new concept for localization, which estimates camera poses by grounding query observations to map primitives. In this paper, we instantiate the concept in the context of planar primitives with PGGT, the Primitive-Grounded Geometry Transformer network. Given a single query image and a set of planar map primitives, the network is trained to predict multiple localization targets for feed-forward 6-DoF camera localization that generalizes to unseen environments. With this straightforward and scalable architecture, our method pushes the state-of-the-art localization performance on both dense surface maps and minimal layout models. The code and models are available at this https URL

</details>


### [63] [ObjectGuidedMap: An Object-Aware Model for Robust Bird's Eye View Map Prediction in High-Occlusion Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41834)
*Christian Bohn,Harsh Yadav,Klaus Friedrichs,Hasan Tercan,Tobias Meisen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多任务BEV地图预测架构,在Mask2Map基础上加入3D目标检测分支,通过可变形注意力与交叉注意力在BEV特征层和实例查询层实现跨任务特征融合,显著提升遮挡场景下的地图预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有BEV地图预测模型基本独立于目标级场景理解,而交通参与者常遮挡道路且隐含几何信息,因此希望显式建模地图与3D目标之间的信息交互。

Method: 基于Mask2Map扩展多任务架构:新增对象检测分支;在BEV特征层用可变形注意力(可采样被遮挡区域上下文)、在实例查询层用交叉注意力(地图实例查询与目标查询交互)进行跨任务特征融合。

Result: 在nuScenes与Argoverse 2上一致提升BEV地图预测精度,在重度目标遮挡场景下mAP提升达21%。

Conclusion: 联合BEV地图与3D目标预测的双任务交互能有效缓解遮挡问题并带来稳定精度增益,说明跨任务特征融合是多任务BEV感知的有效设计。

Abstract: Accurate BEV map prediction is a critical component of autonomous driving systems, enabling downstream planning and control. Recent approaches such as Mask2Map achieve strong performance by predicting vectorized, instance-level map elements directly from multi-view images. However, existing BEV map prediction models operate largely independently of object-level scene understanding, despite the fact that traffic participants frequently occlude or implicitly encode information about road geometry. In this work, we propose a multitask architecture that jointly predicts BEV maps and 3D objects, explicitly enabling information exchange between the two tasks. Building upon Mask2Map, we introduce an object detection branch and incorporate cross-task feature fusion at both the BEV feature level and the instance-query level via deformable attention and cross-attention mechanisms. This design allows map instances to leverage object context while remaining robust to occlusions caused by traffic participants. Experimental results on the nuScenes and Argoverse 2 datasets demonstrate that our approach consistently improves BEV map prediction accuracy, with particularly significant gains of 21% mAP in scenes containing heavy object occlusion.

</details>


### [64] [Open-Weight MLLMs Cannot Compare What They Cannot Name](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41841)
*Wenxin Su,Anna Kreshuk*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究MLLM在蚂蚁合成图像身体部位尺寸比较任务上的基线表现，发现部分部位比较失败，归因于"识别驱动的grounding失败"（recognition-driven grounding failure）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显微镜数据集规模已超出人工视觉分析能力，希望用MLLM自动化生物图像分析，尤其是复杂表型的视觉比较。因此先建立极简基线考察MLLM是否具备最基础的视觉比较能力。

Method: 在合成蚂蚁图像上做身体部位尺寸比较的简单基线评测；测试多种in-context learning策略；分析与相关的物体识别任务的表现；并进一步分析视觉编码器的表征，检验细粒度视觉结构是否在缺乏语义支持时难以保留。

Result: SOTA开源权重MLLM对某些身体部位比较失败、对另一些成功，尽管该比较对模型而言视觉上是平凡的；识别失败部位会被归因到可识别的语义概念，从而同时损害grounding与比较；视觉编码器分析支持"无语义支持时细粒度结构保持不佳"的假设。

Conclusion: 当前MLLM存在一个反直觉的局限：语义识别会门控本不需要语义理解的简单视觉比较；即性能瓶颈在语义识别与grounding，而非底层视觉感知，这对自动化生物图像表型分析提出了重要警示。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are increasingly capable of understanding images and performing complex visual reasoning, suggesting the possibility of using them to automate biological image analysis. This need is becoming increasingly urgent as advances in technology continue to generate ever larger and more diverse microscopy datasets, already exceeding the scale at which manual visual analysis is feasible. With the final aim to use MLLMs for visual comparison of complex phenotypes, we investigate a very simple baseline: comparison of the size of body parts in synthetic images of ants. We find that state-of-the-art open-weight MLLMs fail for some body parts, but not for others, even though the comparison itself is visually trivial for all of them. We evaluate different in-context learning strategies and analyze performance on the related object recognition task. Our results suggest a recognition-driven grounding failure: structures that the models cannot recognize are instead associated with a recognizable semantic concept, impairing the grounding and comparison of both. Further analysis of the vision encoder is consistent with the hypothesis that fine-grained visual structure may be less well preserved without semantic support. Together, our results reveal a surprising limitation of current MLLMs: semantic recognition can gate simple visual comparisons that do not intrinsically require semantic understanding. Our code and data are available at this https URL

</details>


### [65] [TwinPast: Benchmarking Historical Evidence Use in Streaming Video Understanding with Evidence Contrastive Video Pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41983)
*Haotian Yang,Yong Chen,Cheng Peng,Haobo Wang,Zhongle Xie,Xuanru Li,Gang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TwinPast 是一个配对视频基准：固定近期上下文、问题与选项顺序，仅改变关键历史证据及答案，用 350 对人类审核视频对、5 类历史任务检验模型答案是否真正随历史变化（PairAcc）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式视频基准（OVO-Bench、StreamingBench）的高分不可靠：32% 抽样历史问题仅靠近期帧即可作答，且模型仅凭问题+选项也能答对不少题，说明模型未必使用视觉历史。

Method: 构建 TwinPast 配对视频对：每对中近期视频上下文、问题、选项顺序均保持不变，只替换关键历史证据从而改变正确答案；两段视频独立评估，PairAcc 要求两者均答对。评测 34 个模型/输入配置，覆盖 5 类历史任务并辅以案例分析。

Result: 最强流式系统仅达 49.14% PairAcc，而评测的通用模型为 66.57–91.43%，差距在全部五类任务上持续存在；对 Qwen3-VL-8B 与 DeepSeek-Flash，加入历史输入反而提升表现，逆转了现有基准上的"近期帧优势"。案例显示模型虽保留证据，却选错时间状态或违背事件顺序。

Conclusion: 现有流式视频评测夸大了模型的视觉历史利用能力；需将历史记忆/检索与依据问题时间指代和事件关系使用证据的推理相结合。

Abstract: High accuracy on streaming video benchmarks does not establish whether a model uses visual history. Our review of OVO-Bench and StreamingBench finds that recent frames suffice for 32% of sampled historical questions, while models also answer many questions correctly from the question and options alone. We introduce TwinPast to test whether answers correctly follow changes in history, using 350 human-reviewed video pairs spanning five historical tasks. Each pair holds the recent video context, question, and ordered options fixed while changing critical historical evidence and the correct answer. Each video is evaluated independently, and PairAcc requires correct answers for both histories. We evaluate 34 model and input configurations. For Qwen3-VL-8B and DeepSeek-Flash, historical inputs improve performance on TwinPast, reversing the recent-frame advantage on existing benchmarks. The strongest evaluated streaming system reaches 49.14% PairAcc, compared with 66.57-91.43% for the evaluated general-purpose models. This gap persists across all five tasks. Case studies further show that models select states from the wrong time or contradict recorded event order despite retaining the relevant evidence. These findings highlight the need to connect historical memory and retrieval with reasoning that uses evidence according to the question's temporal references and event relations.

</details>


### [66] [FoAViS: Analyzing Learned Fixation Strategies in a Foveated Active Vision System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41987)
*Valentin Hassler,Jonathan Henrich,Yohaï-Eliel Berreby,Haoze Zhu,Alexander Ecker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 FoAViS 系统：用对数极坐标（log-polar）中央凹采样，将视觉信息累积到 2D 空间记忆，并由与任务联合训练的注视策略在 2D 图上选取注视点；同一 2D 记忆可直接全局读出做分类或稠密读出做语义分割，无需改架构。学到的注视策略选择更具信息量、任务相关的位置，并带来更优任务表征与更接近人类扫视路径的特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视觉是中央凹化且主动的（分辨率不均、注视点主动导向）。构建同样处理的机器系统可在组件与目标已知的受控环境中系统研究注视行为，进而反哺对生物视觉的理解。难点在于需要同时具备：类中央凹采样、跨任务（分类到稠密预测）通用、且能与注视策略联合训练的单一模型。

Method: 采用既有的 log-polar 中央凹采样获取视觉输入；信息累积到 2D 空间记忆，因其二维结构，可全局读取做分类、稠密读取做语义分割；注视点由一个 2D 地图上的策略选择，该策略与任务并行端到端训练。实验对比非学习启发式、随机位置、以及"随机位置但给定学习扫视路径"等基线。

Result: 学习到的注视策略比非学习启发式选中更富信息、更任务相关的位置；分类与分割任务中，与策略联合训练的模型优于随机位置训练模型，即使后者获得学习到的扫视路径；学习到的扫视路径在若干经验扫视路径统计特性上与人眼注视行为相似。

Conclusion: 工作是从标准被动、单遍架构迈向具生物依据视觉模型的一步，为研究目标导向注视行为与视觉系统中的信息表征提供了路径。属计算神经科学/主动视觉方法论贡献，非机器人或 3D 重建核心方向。

Abstract: Human vision is foveated and active: resolution is highest at the fovea and falls off towards the periphery, and fixations are actively directed to locations of interest. Building a machine system that processes information in the same way lets us study gaze behavior in a controlled setting where the system's components and objectives are known. The emergence of gaze characteristics can therefore be examined systematically, which may in turn inform our understanding of biological vision. This requires a model that can sample like the fovea, handle various tasks from classification to dense prediction, and be trained concurrently with a fixation policy. In our study, we present such a system and study its capabilities and the fixation policies that emerge from it. Our system, FoAViS, samples visual input by adopting the established log-polar foveation and accumulates information in a 2D spatial memory. Due to its 2D structure, the same memory can be read out globally for classification or densely for semantic segmentation, without any architectural change. Fixations are drawn from a 2D map by a policy trained concurrently with the task. We find that learned fixation policies select more informative, task-specific locations than non-learned heuristics. These policies also shape the task representations in a beneficial way: for both classification and segmentation, models trained concurrently with a policy outperform models trained with random locations, even when the latter are provided with learned scanpaths. Moreover, these scanpaths exhibit similarities to human gaze behavior by capturing aspects of empirical scanpath characteristics. Our work marks an important step away from standard passive, single-pass architectures, towards biologically grounded models of vision. It opens a path for studying goal-directed gaze behavior and information representation in the visual system.

</details>


### [67] [MoETok: Sparse Expert Routing for 1D Visual Tokenizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41992)
*Jiahao Guo,Bao Tang,Haoxiang Cao,Jingfeng Yao,Bo Li,Wenyu Liu,Kun Gai,Changqian Yu,Xinggang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MoETok：发现查询式1D视觉分词器中query自发分化为少数语义Content query与多数细节Detail query，据此用稀疏专家路由（MoE）为不同角色token分配专门处理路径，在匹配监督与激活算力下同时提升重建、语义表征与自回归生成，并在统一AR理解-生成模型中取得离散模型SOTA生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 查询式1D视觉分词器可编码灵活隐序列，但各可学习query如何采集与编码信息缺乏理解；同时token角色异质（语义vs细节）却用同一稠密网络处理，存在容量分配与优化冲突。

Method: 对查询式1D分词器的query进行角色分析，揭示语义监督下Content query（少）捕获高层语义、Detail query（多）保留细粒度细节；提出MoETok，以token级稀疏激活专家+路由器实现角色专业化路径，并与重建损失和语义目标联合优化；在不同模型规模与token预算下对比稠密分词器；进一步扩展到统一自回归理解-生成模型（next-token交叉熵）。

Result: 在ImageNet上于1D分词器族内同时取得强重建、强语义表征与SOTA类条件生成；统一AR模型在DPG-Bench 87.61、GenEval2 40.04 GM、WISE 0.49上匹配或超越BAGEL、Qwen-Image等扩散模型，同时保持有竞争力的视觉理解。

Conclusion: token级角色分化是查询式视觉分词器的内在特性，采用MoE式角色专业化路由可在同等监督与激活算力下系统性优于稠密基线；该思路可扩展至统一离散自回归理解-生成框架并达到SOTA生成质量。分析结论偏生成/表征侧，未涉及机器人或3D/物理场景验证。

Abstract: Modern visual tokenizers aim to preserve fine-grained visual details while learning high-level semantic representations for downstream generation and understanding. Query-based 1D visual tokenizers encode images into flexible latent sequences, yet how individual learnable queries gather and encode image information remains poorly understood. We reveal that these queries spontaneously develop complementary roles under semantic supervision: a small set of Content queries captures high-level semantic information, while a larger set of Detail queries preserves fine-grained visual details for reconstruction. To adapt token processing to these heterogeneous roles, we introduce MoETok, a 1D visual tokenizer that routes tokens through sparsely activated experts. Jointly optimized with reconstruction and semantic objectives, the router learns role-specialized processing paths adapted to each token's representation. Across model scales and token budgets, MoETok consistently improves reconstruction, semantic representation, and autoregressive generation over dense tokenizers under matched supervision and active computation. On ImageNet, it combines strong reconstruction and semantic representation with state-of-the-art class-conditional generation within the 1D tokenizer family. We further validate MoETok at scale in a unified autoregressive model jointly trained for understanding and generation with a next-token cross-entropy objective. The resulting model combines competitive visual understanding with state-of-the-art generation among unified discrete models, matching or surpassing diffusion-based models such as BAGEL and Qwen-Image on benchmarks including DPG-Bench (87.61), GenEval2 (40.04 GM), and WISE (0.49).

</details>


### [68] [GeoFlip: Benchmarking Geometry-Sensitive Physics in Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42002)
*Yuqi Li,Junming Liu,Bowen Hu,Ruize Niu,Huiran Duan,Yingli Tian,Hao Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoFlip：针对"初始几何变化→物理结果翻转"的视频生成基准，含482对配对场景、964段参考视频，评测16个I2V模型的物理一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型能合成逼真运动，但能否正确响应首帧几何条件的改变（如支撑范围、摆放位置、机构配置）尚不清楚；需要可控、可验证的配对式评测来分离"视觉逼真"与"物理正确"。

Method: 固定物理参数与文本提示，对支撑范围、物体摆放、机构构型做受控干预，构造"稳定 vs 倾倒/坠落"等结果相反的成对场景；参考视频由程序化场景构建+物理仿真生成，并经数值验证确认物理事件；从事件正确性、结构完整性、与参考相似度三维度评测16个I2V模型，并用独立VLM评审检验一致性。

Result: 全覆盖的442个条件下，事件正确率均值最高仅27.98%；不同模型在不同物理机制上强弱分化，三个评测维度捕获不同能力；典型失败为忽略几何变化、运动错误、刚性物体弯曲、凭空新增部件；评审间秩相关在事件正确性上达0.906、结构完整性上达0.929。

Conclusion: 配对式几何干预+可验证物理参考+多维评测，可作为从视觉真实迈向"几何条件化物理一致性"的测评框架；当前视频生成模型在物理推理上仍严重不足。

Abstract: Video generation models synthesize realistic motion, but can they respond correctly to changes in initial geometry? We introduce GeoFlip, a video generation benchmark centered on physical outcome flips induced by geometry. With shared physical settings and textual prompts held fixed, GeoFlip applies controlled interventions to support extent, object placement, and mechanism configuration, constructing paired scenes with contrasting reference events, such as remaining stable versus falling or tipping. These pairs test whether models translate geometric differences in the first frame into correct physical evolution. The benchmark comprises 482 scene pairs and 964 reference videos across six physical categories, with reference outcomes established through procedural scene construction, physics simulation, and numerical validation and verification of physical events. We evaluate 16 models for image to video generation along three dimensions: event correctness, structural integrity, and similarity to reference videos. We examine evaluation consistency with independent vision language judges. On the 442 conditions shared by all models, the highest event correctness averaged across tasks is 27.98%, revealing substantial limitations in physical generation conditioned on geometry. Model strengths vary across physical mechanisms, and the three evaluation dimensions capture distinct capabilities. Typical failures include ignoring geometric changes, generating incorrect object motion, bending rigid objects, and introducing additional components. Rank correlations between judges reach 0.906 for event correctness and 0.929 for structural integrity. By combining controlled geometric interventions, verifiable physical references, and multidimensional evaluation, GeoFlip assesses progress from visual realism toward physical consistency conditioned on geometry.

</details>


### [69] [Any Forcing: Any-Step Generality and One-Step Stability for Autoregressive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42031)
*Jiahua Xu,Yijie Fang,Nannan Wang,Xunhao Lin,Li Cao,Lei Hao,Mingrui Zhu,Fei Gao,Xinbo Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Any Forcing: 统一对抗判别器(真实分布对齐+步间一致性+首帧质量增强)实现任意步采样与一步稳定生成,并配合免训练自适应长期记忆选择机制,实现稳定的一步自回归长视频生成(H100单卡短/长视频 20.9/24.7 FPS)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归视频生成受限于固定采样步数,难以灵活权衡质量与效率;而一步生成视觉质量下降且长序列误差累积导致内容漂移与生成崩溃。

Method: 提出 Any Forcing 框架:(1) 统一对抗判别器同时承担真实分布对齐、步间一致性对齐与首帧质量增强,支持灵活采样同时保持一步生成质量;(2) 免训练的自适应长期记忆选择机制,基于全局语义相关性与局部价值贡献挑选历史状态,并对其时间位置重映射以保留记忆缓存内时序关系。

Result: 实现高质量且稳定的一步自回归视频生成,单张 H100 上短视频 20.9 FPS、长视频 24.7 FPS;长序列内容漂移与崩溃得到缓解。

Conclusion: 通过判别器层面的多目标统一对齐与记忆缓存的高效筛选重映射,可在保证一步生成稳定性的同时获得任意步采样的灵活性,面向实时长视频自回归生成。

Abstract: Autoregressive video generation enables efficient inference through streaming prediction. However, existing methods are typically restricted to fixed sampling steps, limiting the flexibility to balance generation quality and efficiency, while one-step generation suffers from degraded visual quality and error accumulation over long sequences, leading to content drift and generation collapse. To address these issues, we propose Any Forcing, a unified framework for achieving Any-Step Generality and One-Step Stability. Specifically, we introduce a unified adversarial discriminator that jointly performs real-distribution alignment, inter-step consistency alignment, and first-frame quality enhancement, enabling flexible sampling while preserving one-step generation quality. Furthermore, we propose a training-free adaptive long-term memory selection mechanism that selects historical states based on global semantic relevance and local value contribution, while remapping their temporal positions to preserve temporal relationships within the memory cache, thereby enabling stable one-step long-video generation. Experiments demonstrate that Any Forcing achieves high-quality and stable one-step autoregressive video generation, achieving 20.9 FPS and 24.7 FPS for short and long video generation on a single H100 GPU, respectively.

</details>


### [70] [Learning Clean Generative Processes from Noisy Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42036)
*Fabian Kruse,Lorenzo Baldassari,Ivan Dokmanić*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 仅用独立噪声观测学习干净图像生成模型：Noise2Noise回归可学到不低于训练噪声水平的后验预测；通过"一致性"约束（预测等于继续采样后的期望）将预测延拓到更低噪声乃至干净分布，并在Poisson、二项、Gamma、负二项噪声下给出唯一性条件，于CIFAR-10、光子计数显微与SAR上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型及其非高斯噪声推广在每一步噪声水平都需要后验预测（去噪器），但实际数据往往只有噪声观测、缺乏干净图像配对，尤其超出高斯情形（如光子计数、雷达）时如何训练成为瓶颈。

Method: 1) 用Noise2Noise式回归从独立噪声视图学习各噪声水平的后验预测，分析其仅在该噪声水平及以上有效；2) 引入一致性关系：预测应等于继续采样过程后的期望，据此从数据噪声水平向下延拓；3) 针对Poisson、binomial、Gamma、负二项测量推导该关系能唯一定义延拓至干净数据分布的条件。

Result: CIFAR-10、光子计数显微成像与合成孔径雷达实验表明，所需后验预测可仅由噪声视图学到；一致性关系修复了数据噪声水平之下的采样延拓，而无该约束时延拓显著退化。

Conclusion: 一致性约束为"仅噪声数据条件下的生成建模"提供了理论可行性与实用方法，将Noise2Noise的可学习范围推广到干净图像生成所需的全部噪声水平，适用于多种非高斯噪声模型。

Abstract: We study how to learn a generative model of clean images using only noisy observations. Diffusion processes and their generalizations to non-Gaussian noise require posterior predictions at every noise level. We first show that it is possible to learn these predictions from independent noisy views using Noise2Noise regression, but only at or above the noise level of the training data. To learn the predictions below that level, needed to generate clean images, we impose consistency: a prediction should equal its expectation after continuing the sampling process. We derive conditions under which consistency uniquely determines the continuation to the clean image distribution and verify them for Poisson, binomial, Gamma, and negative-binomial measurements.
