Experiments on CIFAR-10, photon-counting microscopy, and synthetic aperture radar show that the required posterior predictions can be learned from noisy views alone and that the consistency relation repairs the continuation beyond the noise level of the data, which severely deteriorates without consistency.

</details>


### [71] [Think in 360°: Benchmarking and Bootstrapping Panoramic Video Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42082)
*Lidong Lu,Lidong Zou,Xinyi Mao,Jiaxin Lv,Zhenquan Li,Liangrui Dong,Tong Lu,Guo Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PanoVideoBench（首个全景视频理解人工标注基准，1000题/5类/15题型）与PanoVideoCoT（13465条球-时接地的QA-CoT），并以Panoramic CoT SFT + Evidence-Privileged On-Policy Self-Distillation训练PanoVideoReasoner，在基准上达55.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全景视频覆盖相机全方向，关键证据分散在不同方向与时间，要求模型跨视球与跨时间关联观测；现有基准与训练数据缺乏对"跨视角跟踪"和"球-时证据整合"能力的诊断与监督。

Method: 1) 构建PanoVideoBench：1000题、5类、15题型，含目标搜索与全景能力的诊断性标注；2) 通过sphere-time-grounded synthesis，将经核验的全景观测与成功的agentic exploration转换为13465条QA-CoT对，构成PanoVideoCoT；3) 以通用视频MLLM为起点，先做Panoramic CoT SFT，再做Evidence-Privileged On-Policy Self-Distillation（Vision-OPD OPSD的全景实例，教师用经核验的透视视角引导学生在轨生成轨迹）。

Result: 在11个模型（闭源、开源、全景专用）上评测显示跨视角跟踪与球-时证据整合为最弱能力；PanoVideoReasoner在PanoVideoBench达55.7%，较backbone提升12.5点、较此前SOTA提升2.6点，增益集中于基准暴露的空间、跨视角与时间能力。

Conclusion: 接地的球-时监督可赋予通用视频MLLM全景推理能力；基准与推理数据的诊断-训练闭环有效，但为纯视频理解方向，未涉及三维重建、世界模型或具身控制。

Abstract: A panoramic video captures all directions around the camera, but relevant evidence can appear in different directions and at different times. Understanding it requires models to connect observations across the viewing sphere and time. We introduce PanoVideoBench, the first manually annotated benchmark for comprehensive panoramic video understanding. Its 1,000 questions span five categories and 15 question types, with diagnostic annotations for target search and panoramic capabilities. Evaluation of 11 proprietary, open-source, and panoramic-specialized models identifies cross-view tracking and sphere-time evidence integration as their weakest capabilities. To strengthen these capabilities, we construct PanoVideoCoT, the first reasoning dataset grounded in real panoramic video, through sphere-time-grounded synthesis that converts verified panoramic observations and successful agentic explorations into 13,465 QA-CoT pairs. Starting from a general video MLLM, PanoVideoReasoner learns from this corpus through Panoramic CoT SFT and Evidence-Privileged On-Policy Self-Distillation, a panoramic instantiation of Vision-OPD's OPSD in which the teacher uses verified perspective views to guide student-generated trajectories. It reaches 55.7% on PanoVideoBench, improving the backbone by 12.5 points and the previous SOTA by 2.6. The gains concentrate on the same spatial, cross-view, and temporal capabilities exposed by the benchmark, showing that grounded sphere-time supervision can equip a general video MLLM with panoramic reasoning.

</details>


### [72] [Rethinking Asymmetry in Self-Supervised 3D Geometry Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42092)
*Gyeongjin Kang,Freeman Cheng,Botao Ye,Weijie Lyu,Ming-Hsuan Yang,Eunbyung Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将自监督不对称性从输入空间移到3D表示空间：held-out视图仅从重建场景中排除，但共享主干可用全部视图做对应匹配；并利用稀疏/稠密上下文特征对齐与冻结视觉基础模型对齐作为无几何标注的几何训练信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有从无位姿图像自监督学习3D几何的方法为规避平凡解，在输入空间施加不对称（分叉分支、掩码注意力），限制了视图间信息交互；作者认为当目标函数是几何精度而非渲染质量时，这种限制并非必要，反而有害。

Method: 1) 在3D表示空间施加上下文/目标视图的不对称：共享backbone摄取所有视图进行对应估计，仅在场景重建时剔除held-out视图；2) 视图密度自蒸馏：以稠密上下文特征对齐稀疏上下文特征，在渲染损失之外提供几何监督；3) 额外对齐冻结的视觉基础模型特征；全部信号均无需几何标注。

Result: 在深度与位姿估计上优于输入空间不对称设计的先前方法；定量消融实验分离了各组件贡献；自监督预训练还能提升后续有监督训练效果。

Conclusion: 自监督几何学习的关键不对称应施加于3D表示而非输入；视图密度可作为无需教师模型的自监督几何信号，整体流程无需几何标注且可迁移到有监督设置。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning relies on asymmetry between what a model observes and what it must explain. Recent methods learn 3D geometry from unposed images by predicting cameras and scene structure, then rendering held-out views. To avoid trivial solutions, they impose this asymmetry in the input space, restricting how views interact through separate branches or masked attention. We revisit this design with geometric accuracy, rather than rendering quality, as the main objective. When a held-out view lies between context views, the context already constrains the geometry it observes. We therefore impose the asymmetry in the 3D representation space, excluding held-out views only from the reconstructed scene while a shared backbone uses all views for correspondence. This improves depth and pose estimation over input-space designs. We further exploit view density: sparse and dense observations depict the same scene but provide different amounts of correspondence. Aligning sparse-context features with dense-context features supplies a geometric training signal alongside rendering, without a pretrained geometric teacher. We additionally align features with a frozen vision foundation model, which brings further gains. None of these signals requires geometric annotation, and the resulting model improves geometry estimation over prior self-supervised methods. Controlled experiments isolate the contribution of each component, and self-supervised pretraining also improves subsequent supervised training.

</details>


### [73] [A Better Critic Matters for Distribution Matching Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42135)
*Zongze Wu,Xianbang Wang,Zhengyang Geng,Mingdeng Cao,Zhoutong Zhang,Richard Zhang,Eli Shechtman,Ruiming Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DMD 蒸馏中 critic 需与 teacher 联合缩放(joint scaling)，并引入按生成步条件化的 step-aware critic (STAC)，才能为少步学生提供可靠信号，避免训练崩溃并受益于更大 teacher。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DMD 依赖 teacher score 与在线 critic 估计的学生分布 score 之差来训练少步学生，但 critic 需要多大容量、需要何种信息才能提供可靠训练信号尚不清楚；实践中增大 teacher 反而可能损害学生质量甚至训练崩溃。

Method: (1) 容量层面：在 ImageNet 上对七种 SiT 模型规模做受控实验，比较固定小 critic 与 critic 随 teacher 同步缩放的 joint scaling；(2) 信息层面：放弃让 critic 建模与步数无关的学生输出混合分布，提出 STAC，以学生的生成步为条件估计步特定 score。

Result: 固定小 critic 时增大 teacher 会降低学生质量并导致训练崩溃；joint scaling 可防止崩溃，使学生从更大 teacher 中获益；joint scaling + STAC 在类条件 ImageNet 生成、SDXL 与 FLUX.2 的文生图、Wan2.1 的文生视频三类任务上一致提升少步蒸馏效果。

Conclusion: 扩散/流匹配少步蒸馏中的 critic 既需足够容量(与 teacher 联合缩放)，也需步信息(步条件化 score 估计)；二者结合构成通用、跨模态有效的 DMD 改进方案。

Abstract: Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD) trains a few-step student using the difference between a teacher's score and an online critic's estimate of the student distribution's score. We investigate what the critic needs, in capacity and in information, to provide a reliable training signal. On the capacity side, controlled experiments across seven SiT model sizes on ImageNet show that increasing teacher size while keeping a small critic fixed can degrade student quality and cause training collapse. Scaling the critic alongside the teacher, a strategy we call joint scaling, prevents collapse in our experiments and allows the student to benefit from larger teachers. On the information side, rather than forcing the critic to model a step-agnostic mixture of student outputs, we introduce a step-aware critic (STAC) that conditions on the student's generation step to estimate step-specific scores. Together, joint scaling and STAC consistently improve few-step distillation across class-conditional ImageNet generation, text-to-image generation with SDXL and FLUX.2, and text-to-video generation with Wan2.1.

</details>


### [74] [ReLift: Dense 3D Tracking as Lifted Trajectory Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42136)
*Assaf Singer,Maroun Khriesh,Noam Rotstein,Yoni Kasten,Or Litany*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReLift将单目视频稠密3D跟踪重构为"轨迹修复与补全"任务：用预训练2D跟踪器与3D几何模型得到提升（lifted）轨迹，再以轨迹校正器交替沿轨迹/跨轨迹注意力修复误差并补全遮挡段，仅用几何输入训练，可直接泛化到不同跟踪器与几何模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合学习对应、深度与遮挡轨迹需要稀缺的4D监督。作者观察到强预训练2D跟踪器+3D几何模型得到的"提升轨迹"（遮挡段仅插值）已可与专用3D跟踪器持平，但继承了对应与深度误差，且遮挡期深度来自遮挡物而非被跟踪点。

Method: 提出前馈框架ReLift，核心为轨迹校正器（trajectory corrector），沿轨迹与跨轨迹交替注意力，保留可靠观测、修复错误位置、补全缺失运动。训练在3D轨迹上进行并注入模拟的提升误差，从而与图像及具体跟踪器/几何模型解耦，4D监督集中于轨迹精化。

Result: 在WorldTrack上取得最佳平均精度，全点提升2.1 APD、动态点提升3.0 APD；更快配置仍具竞争力且速度快于4RC。

Conclusion: 将3D跟踪分解为强2D跟踪+几何提升+可复用轨迹修复，是高效且可插拔的范式，无需重训即可随上游跟踪器/几何模型进步而受益。

Abstract: Dense 3D tracking from monocular video requires establishing point correspondences over time, estimating their depth, and inferring their trajectories during occlusion. Existing methods typically learn these tasks jointly, a demanding objective given the scarcity of 4D supervision. We show that strong pretrained 2D trackers and 3D geometry models provide an effective foundation: lifted trajectories, with occluded segments simply interpolated, already rival dedicated 3D trackers using the same geometry. However, lifted trajectories inherit correspondence and depth errors, and under occlusion, their depth comes from the occluder rather than the tracked point. We introduce ReLift, an efficient feed-forward framework that recasts dense 3D tracking as geometric trajectory repair and completion. At its core, a trajectory corrector alternates attention along and across trajectories to preserve reliable observations, repair corrupted positions, and complete missing motion. Using only geometric inputs allows us to train directly on 3D trajectories with simulated lifting errors, decoupling training from images and from any specific tracker or geometry model. Concentrating 4D supervision on trajectory refinement yields a reusable model that consistently improves lifted trajectories across trackers and geometry providers without retraining, so it benefits directly from future advances in either component. On WorldTrack, ReLift achieves state-of-the-art mean accuracy, improving on the strongest baselines by 2.1 APD on all points and 3.0 on dynamic points, with faster configurations remaining competitive with leading 3D trackers while running faster than 4RC. Visit our supplementry page for more examples and code.

</details>


### [75] [Latent Reasoning in Continuous Space for Unified Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42149)
*Byungwoo Jeon,Yoonwoo Jeong,Hyunseok Lee,Minsu Cho,Jinwoo Shin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LARC将统一多模态模型的推理从离散token空间迁移到连续隐状态空间，用课程式SFT+信息增益RL两阶段训练，桥接推理与图像生成的模ality gap，在GenEval上超BAGEL 7个百分点并展现测试时扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理增强的统一多模态模型在图像生成上无法继承LLM式的测试时扩展与细粒度可控性，作者将其归因于模态错配：推理发生在离散token空间，而图像生成在连续空间进行，token级瓶颈限制了推理信息的表达与传递。

Method: 提出Latent Reasoning in Continuous space（LARC）框架，直接在连续隐状态上推理。训练分两阶段：(1) 课程式监督微调，逐步把token级推理迁移为隐式推理；(2) 信息增益强化学习，在无ground-truth监督下自我进化隐式推理能力，以信息增益为奖励信号驱动推理轨迹优化。

Result: 在多个文本到图像生成基准上稳定提升生成质量、提示词跟随与组合保真度；GenEval上相比BAGEL提升约7%p达到SOTA；在并行与串行两种测试时扩展策略下均优于基于token的生成，呈现出清晰的test-time scaling行为。

Conclusion: 隐空间推理可有效缓解离散token瓶颈，使统一多模态模型的推理能力可被测试时扩展并转化为生成性能增益，为推理增强的图像生成提供新范式。

Abstract: Unified Multimodal Models (UMMs) achieve remarkable success on diverse multimodal understanding and generation tasks, yet they still struggle to effectively leverage language-based chain-of-thought reasoning for image generation. As a result, existing reasoning-augmented UMMs often fail to inherit the test-time scaling and fine-grained controllability observed in large language models. We attribute this failure to a fundamental modality mismatch: reasoning is performed in a discrete token space, whereas image generation operates in a continuous space. To bridge this gap, we introduce a Latent Reasoning framework in Continuous space (LARC), which alleviates the token-level bottleneck by reasoning over continuous hidden states. Specifically, the training framework consists of two stages: (i) curriculum supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and (ii) information-gain reinforcement learning (RL). First, the curriculum SFT stage gradually converts token-level reasoning into latent reasoning. Subsequently, the information-gain RL stage self-evolves latent reasoning without ground truth supervision. Experiments across diverse text-to-image generation benchmarks show that LARC consistently improves generation quality, prompt following, and compositional fidelity. Notably, LARC achieves state-of-the-art performance on GenEval with a 7%p gain over BAGEL and demonstrates clear test-time scaling behavior, outperforming token-based generation under both parallel and sequential test-time scaling strategies.

</details>


### [76] [GazeRIB: Systematic Evaluation of Multi-Reader Gaze-Guided Chest X-ray Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42152)
*Nattakorn Kittisut,Roy M. Gabriel,Marly van Assen,Mohammadreza Zandehshahvar,Carlo N. De Cecco,Elizabeth A. Krupinski,Ali Adibi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GazeRIB：训练时按放射科医生注视掩蔽未观察区域，推理时用完整图像；8 位专家重复读同一批 CXR，组合出 3/5/8 人小组，在 7 种架构 × 10 个种子（910 个模型）上比较，四人组以外小组亦显著优于无注视基线，三人组即可获得八人组 73–80% 的增益，迁移到外部队列效果依赖数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射科医生的视觉注意力可作为医学图像学习的监督信号，但“读者小组构成”如何影响该信号的价值尚未被检验；作者希望确定多大的专家小组规模足以获得注视监督收益，并考察跨队列可迁移性。

Method: 提出 GazeRIB（Gaze-guided Radiologist-Informed Blackout）：训练时用多读者注视热图掩蔽未被观察的区域，迫使模型关注临床相关区域；推理阶段使用未掩蔽图像以保证输入分布一致。8 位专家重复阅读 80 张胸片，构造单人读者、3 人、5 人、8 人小组四种注视聚合方案；在 7 种网络架构 × 10 个随机种子（共 910 个模型）上训练，在独立的 2,897 例胸片队列上评估四类严重程度预测，并在 SIIM-FISABIO-RSNA 与 MIMIC-Eye 上做零再训练外部验证。

Result: 所有小组方案相对无注视基线 macro F1 提升 5.33–7.47 pp（Bonferroni 校正 p<0.007），而任何单读者均在主指标上未达显著；3 人组即可获得 8 人组在 macro F1、ROC-AUC、AU-PRC 上 73–80% 的增益。外部验证中，四种方案在 SIIM-FISABIO-RSNA 上 ROC-AUC 提升 +1.93 至 +4.24 pp（p≤0.004）；在 MIMIC-Eye 上无显著差异，而单读者平均反而下降 1.40 pp（p=0.008）。

Conclusion: 读者小组构成显著影响注视监督的价值：小组聚合优于个体读者，约 3 名读者即可捕获大部分收益，边际收益递减；跨队列迁移依赖目标数据分布，小样本单读者注视最不稳定。

Abstract: Radiologists' visual attention can supervise medical image learning, but how reader panel composition affects its value remains untested. We evaluated GazeRIB (Gaze-guided Radiologist-Informed Blackout), which masks unviewed regions during training and uses unmasked images at inference. Eight experts repeatedly read the same 80 chest X-rays (CXRs). We compared individual readers with three-, five- and eight-reader panels across seven architectures and 10 seeds, evaluating 910 models on a separate 2,897-CXR cohort for four-class severity prediction. Every panel improved macro F1 over the no-gaze baseline by 5.33-7.47 percentage points (pp; Bonferroni-adjusted p < 0.007); no individual reader reached significance on this endpoint. Three readers captured 73-80% of the eight-reader gain in macro F1, the area under the receiver-operating-characteristic curve (ROC-AUC) and the area under the precision-recall curve (AU-PRC). On external validation without retraining, all four panels improved ROC-AUC on SIIM-FISABIO-RSNA by +1.93 to +4.24 pp (p <= 0.004); on MIMIC-Eye no panel differed significantly from baseline, whereas the single-reader average declined (-1.40 pp, p = 0.008). Reader composition therefore influenced performance, although transfer depended on the cohort.

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [77] [Inter-Frame Predictive Contour Coding for Semantic Map Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40887)
*Runyu Yang,Hyomin Choi,Fabien Racapé,Ivan V. Bajić*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 面向动态语义地图的无损压缩，提出帧间预测的轮廓(链码)编码框架，利用相邻帧语义图的时间相关性，并配合帧内/帧间模式选择以降低码率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态语义地图作为中间表示被广泛使用，其存储与传输成本需要高效无损压缩；已有基于轮廓的方法主要利用单帧内空间冗余，忽略相邻帧间的强时间相关性。

Method: 首先由参考帧预测blob位置与标签索引，再在参考帧对应轮廓的链码单元上进行轮廓预测；引入模式选择机制，依据估计的编码代价在帧内编码与帧间编码间切换，从而在保留轮廓表示优势的同时利用时间轮廓冗余。

Result: 在VSPW动态语义分割数据集上，方法稳定优于现有SOTA无损语义地图压缩方法；相较近期context-adaptive extended chain coding框架，平均码率降低3.0%，而平均编码时间无显著增加。源码已公开。

Conclusion: 通过帧间轮廓预测与模式选择，可在低计算开销下有效提升动态语义地图的无损压缩效率，实现压缩性能与复杂度的良好折中；属于语义地图编码/传输的支撑性工作，与机器人感知数据链路相关但非核心方法突破。

Abstract: Dynamic semantic maps have become an important intermediate representation for many computer vision applications. Efficient lossless compression of such maps is essential for reducing storage and transmission costs. Existing contour-based semantic map compression methods mainly exploit spatial redundancy within individual frames while largely ignoring the strong temporal correlation between adjacent semantic maps. In this paper, we propose an inter-frame predictive contour coding framework for lossless semantic map compression. The proposed framework first predicts blob positions and label indexes from the reference frame and then performs contour prediction using corresponding chain-code units in the reference contours. A mode selection mechanism is further employed to choose between intra and inter coding according to the estimated coding cost. By exploiting temporal contour redundancy while preserving the advantages of contour-based representation, the proposed framework significantly improves coding efficiency. Experimental results on the VSPW dynamic semantic segmentation dataset demonstrate that the proposed method consistently outperforms state-of-the-art lossless semantic map compression methods. Compared with the recent context-adaptive extended chain coding framework, the proposed inter-frame coding achieves an average bitrate reduction of 3.0\% without significant increase in the average encoding time, demonstrating an effective trade-off between compression performance and computational complexity. The source code is made publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [78] [PBSR: Pseudo-Burst Super-Resolution via Zero-Shot Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41258)
*Yuki Kondo,Norimichi Ukita*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 用零样本新视角合成（NVS）从单图生成带真实视差的伪爆发帧，再送入具备几何感知对齐的爆发超分骨干进行微调，实现单图上的爆发式超分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 爆发超分（BSR）靠多帧亚像素信息获得高保真重建，但需要近静态多次连拍，单张照片与档案图像无法满足；作者希望把 BSR 的重建优势迁移到单图输入场景。

Method: 1）按真实爆发数据集的像素位移分布校准视角采样策略，用零样本 NVS 合成具真实视差的伪爆发帧；2）BSR 骨干引入几何感知对齐模块，复用 NVS 相机参数与估计深度做帧间对齐；3）骨干使用伪爆发帧进行"伪影感知"微调，以适应生成帧的合成伪影。

Result: 在 BurstSR 数据集上，与相同重建骨干的单帧基线相比，PSNR、SSIM、LPIPS 三项指标均有一致提升。

Conclusion: 生成式 NVS 可合成有效伪爆发观测，配合几何对齐与伪影感知微调，使单图输入也能受益于多帧聚合；属于对爆发超分数据获取限制的规避思路，尚未在真实单图/档案图像上验证泛化。

Abstract: Burst super-resolution (BSR) achieves higher reconstruction fidelity than single-image super-resolution (SISR) by aggregating sub-pixel information from multiple frames. However, it requires a burst of frames captured under near-static conditions, and such frames are unavailable for single photographs or archival images. We propose Pseudo-Burst Super-Resolution (PBSR), which brings BSR-style reconstruction to the single-image input setting by synthesizing pseudo-burst frames via zero-shot novel view synthesis (NVS). PBSR first generates pseudo-burst frames with realistic parallax using a viewpoint sampling strategy calibrated to the pixel-shift distribution of real burst datasets. The frames are then processed by a BSR backbone equipped with a geometry-aware alignment module that reuses the NVS camera parameters and estimated depth. While the NVS stage is zero-shot, the backbone is fine-tuned with an artifact-aware adaptation so that it can exploit the synthesized frames despite their generative artifacts. Experiments on the BurstSR dataset show that PBSR consistently improves in PSNR, SSIM, and LPIPS over the single-frame baseline with the same reconstruction backbone.

</details>


### [79] [With foundation-model features, aggregator complexity does not improve cross-hospital discrimination in gastric whole-slide image classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41328)
*Nheng Vanchhay,Ali Hussain,Khan Ziaullah,Moon-Il Joo,Md Ariful Islam Mozumder,Rashedul Islam Sumon,Won-Ae Lee,Min-Sun Cho,Hyeong-Chan Shin,Yujun Park,Baek Hwan Cho,Nam-Hoon Cho,Heejung An,Heung-Kook Choi,Haeyoun Kang,Hyunki Kim,Hee Cheol Kim*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 跨五家韩国医院5,036张胃H&E全切片、20x/40x双尺度，在冻结病理基础模型特征上做留一医院验证；提出的分层patch-region-slide图聚合器(CHIME-MIL)配合无标签站点对齐与双尺度融合达AUC 0.837，但同等配方下不如注意力池化，全局最大池化+对齐融合更优(AUC 0.855)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全切片图像分类器通常在单一医院开发、缺乏跨院测试，作者希望系统评估聚合器在未见医院上的泛化，并检验"更复杂聚合器"是否真的带来增益。

Method: 构建5家医院5,036张胃H&E切片（20x与40x），在冻结的病理基础模型特征上比较多种多示例学习聚合器，采用留一医院交叉验证；提出分层patch-region-slide图聚合器CHIME-MIL，结合无标签的站点对齐（减去各医院平均特征向量）与双尺度融合，并做十张无标签切片的新院接入测试、校准误差分析及注意力图删除实验。

Result: CHIME-MIL平均AUC 0.837，优于六个未加对齐/融合的常规注意力与Transformer聚合器；但在匹配配方下未超过注意力池化，全局最大池化在同一对齐与融合下更优(AUC 0.855, P=0.0002)。站点对齐可用10张无标签切片接入新院，无留出医院数据时AUC仅损失0.002，但准确率增益小且不稳定。模型top-label校准误差低(0.053)但恶性概率置信偏低；移除最受关注patch比随机移除显著更降低恶性概率。

Conclusion: 在该设定下，更复杂的聚合器并未提升判别力；可部署系统真正的增量价值在于无标签新院接入、校准检查与可审计证据，而非架构复杂度。

Abstract: Whole-slide image classifiers are usually developed within one hospital and often go untested at unseen hospitals. We assembled 5,036 haematoxylin-and-eosin-stained gastric slides from five Korean hospitals, scanned at 20x and 40x, and compared multiple-instance learning aggregators under leave-one-hospital-out validation on frozen pathology foundation-model features. A hierarchical patch-region-slide graph aggregator (CHIME-MIL) with site alignment and two-scale fusion reached a mean area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) of 0.837, above six conventional attention and transformer aggregators trained without these steps. Under a matched recipe it did not exceed attention-based pooling, and global max pooling given the same alignment and fusion was better (AUC 0.855, P = 0.0002). The alignment, which subtracts each hospital's mean feature vector without labels, onboarded a new hospital from ten unlabelled slides and cost 0.002 AUC without held-out-hospital data, but its accuracy gains were small and inconsistent. The model had low top-label calibration error (0.053) but under-confident malignancy probabilities, and removing its most-attended patches lowered the malignancy probability far more than removing random patches. With these features, more complex aggregators did not improve discrimination; what a deployable system adds is label-free onboarding, calibration checks and auditable evidence.

</details>


### [80] [Bayesian PET Reconstruction with Learned Flow Matching Priors via Langevin Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41337)
*Hengjia Ran,Huafeng Liu,Bo Zhao*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出基于流匹配生成先验的贝叶斯PET重建框架，在潜空间用ULA做后验采样，性能优于经典与深度学习方法并可给出不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PET重建是病态逆问题且受泊松计数噪声影响，低剂量采集尤其困难；需要更强先验并同时具备不确定性量化能力。

Method: 从高质量PET图像学习flow-matching生成先验，将重建表述为贝叶斯推断，在潜空间用Unadjusted Langevin Algorithm进行后验采样。

Result: 数值实验中对标经典重建方法与SOTA深度学习基线，重建性能提升，并实现后验不确定性量化。

Conclusion: 流匹配先验+潜空间后验采样是医学图像逆问题的有效范式；属医学成像领域，与具身智能/3D重建主线关联弱。

Abstract: Image reconstruction for positron emission tomography (PET) is a challenging inverse problem due to its inherent ill-posedness and Poisson counting noise, particularly in low-dose acquisitions. In this work, we introduce a novel Bayesian PET reconstruction framework that leverages a flow-matching generative prior learned from high-quality PET images. By incorporating this prior, we formulate PET reconstruction as a Bayesian inference problem and solve it via latent-space posterior sampling using the unadjusted Langevin algorithm. We evaluate the proposed method through numerical experiments and benchmark it against classical reconstruction methods and state-of-the-art deep learning-based baselines. The proposed method achieves improved reconstruction performance while enabling posterior uncertainty quantification.

</details>


### [81] [PRIN: A Perceptual-Structural Retinex-Inspired Network for Low-Light Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41338)
*Qiyi Xie,Wei Chen Ng,Ziyao Li,Yuning Wu,Huasen Wu,Tianshi Miao,Jian Ding Tan*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出感知-结构 Retinex 启发网络 PRIN，用共享编码器+非对称解码器分别恢复反射率与光照，反射率分支做注意力引导的边缘感知小波细化，光照分支做空间一致亮度调整，重组后用曲线式后处理头校正色调与曝光。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习式低光增强方法像素保真度好但纹理过平滑、偏色、光照空间不一致；Retinex 类方法虽可解释分解，却未通过分支特定的频域细化来刻画反射率与光照在恢复中的非对称作用。

Method: Retinex 先验 + 恢复角色感知增强：共享编码器与非对称解码器估计反射率和光照；反射率分支采用注意力引导、边缘感知的小波域细化以保纹理与边界；光照分支强调空间连贯的亮度调整；重组后由基于曲线的后合成增强头校准残余色调与曝光偏差。

Result: LOL-v1 上 SSIM 并列最优、LPIPS 次低；LOL-v2-Syn 上 SSIM 与 LPIPS 均最优；LOL-v2-Real 上 PSNR 最高、LPIPS 次低。指标提升为增量式，且未报告效率、泛化到非配对真实场景或下游任务验证。

Conclusion: 非对称分支+频域细化是合理的 Retinex 增强设计，但属低光图像增强领域的增量改进，与具身智能、3D 重建/世界模型、VLA 等核心方向无直接关联，优先级偏低。

Abstract: Low-light image enhancement (LLIE) focuses not only on visibility recovery but also on preserving structurally meaningful content and perceptual naturalness. Existing learning-based methods may achieve favorable pixel-wise fidelity while still producing over-smoothed textures, color deviations, or spatially incoherent illumination. Retinex-inspired methods provide interpretable decomposition into reflectance and illumination components, however, the asymmetric restoration roles of these components are rarely encoded through branch-specific frequency-domain refinement. To address this issue, we propose a Perceptual-Structural Retinex-Inspired Network (PRIN), which integrates Retinex priors with restoration-role-aware enhancement. PRIN employs a shared encoder and asymmetric decoders to estimate reflectance and illumination components. The reflectance branch performs attention-guided and edge-aware wavelet refinement to preserve textures and boundaries, whereas the illumination branch promotes spatially coherent brightness adjustment. After the recomposition of reflectance and illumination branches, a curve-based post-composition enhancement head calibrates residual tone and exposure deviations. Experiments on LOL-v1, LOL-v2-Syn, and LOL-v2-Real show that the proposed network ties for the best structural similarity index (SSIM) and obtains the second-lowest learned perceptual image patch similarity (LPIPS) on LOL-v1, achieves the best SSIM and LPIPS on LOL-v2-Syn, and obtains the highest peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR) and the second-lowest LPIPS on LOL-v2-Real.

</details>


### [82] [TopoHyperClinicalGraph: A Geometry and Topology-Aware Multimodal Framework for Cancer Survival Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41556)
*Noaman Khan,Zubair Shah*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出TopoHyperClinicalGraph多模态框架，融合双曲组织几何、持续性拓扑描述子与五节点临床图学习，用于TCGA/CPTAC八个队列的癌症生存预测，C-index普遍优于病理only的PatchGCN，其中几何贡献最大、拓扑与临床图增益较小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有病理基础模型生存预测忽略了组织结构的层级性与多模态临床信息；作者希望把双曲几何、持续性同调拓扑特征与临床表格信息分层叠加，并支持各成分贡献的可分离评估。

Method: UNI2-h特征经训练折PCA降至1024维；病理骨干用双曲邻域+Lorentz质心注意力池化得到生存logit并冻结；再用每切片最多64个landmark的持续性描述子加采样控制校正；最后五节点临床图（age/sex/stage/T/N）学习额外修正。五折患者级交叉验证，八队列2364例。

Result: C-index：COADREAD 0.7563 vs PatchGCN 0.7386，HNSC 0.6220 vs 0.5998，BRCA 0.6755 vs 0.6535，STAD 0.6409 vs 0.6176；CPTAC HNSC 0.5984、PDA 0.6060、CCRCC 0.8055、LUAD 0.7192。增益主要来自几何，持续性拓扑增益较小；临床图融合仅在5个队列超过节点级对照，3个CPTAC队列中临床单模态优于融合。

Conclusion: 该框架可分离评估几何、拓扑、临床三类贡献，但所有指标均经验证集选择，缺乏独立外部测试，泛化性未确立；增益幅度有限且临床融合不稳定。

Abstract: We developed TopoHyperClinicalGraph, a multimodal framework combining hyperbolic tissue geometry, persistent topology and clinical graph learning for cancer survival prediction. Pre-extracted UNI2-h features are reduced from 1,536 to 1,024 dimensions using training-fold PCA. A pathology backbone combines hyperbolic neighborhoods with Lorentz-centroid attention pooling. Once frozen, its survival logits are adjusted using persistence descriptors from up to 64 landmarks per slide and sampling controls. A five-node clinical graph then learns a further correction from age, sex, stage, T and N. Five-fold patient-level evaluation covered eight TCGA and CPTAC cohorts with 2,364 patients. Mean validation-selected C-indices for TopoHyperClinicalGraph versus pathology-only spatial PatchGCN were 0.7563 versus 0.7386 in COADREAD, 0.6220 versus 0.5998 in HNSC, 0.6755 versus 0.6535 in BRCA and 0.6409 versus 0.6176 in STAD. CPTAC scores were 0.5984 in HNSC, 0.6060 in PDA, 0.8055 in CCRCC and 0.7192 in LUAD. CPTAC-HNSC used TCGA-initialized adaptation; other CPTAC backbones were trained within their cohorts. Geometry accounted for most pathology-only gains, with smaller persistence effects. Clinical graph fusion exceeded its matched node-wise control in five cohorts, tied in one and scored lower in two; clinical-only prediction exceeded fusion in three CPTAC cohorts. The framework supports separate evaluation of geometric, topological and clinical contributions. All scores were validation-selected; independent testing is needed to establish generalizability.

</details>


### [83] [Enhancing Medical Lesion Segmentation via Several Simple Modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41652)
*Ruichen Li,Zhao Yao*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 该论文为医学病灶分割提出三个轻量增强模块：DT-Morph组合损失（连续空间池化优化可微拓扑结构与边界连续性）、3D Group Prompt（基于SAT基线的体素-掩码对齐提示与文本提示提取域通用知识）、以及带同义词解析路由的Many-to-One条件分割头，实现近乎零开销的增量类别学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 罕见病理缺乏高质量标注导致推理时形态畸变（DSC虚高）；分割任务孤立开发阻碍跨域方法创新，需共享同解剖区域不同病变的知识；历史数据与标注受限使高效知识提取与增量学习（避免灾难性遗忘）成为瓶颈。

Method: 1) DT-Morph Combined Loss + 连续空间池化，优化可微拓扑结构与边界连续性；2) 3D Group Prompt，在SAT基线上用体素图像-掩码对齐提示与文本提示提取域通用知识；3) Many-to-One Conditional Segmentation Head，配同义词解析路由替代传统输出层，计算开销可忽略并支持快速增量学习。

Result: 在腹部与腹部外数据集上，覆盖器官与病灶分割的消融实验验证了各模块有效性。

Conclusion: 轻量模块组合可提升数据稀缺、算力受限场景下的医学分割临床可用性，兼顾拓扑正确性与增量学习能力。

Abstract: This work introduces three lightweight enhancement modules for medical lesion segmentation to improve clinical applicability, particularly under data-scarce and computationally constrained environments. Current mainstream methods struggle with the lack of high-quality annotations for rare pathologies, which often leads to severe morphological distortions during inference, despite achieving seemingly ideal DSC scores. Furthermore, the isolated development of individual segmentation tasks restricts cross-domain methodological innovation, underscoring the need for leveraging shared knowledge across different lesions within the same anatomical region. However, constrained access to historical data and annotations hinders efficient knowledge extraction and triggers catastrophic forgetting when adapting to new classes. To address these challenges, we first propose DT-Morph Combined Loss with continuous spatial pooling to optimize differentiable topological structures and boundary continuity. Second, we introduce 3D Group Prompt, which extracts domain-universal knowledge via curated volumetric image-mask alignment prompts together with text prompts built on SAT baseline. Finally, we replace conventional output layers with Many-to-One Conditional Segmentation Head equipped with synonym-resolved routing, which brings negligible computational cost and enables fast incremental learning for new categories without sacrificing performance on old tasks. Ablation studies on abdominal and extra-abdominal datasets covering both organ and lesion segmentation verify the efficacy of our modules.

</details>


### [84] [IGDA-ICMH: an Importance Guided Feature and Network Dual Adaptation Framework towards Image Compression for Machine and Human Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41924)
*Yanbo Gao,Xinyu Zhao,Shuai Li,Xun Cai,Mao Ye,Li Li*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出重要性引导的特征与网络双重自适应框架（IGDA），用于机器与人类视觉联合的图像压缩（ICMH），通过量化分析特征/网络层对下游任务的不等贡献，结合隐式神经表示实现高效的任务自适应压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ICMH研究多聚焦任务特定适配或多任务联合优化，忽视不同特征与网络层对下游机器视觉任务贡献不均的问题，导致码率与适配参数浪费。

Method: 以隐式神经表示为基本架构；先定量研究不同特征与网络层对机器视觉性能的影响；设计重要性引导网络自适应（IGNA）模块，采用低秩适配按任务重要性选择性调整关键网络层；设计重要性引导特征自适应（IGFA）模块，使用低秩+Kronecker积变换挖掘特征非局部性以减少适配参数。

Result: 在图像分类与目标检测等代表性机器视觉任务上，IGDA-ICMH显著优于现有基于隐式神经表示的ICMH方法。

Conclusion: 特征与网络层的重要性差异可被定量刻画并用于指导压缩适配；特征—网络双重低秩适配在参数高效前提下提升了机器视觉任务的压缩后性能。

Abstract: With the surge of machine vision applications, image compression for both machine and human vision (ICMH) has become an important research area. Existing studies mainly focus on task-specific adaptation or multi-task joint optimization, yet they often overlook the unequal contributions of different features and network layers to downstream machine vision tasks. To address this issue, we first quantitatively investigate the impact of different feature and network layers on machine vision performance, providing a principled understanding of their importance. Based on this analysis, an Importance Guided feature and network Dual Adaptation (IGDA) framework is developed for ICMH. It adopts implicit neural representation as the basic architecture and designs an efficient adaptation structure with both feature and network weight adaptation. An Importance-Guided Network Adaptation (IGNA) module is developed with a low-rank adaptation strategy to selectively adjust key network layers according to their importance in different tasks. An Importance-Guided Feature Adaptation (IGFA) module is developed with a low-rank and Kronecker product-based transform to explore the non-local property of the features in order to reduce the number of adaptation parameters. Extensive experiments on representative machine vision tasks, including image classification and object detection, demonstrate that our IGDA-ICMH framework achieves significantly better performance than the existing implicit neural representation-based methods for ICMH.

</details>


<div id='cs.GT'></div>

# cs.GT [[Back]](#toc)

### [85] [How a group that cannot communicate knows itself by its enemy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40835)
*Gregory Falco*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 通信受限的团队可通过观察对手对其整体行动的"泄露信号"(tell)来获得任何单个成员都不知道的信息；团队预先承诺响应策略可控制对手隐藏信息的代价，从而决定信息博弈的均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对无法互相通信的团队（静默猎手、被干扰机器人、无接触交易者）如何在缺乏内部消息的情况下获取全局信息的问题，作者主张信息不仅是被采集或聚合的，而是由团队对响应的承诺方式"生产"出来的。

Method: 将团队与一个对整体行为做出反应、并希望隐藏自身状态的对手建模为信号博弈：单个"tell"可替代所有成员的消息；团队预先承诺响应策略以设定对手隐藏的成本；在可解的高斯模型中求均衡，并给出判定该结论在有限博弈中成立的精确检验；进一步分析信任在成员间分配方式（单成员+私密随机源 vs 两个可互换成员差异化响应、信任折扣机制）。

Result: 承诺团队在对手更看重自身目标时不会失败；当对手的利害超过撒谎收益时，团队性能等同完全知情团队；低于该阈值时"少信任反而学到更多"，高斯模型中团队承诺不使用全部真值仍可获真值并保留对手本应支付的隐藏成本；两个差异化响应的可互换成员可替代私密随机源（平方误差损失下），小折扣信任可防误判对手。

Conclusion: 把信息获取重新理解为团队承诺策略的产出，为无通信多智能体协作提供了一条不依赖消息传递的信息机制与可验证的均衡判据。

Abstract: Teams that cannot communicate seem stuck with what each member sees. We show that their opponent can tell them the rest. Silent hunters, jammed robots and traders who never meet all face an adversary that reacts to the group as a whole; every member can read that reaction, a tell, and learn what no member knows alone. One tell can do the work of a message from every member. The adversary would rather hide, but hiding interferes with its own task, and a team that commits in advance to how it will respond decides how much hiding is worth. A committed team never loses when its opponent cares more about its own goals, and does as well as a fully informed team exactly when the opponent's stake outweighs any gain from lying. Below that point the team learns more by trusting less: in a solved Gaussian model it obtains the truth by promising not to use all of it and keeps the cost the opponent would have paid to conceal; an exact test decides when this holds in finite games. How a team spreads its trust matters too. A lone uninformed member with a full-support prior needs a private coin, but with additive squared-error losses two interchangeable members who respond differently replace it, and a small discount on trust insures against misjudging the enemy. Information, on this view, is not only gathered or aggregated; it is produced by how a team commits to respond.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [86] [TalkFlow: Structured Latent Flow Matching for Speech Driven Holistic Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40972)
*Xinmu Wang,Fumi Honda,Xiang Gao,Xianfeng Gu,Xiyun Song,Heather Yu*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: TalkFlow 提出三流结构化潜空间 + 流匹配框架，从语音生成上身、下身与面部协调的整体动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 整体语音驱动动作生成面临部位异构动力学、长程时序一致性与跨部位协调三大难点；直接建模高维动作不稳定，而过度压缩或缺乏结构的潜空间会丢失细粒度动作细节，且把异构动作塞进单一潜空间不合理。

Method: 学习上身、下身、面部三个连续潜流表示以保留部位特定动力学；在潜表示学习与基于流的生成两阶段引入轻量跨流交互以维持整体协调；以多层级语音特征为条件做语音条件潜空间流匹配，学习从源潜变量到真实动作潜变量的连续传输过程。

Result: 在 BEAT2 数据集上，面部表情准确度、身体姿态质量与语音-动作同步性均表现良好；定性对比与用户研究显示生成结果比现有 SOTA 更自然、更具表现力且更贴合语音。

Conclusion: 结构化解耦潜空间 + 跨流交互 + 流匹配是可扩展的语音驱动整体动作生成范式，但缺少定量指标数字与真实 embodied 场景（如机器人/数字人闭环）验证。

Abstract: Holistic co-speech motion generation aims to synthesize coordinated facial expressions, hand gestures, and body movements from speech, which is challenging due to heterogeneous part-specific dynamics, long-range temporal coherence, and cross-part coordination. Directly modeling high-dimensional motion can be unstable, while overly compact or unstructured latent spaces may lose fine-grained motion details. We present \textbf{TalkFlow}, a structured latent flow framework for speech-driven holistic motion generation. TalkFlow learns a continuous three-stream latent representation for upper-body motion, lower-body motion, and facial expressions, preserving part-specific dynamics without forcing heterogeneous motion components into a single latent space. To maintain holistic coordination, lightweight cross-stream interactions are incorporated in both latent representation learning and flow-based generation. Conditioned on multi-level speech features, TalkFlow performs speech-conditioned latent flow matching, learning a continuous transport process from source latents to realistic motion latents. Experiments on the BEAT2 dataset demonstrate strong performance across facial expression accuracy, body gesture quality, and speech-motion synchronization. Qualitative comparisons and user studies further show that TalkFlow generates more natural, expressive, and speech-aligned holistic motion than state-of-the-art methods. The project page is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [87] [SemEllipsoid: Semantic-aware Shape Abstraction with Ellipsoid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42145)
*Kuankuan Cheng,Haocheng Yuan,Changjian Li*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 提出“语义感知形状抽象”新任务：用椭球基元将3D形状分解为既几何保真又严格贴合单一语义部件的抽象表示；SemEllipsoid为免训练优化方法，借助基础模型语义线索交替优化语义分配与基元配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有几何感知形状抽象只追求以最少基元逼近几何，忽略语义一致性，基元常跨越多个语义部件，导致部件级编辑、操作等下游任务出现伪影。作者希望基元不跨越语义边界。

Method: 以椭球为基元，将语义约束注入基元优化：设计完全可微的“投影—混合—评估”流程度量基元与输入语义掩码的语义偏差，并用交替优化迭代求解语义分配（每个基元归属单一部件）与基元几何参数。方法非学习式，无需数据收集或网络训练，可泛化到任意类别，并支持按用户需求调整抽象层级。

Result: 在多个3D形状集合上的实验与评估验证了方法有效性，可在保持几何保真度的同时实现语义一致的基元分解，并支持多层级抽象。

Conclusion: 语义感知形状抽象是可定义为新任务的重要问题；将基础模型语义先验化为可微约束并配合交替优化，是无训练、跨类别可用的有效方案，有利于部件级编辑与操作等下游应用。

Abstract: Representing 3D shapes using a set of primitives - known as shape abstraction - is an actively studied problem in computer graphics with wide applications in shape understanding, analysis, and simulation due to the compactness and computational efficiency of the primitive-based representation. Prior work has primarily focused on geometry-aware shape abstraction, where the objective is to approximate the input shape with a minimal set of primitives while preserving geometric fidelity. However, these methods often overlook semantic consistency, inevitably leading to primitives that span across multiple semantic parts. Such inconsistency can cause undesirable artifacts in downstream tasks such as part-level shape editing or manipulation. In this paper, we introduce a new task - semantic-aware shape abstraction, where the goal is to produce abstractions that are both geometrically faithful and semantically consistent, ensuring that each primitive adheres strictly to a single semantic part without crossing semantic boundaries. We propose SemEllipsoid, a novel method to address this problem using ellipsoidal primitives. The core idea is to inject semantic constraints into the primitive optimization process leveraging the availability of semantic cues from large-scale foundation models. We design a fully differentiable projection-blending-evaluation scheme to measure semantic divergence against input semantic masks, and formulate an alternating optimization strategy to iteratively solve for optimal semantic assignment and primitive configurations. Our method is non-learning-based, requiring no data collection or network training, and generalizes well to shapes from arbitrary categories. It also supports different levels of abstraction according to user demand. Extensive experiments and evaluations validate the effectiveness of our approach across several 3D shape collections.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [88] [CuPIQP: A Batched and Differentiable QP Solver on GPUs for Robot Learning and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41021)
*Fenglong Song,Roland Schwan,Yuwen Chen,Yuning Jiang,Colin N. Jones*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: cuPIQP：基于近端内点法的GPU批量化可微QP求解器，统一支持稠密、稀疏与多阶段问题，单批最多16384个QP，求解速度较Moreau快18.8×、较8核PIQP快4.26×，端到端学习训练加速4.6×（vs QPAX）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆运动学、全身控制、MPC等机器人任务以QP为核心构件，向大规模机器人学习与端到端训练扩展时需并发求解大量QP并对解求导，现有求解器缺乏高效GPU批处理与统一结构支持。

Method: 提出近端内点法（proximal interior-point）GPU批处理求解器，跨独立QP并行化并利用问题内部结构，同时保持逐问题的数值更新与终止判定的独立性；通过可微实现支持反向传播，并统一处理稠密、稀疏与多阶段（multistage）形式。

Result: 批量规模达16384时，求解时间较Moreau加速最高18.8×、较8核CPU版PIQP加速4.26×；端到端学习任务中训练较QPAX加速4.6×、较Moreau加速7.8×。

Conclusion: cuPIQP以更低的计算成本提供高效、可复用的基础设施，便于将基于模型的优化嵌入大规模机器人学习与控制流程；属工具型贡献，未涉及真实机器人部署验证。

Abstract: Quadratic programs (QPs) are fundamental building blocks of many robotic tasks such as inverse kinematics, whole-body control, and model predictive control. Extending these methods to large-scale robot learning and control requires solving many QPs concurrently and, for end-to-end learning, differentiating through their solutions. Efficient batched optimization is therefore essential to bringing established model-based methods into GPU-based robotics pipelines. We present \textsc{cuPIQP}, a batched and differentiable QP solver on GPUs based on a proximal interior-point method, with unified support for dense, sparse, and multistage problems. The solver exploits parallelism across independent QPs and their internal structure while preserving per-problem numerical updates and termination decisions. Benchmarks with up to 16384 QPs per batch demonstrate solve-time speedups of up to $18.8\times$ over \textsc{Moreau} and $4.26\times$ over eight-core \textsc{PIQP}. In an end-to-end learning task, \textsc{cuPIQP} accelerates training by $4.6\times$ over \textsc{QPAX} and $7.8\times$ over \textsc{Moreau}. By reducing the computational cost, \textsc{cuPIQP} provides efficient, reusable infrastructure for integrating model-based optimization into large-scale robot learning and control.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [89] [No Channels Left Behind: Understanding Diffusion Acceleration through Residual Gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40914)
*Junha Park,Jaemyung Yu,Jaehui Hwang,Jong-Seok Lee,Dongyoon Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视扩散Transformer训练加速方法（REPA、色散正则化）的机理，发现这些方法显著重组了AdaLN-Zero残差门控α的通道组织，并提出nPR指标与无需教师的径向目标以拓宽门控参与度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解释将加速归因于额外监督、空间结构先验或特征几何改善，作者怀疑加速部分源于残差门控通路的重塑，需给出可测量的机理视角。

Method: 分析AdaLN-Zero中α的门控几何与敏感性；提出归一化参与比（nPR）刻画门的通道级组织；提出简单、无需教师的径向目标，通过特征范数正则化拓宽门控参与度，避免成对特征比较。

Result: 加速模型对α扰动更敏感，门几何反映类别语义关系；在REPA教师、投影器架构与各类径向目标上，更宽的门参与度对应更好生成质量；ImageNet 256×256 SiT-B/2上FID优于色散损失，且跨时间步与类别的平均门参与度提升。

Conclusion: 残差门控重组是扩散训练加速的一条可测量轴，为加速提供了简单可行的方向，但不涉及智能体、具身或3D重建等核心议题。

Abstract: Training acceleration methods for diffusion transformers, such as REPA and dispersive regularization, are commonly explained by additional supervision, spatial-structure priors, or improved feature geometry. We revisit this view and uncover a striking internal change: the AdaLN-Zero residual gate $\alpha$, which directly controls how conditioned updates enter the residual stream, is substantially reorganized by these methods. This observation motivates our hypothesis that acceleration arises, at least in part, from reshaping the residual gating pathway rather than solely from the feature structures explicitly imposed by auxiliary objectives. Supporting this view, accelerated models are markedly more sensitive to perturbations of $\alpha$, and their gate geometry reflects semantic relationships among classes. We further introduce the normalized participation ratio (nPR) to characterize the channel-wise organization of the gates and find that, across REPA teachers, projector architectures, and radial-objective targets, broader gate participation accompanies better generation quality. Guided by this insight, we propose a simple teacher-free radial objective that promotes broader gate participation through feature-norm regularization, without pairwise feature comparisons. On ImageNet $256\times256$ with SiT-B/2, our method improves FID over dispersive loss while increasing mean gate participation over timesteps and classes, further supporting the link between gate organization and training acceleration. Our results identify residual-gate reorganization as a measurable axis of diffusion training acceleration and suggest a simple direction for further acceleration.

</details>


### [90] [CAMEL: Cross-Architecture Masked Embedding Learning for Ultra-Lightweight Retinal Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40954)
*Andre Guarnier De Mitri,Giovani Decico Lucafo,Miller Matheus Lima Anacleto Rocha,Diego Furtado Silva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CAMEL用跨架构、非对称掩码的嵌入蒸馏，把RETFound类视网膜基础模型压缩为轻量学生模型，在四个数据集上兼顾精度与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RETFound等眼科视网膜基础模型性能优异但计算开销大，难以在硬件受限、时延敏感的临床环境部署，SOTA与实际临床可扩展性之间存在鸿沟。

Method: 提出CAMEL（跨架构掩码嵌入学习）蒸馏框架：在嵌入学习中采用非对称掩码策略，将大型教师基础模型的知识迁移到紧凑轻量的学生模型，并允许教师与学生架构不同。

Result: 在四个数据集上的实验表明，CAMEL在显著降低计算复杂度的同时保持精度，得到适用于资源受限临床场景的轻量高精度模型。

Conclusion: 跨架构掩码嵌入蒸馏是提升眼科基础模型临床可部署性的可行路径，为资源受限环境下的医学影像筛查提供了高效方案。

Abstract: Recent progress in deep learning has substantially advanced automated grading of ophthalmologic pathologies, supported by large annotated datasets and improved representation learning. However, a significant gap remains between state-of-the-art performance and clinical scalability. In particular, foundation models have reshaped image-based medical screening and diagnosis. These large-scale architectures trained on extensive datasets learn rich, generalizable feature representations that can be adapted to a wide range of downstream tasks. In ophthalmology, RETFound, one of the most relevant foundation models for retinal images, performs strongly across classification, segmentation, and risk prediction tasks. Despite their effectiveness, foundation models are often computationally prohibitive, hindering their deployment in real-world clinical environments where hardware constraints and latency are critical. To bridge this gap, we introduce CAMEL (Cross-Architecture Masked Embedding Learning), a novel cross-architecture distillation framework that uses an asymmetric masking strategy during embedding learning to effectively transfer knowledge from massive teacher foundation models to compact, lightweight student models. Our experimental evaluation across four datasets shows that CAMEL preserves accuracy while drastically reducing computational complexity, enabling lightweight yet highly accurate models suitable for resource-limited clinical settings.

</details>


### [91] [Beyond Scarcity: When Encoder Adaptation Is Worthwhile](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41213)
*Zexing Zhang,Jichao Li,Tianyang Lei,Yiyin Zhang,Kewei Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个受控实验框架，把"稀有类可用样本唯一支撑量（unique support）"与"表示分配（representation allocation）"解耦，并通过对同一已校正模型做"仅分类器微调"与"全网络继续训练"的匹配对比，量化编码器继续适配的增量价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长尾识别通常先在共享表示上学习、再校正分类器偏置；但分类器重平衡之后，最终准确率无法说明"是否还值得继续适配编码器"。而标准长尾基准同时混淆了两件事：稀有类可用的不同样本数量，以及这些样本影响表示学习的频次，因而难以回答上述问题。

Method: 构建受控评估框架：分阶段控制（前置共享表示学习 + 后置分类器校正）、多种表示目标与适配目标（如不同损失/归一化/数据增强设置）、全分布指标；从同一已校正模型出发，分别进行仅分类器继续训练与全网络继续训练，估计编码器更新的增量收益；在类别分配、支撑集重构与优化运行三个层面做分层统计推断（层次化置信区间），并以预设的1个百分点操作裕度为判据。

Result: 在唯一支撑量固定不变的情况下，仅当表示分配变得更不均衡时，同一"低支撑"设定才从"仅训练分类器已足够"转变为"编码器适配带来实际意义收益"；该转变在多种分类器校正方式、表示目标、归一化与增强控制条件及 ImageNet 规模下均稳定出现，同时不牺牲常见类准确率；在1个百分点裕度下，两种状态由同时置信界清晰分离。

Conclusion: 长尾基准的构造方式本身会改变"校正后是否继续训练编码器"这一决策；仅凭支撑样本数量并不能刻画表示适配的剩余价值，表示分配（样本被用于塑造表示的频率）是关键混淆因素。

Abstract: Long-tailed recognition often learns a shared representation before correcting the classifier for class imbalance. Once the classifier has been rebalanced, however, final accuracy does not reveal whether further encoder adaptation remains worthwhile. Standard imbalance benchmarks obscure this question because they jointly vary the number of distinct examples available to rare classes and the frequency with which those examples shape the representation. We introduce a controlled evaluation framework that separates unique support from representation allocation and estimates the incremental value of encoder updates through matched classifier-only and full-network continuations from a common corrected model. The framework combines stage-specific controls, multiple representation and adaptation objectives, whole-distribution metrics, and hierarchical inference over class assignments, support reconstructions, and optimization runs. Across the controlled study, the same low-support regime moves from one in which classifier-only training is adequate to one in which encoder adaptation yields a practically meaningful benefit solely as representation allocation becomes more imbalanced. The transition persists across classifier corrections, representation objectives, normalization and augmentation controls, and ImageNet-scale evaluation, while preserving frequent-class accuracy. Under the prespecified one-percentage-point operating margin, the two regimes are separated by simultaneous confidence bounds. These results show that benchmark construction can alter a post-correction training decision and that support size alone does not characterize the remaining value of representation adaptation.

</details>


### [92] [FOCUS: First-Order Control Using Optimal Transport Supervision for Imitation from Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41540)
*João A. Cândido Ramos,Lionel Blondé,Hugues Vinzant,Jacopo Castellini,Alexandros Kalousis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FOCUS结合局部最优传输对齐与短时域可微actor-critic，实现无需时间对齐的观测式模仿学习，在接触丰富任务（含152肌肉人形）中提升样本效率与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观测式模仿学习（LfO）需在无时间对应的情况下从纯状态演示中恢复行为；可微仿真器虽提供路径级策略梯度，但长时域、接触丰富轨迹的反向传播易导致优化不稳定。

Method: 用最优传输将智能体 rollout 窗口与多个采样的专家片段匹配，构造软 best-of-K 目标，强调局部相容匹配；由传输代价生成可微模仿奖励，短时域可微 actor-critic 中的学习 critic 对超出 rollout 时域的价值进行 bootstrap；无需时间对齐轨迹、专家初始化或 teacher forcing。

Result: 在 DFlex 的接触丰富运动任务（含152个肌肉激活控制的人形）上，相比对抗式 LfO 方法与已有可微模仿基线，样本效率与训练稳定性更优。

Conclusion: 短时域可微优化＋局部最优传输对齐是缓解长轨迹可微不稳定性的有效方案，为接触丰富场景下的观测式模仿提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Imitation learning from observations must recover behavior from state-only demonstrations without known temporal correspondence. Differentiable simulators provide pathwise policy gradients, but differentiating through long, contact-rich trajectories can make optimization unstable. We introduce FOCUS, which combines local optimal-transport alignment with short-horizon differentiable actor-critic learning. FOCUS matches agent rollout windows to multiple sampled expert segments using a soft best-of-$K$ objective that emphasizes locally compatible matches. The resulting transport-based costs provide differentiable imitation rewards, while a learned critic bootstraps value beyond the rollout horizon. FOCUS requires neither temporally aligned trajectories nor expert-aligned initialization or teacher forcing. Across contact-rich locomotion tasks in DFlex, including a musculoskeletal humanoid controlled by 152 muscle activations, FOCUS improves sample efficiency and training stability over adversarial imitation-from-observation methods and prior differentiable imitation baselines.

</details>


<div id='physics.flu-dyn'></div>

# physics.flu-dyn [[Back]](#toc)

### [93] [ART of PIV: Agentic Real-Time Particle Image Velocimetry for Active Flow Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41304)
*Antonio Terpin,Francesco Banelli,Alan Bonomi,Raffaello D'Andrea*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: ART of PIV: a learned agent selects a PIV pipeline configuration (preprocessing/estimator/postprocessing) per image pair from a speed-budgeted library, cutting AEPE 12.2–22.4% and improving closed-loop velocity regulation.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PIV accuracy depends on many expert-tuned pipeline choices that degrade when flow conditions change; real-time control feedback demands simultaneously accurate and fast measurements under varying conditions.

Method: Build a library of pipeline configurations spanning cross-correlation, classical optical flow, and learning-based estimators under a given speed budget; a learned agent selects one configuration per image pair according to a specified measurement objective. Deployed in a simulated local velocity regulation loop (classical controller) and in a tabletop water channel fluidic pinball (three rotating cylinders), with ROI/estimator selection variants and a DreamerV3 controller trained directly in the physical world.

Result: 12.2–22.4% AEPE reduction vs. best single estimator at matched speed, gains holding at 1000–4000 image pairs/s; 13.24–13.59 pp lower mean normalized closed-loop cost vs. a tuned fixed estimator; physical-world tracking of a prescribed wake flow field with a DreamerV3 controller.

Conclusion: Adaptive, agent-driven per-frame configuration selection outperforms any fixed estimator for real-time flow measurement and control, and transfers to physical fluidic experiments where RL controllers are trained directly on hardware.

Abstract: Particle image velocimetry (PIV) measures fluid motion from images of tracer particles, but its accuracy depends on choices throughout the estimation pipeline, including image preprocessing, flow estimation, and postprocessing. These choices are typically left to expert tuning, and a fixed configuration can become less accurate as conditions change. This matters especially when PIV supplies feedback for real-time control, where measurements must be both accurate and fast, and flow conditions vary. We introduce ART of PIV -- short for Agentic Real-Time and also a nod to the art of tuning a PIV pipeline -- which automates pipeline choice for each image pair. A learned agent selects a configuration according to a chosen measurement objective from a library assembled for a specified speed budget. Across cross-correlation, classical optical-flow, and learning-based estimators, ART of PIV reduces average end-point error (AEPE) by 12.2-22.4 % relative to the best single estimator at matched speed, with gains extending to 1000-4000 image pair/s. In a simulated local velocity regulation task, it reduces mean normalized closed-loop cost by 13.24-13.59 percentage points relative to a tuned fixed estimator, with the same classical controller in both cases. In a tabletop water channel, we illustrate two ways to tune ART of PIV for a fluidic pinball of three rotating cylinders: selecting between complementary optical-flow tunings for accuracy, and selecting regions of interest with coherent rotation or shear using a common flow estimator. For the first variant, we train a DreamerV3 reinforcement learning controller directly in the physical world and demonstrate tracking of a prescribed flow field in the wake of a fluidic pinball.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

### [94] [Robot Navigation and Canine Welfare: A Pilot Study Surfacing Challenges for Cross-Species Legibility and Proxy Validity in Animal-Machine Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40921)
*Haoxiang Zhang,Nancy Smith*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 一项先导研究：在城市住宅环境中让自主配送机器人以"外弧绕行"(curve-out)方式略过伴侣犬，并依据犬类社交沟通与"可读运动"(legible motion)理论设计，实验表明其反而比直线基线引发更强的急性应激反应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配送机器人日益与伴侣动物共享居住空间，但导航行为设计几乎不考虑动物福利；现有导航/可读运动研究均以人类为受众，缺乏面向非人类动物的可读性设计依据。

Method: 采用research through design（通过设计做研究）方法，以犬只"警戒距离"触发横向弧形绕行轨迹构成第一次设计迭代；随后在自然家庭环境中与一只伴侣犬进行受控对比测试，将其与直线通过基线在应激指标上比较。

Result: 实验结果与假设相反：curve-out绕行导致比直线基线更剧烈的应激反应。作者归因于"不连续执行局限"——机器人需多次停止、重新定向以逼近弧形轨迹，破坏了行为可读性，而不可读本身成为应激源。

Conclusion: 该先导研究揭示两大结构性挑战：机器人行为在非人类视角下的可读性，以及替代刺激(proxy stimulus)效度问题；据此重新定义了面向动物福利的AMI设计问题。方法上仍属单个案探索，需更大样本与更平滑轨迹执行加以验证。

Abstract: Autonomous delivery robots increasingly share residential spaces with companion animals, yet navigation behavior is rarely designed with animal welfare in mind. This paper presents a pilot study evaluating a curve-out maneuver, a laterally arcing pass-by trajectory triggered at the dog's alert distance, proposed as a candidate welfare-informed navigation intervention grounded in canine social communication research and legible motion theory. Following a research through design approach, we developed and evaluated a first design iteration of the artifact in a naturalistic home environment with a companion dog. Contrary to our hypothesis, the curve-out produced a more acute stress response than the straight-line baseline. We argue that discontinuous execution limitation, the robot stopping and reorienting multiple times to approximate the arc, undermined legibility, which in itself became a stressor. The pilot surfaces the legibility of robot behavior to non-humans' eyes and proxy stimulus validity as structural challenges for AMI and repositions the design problem for future work.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [95] [WorldBridge: Bridging Video and Action for Task Generalization in World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40748)
*Jun Cen,Yubo Guo,Hao Li,Liangkai Li,Yaoxu Lyu,Meng Wei,Zeqi Xiao,Sizhe Yang,Yuqiang Yang,Yifan Ye,Shanghang Zhang,Duo Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WorldBridge：通过分离的视频专家与动作专家（结构化注意力+联合流匹配）构建World Action Model，并提出"视频泛化+动作泛化"互补视角，量化视频监督与稀疏动作监督对OOD任务迁移的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身模型在物理环境中的广谱任务泛化仍是核心难题，需厘清视频预测与动作落地各自对任务迁移的贡献。

Method: 消融实验：仅ID视频-动作监督→OOD任务加入无动作标签视频→每任务加1条动作标注演示；模型WorldBridge：视频/动作双专家+结构化注意力+联合流匹配，双时间尺度视觉记忆、action forcing、统一数据处理管线（规范化/过滤异构机器人轨迹与自我中心手部数据）。

Result: RoboTwin 2.0 OOD成功率25.07%→55.90%→70.70%；WorldBridge在RoboTwin 2.0全量96.23%、clean→random 80.9%、RoboCasa GR1 TableTop 82.8%、DOMINO Level-1 57.6%，均SOTA。

Conclusion: 更广的视频覆盖可借现有逆动力学能力实现任务迁移，稀疏动作监督进一步强化该能力；视频预测与动作接地互补是泛化关键。

Abstract: Achieving the broad task generalization of embodied models in physical environments remains a central challenge in robot learning. We study this challenge in World Action Models (WAMs) through two complementary capabilities: video generalization, which predicts the visual evolution of new tasks, and action generalization, which translates these predictions into executable behavior through a video-to-action bridge. By varying video and action supervision for tasks outside an initial training set, we examine their respective contributions to task transfer. On RoboTwin 2.0, using only in-distribution video-action supervision yields a 25.07% success rate on out-of-distribution tasks. Introducing videos of these tasks without their action labels raises success to 55.90%, while adding just one action-labeled demonstration per task further improves it to 70.70%. These findings suggest that broader video coverage enables task transfer by leveraging existing inverse-dynamics capabilities, while even sparse action supervision further strengthens those capabilities. Guided by this perspective, we introduce WorldBridge, a WAM with separate video and action experts connected through structured attention and trained with joint flow matching. WorldBridge incorporates visual memory at two temporal scales and action forcing, alongside a unified data processing pipeline that standardizes and filters heterogeneous robot trajectories and egocentric hand-derived supervision. Across multiple manipulation benchmarks, WorldBridge achieves state-of-the-art performance among the compared methods, including 96.23% on RoboTwin 2.0 full data setting and 80.9% on clean to random setting, 82.8% on RoboCasa GR1 TableTop, and 57.6% on DOMINO Level-1. Our study highlights the complementary roles of video prediction and action grounding in developing robots that generalize across tasks.

</details>


### [96] [CaPtain: A Pipeline for Post-Training Code-as-Policy Robot Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40799)
*Yijia Fan,Xilin He,Yilei Jiang,Dongming Wu,Jiaxu Wang,Xiangyu Yue*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CaPtain 是一个开放后训练管线，将小型开源 VLM（Qwen3.5-4B）训练为可直接写代码操作机器人的 Code-as-Policy 智能体，无需 VLA、前沿闭源模型或特权状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 CaP 智能体回避训练：要么依赖闭源前沿模型提示，要么委托给预训练 VLA 与高层技能，小型开源 VLM 完全失败。作者希望证明"SFT+多轮RL"这一通用智能体配方同样适用于机器人代码策略。

Method: 构建 CaPtain-Harness（暴露底层感知、几何与带保护的底层运动原语，仿真与真机共用）；用 GPT-6 Astra 生成合成轨迹做 SFT 对齐基础模型；再用多轮 RL 训练。针对长程信用分配难题，提出"物理评论家"（physical critic）：读取策略不可见的仿真特权状态，按每个请求对预测成功率的改变量分配信用。

Result: LIBERO 留出集成功率从 0%（零样本）→55.4%（SFT）→73.8%（RL）；LIBERO-PRO 达 78.5%，超过多个前沿模型智能体；RL 增益在三个扰动套件上稳健（+17.6~+21.2 点）；无目标域训练下 SimplerEnv WidowX 达 83.3%，CaP-Bench 超越前沿模型；仅仿真适配后可在真机运行。代码、数据、权重全开源。

Conclusion: 小开源 VLM 经合成数据 SFT + 物理评论家辅助的多轮 RL，可成为独立、可部署的 CaP 机器人智能体，且范式可复现、全开源，为机器人智能体后训练提供可推广配方。

Abstract: Across coding, web, and tool-use tasks, language-model agents are now routinely trained with the same recipe: supervised fine-tuning (SFT) to learn the interface, then multi-turn reinforcement learning (RL) to master it. Code-as-policy (CaP) casts robot manipulation in the same form, as the policy writes code against robot APIs and reads back the results, so the same recipe should apply. Yet existing CaP agents sidestep training: they either prompt closed frontier models or delegate manipulation to pre-trained vision-language-action models (VLAs) and high-level skills, while small open vision-language models (VLMs) fail outright. We present CaPtain, an open post-training pipeline that turns a small open VLM into a capable CaP agent, with no VLA, frontier model, or privileged state at test time. CaPtain trains and deploys the policy inside CaPtain-Harness, which exposes low-level perception, geometry, and guarded-motion primitives across simulation and real hardware. We first align the base model via SFT on synthetic trajectories generated with GPT-6 Astra, then apply multi-turn RL. Multi-turn RL must credit tens of agent requests, whose physical effects often surface turns later, from a single end-of-episode outcome. We therefore introduce a physical critic that reads privileged simulator state, never seen by the policy, and credits each request by how much it changes the predicted chance of success. Starting from Qwen3.5-4B (0% zero-shot), CaPtain-4B reaches 55.4% held-out LIBERO success after SFT and 73.8% after RL. On LIBERO-PRO, it reaches 78.5%, surpassing several frontier-model agents, and its RL gains persist across three perturbation suites (+17.6 to +21.2 points). Without target-domain training, it attains 83.3% on SimplerEnv WidowX, exceeds frontier models on CaP-Bench, and runs on a real robot after simulation-only adaptation. We release all code, data, and model weights.

</details>


### [97] [From Teleoperation to Long-Horizon Dexterity: Evaluating Visuomotor Skills and Offline Proxies for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40828)
*Ammar N. Abbas,Aayush Jain,Prashanth Viswanath,Nivesh Gadipudi,Giulia Vilone,Jim Gibbons*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文在真实五指机器人上对281个模仿学习策略（7类策略族）进行约1700次真机RJ45抓取与插接试验，通过受控消融发现观测/动作表征选择远比优化器与采样超参重要，点云ROI裁剪、机器人基座系表示与编码器内归一化各提升70-100个百分点成功率；67个离线指标中仅5个运动平滑/直达性指标与插接成功可靠相关(r=-0.70至-0.80)且仅在单一策略族内成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习策略的真机性能难以预测：验证损失等常规优化信号与机器人成功率无一致关系，策略设计空间（表征、优化器、采样、技能分解等）中究竟哪些选择决定真机表现缺乏系统性、大规模真机证据。

Method: 在五指机器人RJ45连接器抓取+插接任务上开展大规模受控真机实验：281个策略、7个策略族、约1700次真机试验；对观测表征（ROI裁剪、坐标系、归一化）、动作表征（绝对vs相对）、优化器/采样、技能分解、多视角、恢复示范等做消融；并系统评估67个离线指标与真机成功率的关联（相关系数分析）。

Result: 表征维度主导：点云策略中ROI裁剪、基座系表示、点编码器内归一化各带来70-100个百分点成功率提升；VLA策略采用绝对而非相对动作预测使插接成功率最多提升70个百分点；技能分解、多视角与恢复示范与最强系统相关但未独立隔离。67个离线指标中仅5个预测运动平滑性/直达性指标与插接成功率显著相关(r=-0.70至-0.80)，且仅限单一策略族内。传统优化器与采样选择影响远小于表征。

Conclusion: 表征选择是决定真实世界模仿学习性能的关键因素；离线指标可用于筛选候选策略但不能替代真机评测。为具身策略设计提供了可操作的实证准则（坐标系一致性、ROI与归一化、动作空间绝对化、技能分解与多视角）。

Abstract: Imitation learning policies are intended to make robotic manipulation robust to variations in object pose, appearance, and environment that challenge pre-programmed motion. However, real-world performance remains difficult to predict, as conventional optimization signals such as validation loss show no consistent relationship with robot success. To identify the design choices that govern real-world performance, 281 imitation-learning policies spanning seven policy families were evaluated in approximately 1,700 real-robot trials of RJ45 connector grasping and insertion using a five-fingered robotic hand. Controlled ablations showed that observation and action representations dominated conventional optimizer and sampling choices. For point-cloud policies, region-of-interest cropping, representation in the robot-base frame, and normalization within the point encoder each improved success by 70-100 percentage points. For the vision-language-action policy, absolute rather than relative action prediction improved insertion success by up to 70 percentage points. Skill decomposition, multi-view perception, and recovery demonstrations were associated with the strongest-performing systems, although their effects were not independently isolated. Of 67 offline metrics evaluated, only five measures of predicted-motion smoothness and directness reliably correlated with insertion success (r = -0.70 to -0.80), and only within a single policy family. These results identify representation choices as key determinants of real-world imitation-learning performance and show that offline metrics can shortlist policies, but cannot replace real-robot evaluation.

</details>


### [98] [FlapSim: A Composable MuJoCo Simulation Platform and Reduced-Order Aerodynamic Model Hierarchy for Flapping-Wing Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40902)
*Mingshan He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FlapSim：模块化开源 MuJoCo 扑翼机器人仿真平台，在 1 ms 控制步长下用多速率子步同步、三维入流/阵风耦合、叶片元几何缩放和锁相谐波重建，将降阶气动模型平均 wrench 保真度从 0.6593 提升至 0.9646，同时保持约 65–68× CPU 加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扑翼仿真需同时兼顾高频翼拍气动与控制/强化学习所需的低速动态：准稳态叶片元参考（50 μs）精度高但过慢，循环平均降阶模型快却保真度不足。论文旨在刻画降阶气动模型的精度—计算权衡，并给出可复用的开源仿真基座。

Method: 基于 MuJoCo 的确定性模块化流水线（机器人描述 + 翼面几何 + 可组合气动模型）。在 48 个留出场景（频率、万向节、风、频率阶跃、阵风、跨机器人平板）上比较 5 种 1 ms 步长的降阶配置与 50 μs 准稳态叶片元参考。核心改进：多速率 21 子步时钟同步、三维线性/二次入流与阵风耦合、解析叶片元几何缩放、锁相 1f–6f 谐波；并做受控消融与传递函数分析。

Result: 内置悬停循环平均基线：67.51× 加速但保真度仅 0.6593。五模块模型达 0.9646 保真度/65.19× 加速；精简四模块（无显式阶跃瞬态滞后状态）达 0.9662/67.98×。消融显示冲程谐波在频率切换时最关键（频率阶跃保真度 +0.3176）；一旦含相位对齐与谐波合成，显式瞬态滞后状态不再带来额外收益。

Conclusion: 循环平均在固定窗口内仍保留谐波贡献，故窗内重建可同时改善滤波后的 wrench，从而可舍弃瞬态滞后状态。时序对齐、入流建模、几何缩放与谐波表示是低阶扑翼仿真的关键要素；论文给出开源平台与明确的精度—算力层级。

Abstract: Flapping-wing simulation must reconcile high-frequency wingbeat aerodynamics with the slower dynamics relevant to flight control and reinforcement learning. We present FlapSim, a modular open-source MuJoCo simulation platform that combines robot descriptions, wing geometry, and composable aerodynamic models in a deterministic simulation pipeline. Across $48$ held-out scenarios spanning frequency, gimbal, wind, frequency-step, gust, and cross-robot flat-plate regimes, we compare five reduced-order configurations operating at $1\,\text{ms}$ with a $50\,\mu\text{s}$ quasi-steady blade-element reference. The built-in hover cycle-averaged baseline achieves a $67.51\times$ CPU speedup but only $0.6593$ mean wrench fidelity. Combining multirate $21$-substep clock synchronization, three-dimensional linear/quadratic inflow and gust coupling, analytical blade-element geometry scaling, and phase-locked $1f\text{--}6f$ harmonics raises mean fidelity to $0.9646$ at a $65.19\times$ speedup in a five-block model. A streamlined four-block model without explicit step-transient lag states achieves $0.9662$ fidelity at a $67.98\times$ speedup. Controlled ablations show that stroke harmonics are particularly important during frequency transitions, improving held-out frequency-step fidelity by $+0.3176$. Transfer-function analysis explains this effect: fixed-window cycle averaging retains harmonic contributions when the wingbeat frequency changes, so within-cycle reconstruction also improves the filtered wrench. Once phase alignment and harmonic synthesis are included, explicit transient lag states provide no additional benefit in this benchmark. These results characterize the accuracy--computation tradeoff of the aerodynamic hierarchy and identify timing, inflow, geometry, and harmonic representation as central considerations for reduced-order flapping-wing simulation.

</details>


### [99] [Confidence-Modulated Safety Filtering for End-to-End Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40922)
*Javier Borquez,Jaime F. Fisac*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种与安全滤波器无关的运行时管线，用策略自置信度自适应调节滤波保守度：ODD 内高置信度换取性能，ODD 外低置信度增加安全裕度；F1TENTH 仿真初步验证可兼具激进与保守滤波之长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端策略缺乏安全保证需外包安全滤波，但滤波器保守度固定，无法随策略能力变化权衡 ODD 内性能与 ODD 外裕度，导致次优。

Method: 运行时置信度调制：以策略置信度动态设定滤波器保守参数，ODD 内（保证安全）提高性能，ODD 外低置信度时放宽裕度以补偿滤波器模型失配。

Result: 仅 F1TENTH 仿真初步结果，显示调制滤波器综合了激进与保守滤波器的优势；无实车验证。

Conclusion: 策略置信度可作为滤波器保守度的有效调节信号，但证据局限于单一小型仿真平台。

Abstract: End-to-end policies, from task-specific controllers to robot foundation models, learn complex behavior from raw observations but are not guaranteed to remain safe, so they are often wrapped in a safety filter. The resulting system operates under two domains: the filter's operational design domain (ODD), where its assumptions hold and safety is certified, and the policy's competence domain, where it is reliable. Filters typically fix their conservativeness regardless of the policy's competence, trading performance inside the ODD against margin beyond it. We ask whether the policy's confidence should set that level, and propose a filter-agnostic pipeline that modulates it at runtime: inside the ODD, where every setting is safe, confidence buys performance; beyond it, low confidence adds margin, which helps when it flags where the filter's model fails. Preliminary F1TENTH simulations, where this holds, show the modulated filter combining the strengths of permissive and conservative filters.

</details>


### [100] [STAR: Steering Action Representations during Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41009)
*Guangyu Zhao,Yongchang Zhang,Haoxuan Ru,Kaichen He,Haoran Wen,Kun Zhan,Zeyu Zhang,Yin Li,Yiwu Zhong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: STAR：免训练推理方法，通过对VLA后期层残差更新做指数滑动平均（EMA），使动作表征继承早期层的物体感知更新方向，并用本体感受状态自适应调节混合强度，机器人操作任务平均成功率提升4.0个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探针实验发现预训练VLA在动作输出附近的表征未充分保留任务相关的视觉grounding，存在“后层grounding缺口”，导致动作输出缺少物体感知信息。

Method: 训练无关的推理期干预：对后层残差流更新施加EMA，融合早期层的object-aware更新方向；无权重更新与结构改动。利用近期proprioceptive状态估计执行动力学变化，动态变化快时采用保守混合系数。

Result: 在机器人操作基准上提升后层物体感知能力，平均任务成功率相对基线提高4.0个百分点；方法可直接叠加于现有预训练VLA。

Conclusion: 后层表征grounding缺口是可诊断且可低成本修复的；推理期表征steering可在不重训练条件下提升VLA操作性能，但增益幅度中等，缺乏真机大规模验证。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models (VLAs) map language instructions and visual observations to robot actions, showing strong capabilities across robotic manipulation tasks. However, in our probing experiment, we find that action representations near the action output do not fully preserve task-relevant visual grounding. Motivated by this late-layer grounding gap, we introduce STAR, for STeering Action Representations, a training-free inference method for pre-trained VLAs. STAR applies an exponential moving average over late-layer residual updates, allowing later action representations to inherit object-aware update directions from earlier layers without model updates or architectural changes. It further uses recent proprioceptive states to adapt the mixing strength, using conservative mixing when execution dynamics change rapidly. Experiments on robot manipulation benchmarks show that STAR improves late-layer object awareness and increases average task success rates by 4.0 percentage points. Our code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [101] [Contact Imitation Interface: Transferring Dexterous Skills by Showing How Contact Unfolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41028)
*Zhixuan Xu,Yichen Li,Bingchen Wu,Rui Wang,Yiwen Hou,Zixuan Liu,Tianyu Qiu,Lin Shao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CIMI：无机器人人类演示框架，用共享接触几何+指尖RGB视角实现"接触对齐优先"的重定向，并配分层策略（接触面规划器+腕手协调控制器），在LEAP/Wuji六个手-任务对上末端阶段成功率至少提升50个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人演示数据采集成本高，无机器人的人类演示可扩展灵巧操作数据；但难点在于既保留演示者自然灵巧性，又产生可迁移到机器人手的、以接触为基础的观测与动作。

Method: 演示接口：共享接触几何 + 指尖RGB视图，采用"接触对齐优先"的重定向与更广场景视图的重投影；学习接口：分层策略，高阶接触面规划器预测相对接触面轨迹，低阶控制器协调腕部与手指指令以提升接触对齐。

Result: 在LEAP与Wuji两种手、共六个手-任务对上，平均最终阶段成功率相对基线策略提升≥50个百分点。

Conclusion: 将接触基础的观测、重定向与动作表示统一，是把无机器人人类演示迁移到多指灵巧手的一种可行范式，且硬件设计与软件开源。

Abstract: Robot-free human demonstrations offer a promising route to scaling dexterous manipulation data. The key challenge is to preserve the demonstrator's natural dexterity while producing contact-grounded observations and actions that transfer to robot hands. We present Contact Imitation Interface (CIMI), a framework with contact-grounded demonstration and learning interfaces. The demonstration interface combines shared contact geometry and fingertip RGB views with contact-alignment-first retargeting and reprojection of wider scene views. The learning interface introduces a hierarchical policy in which a contact-surface planner predicts relative contact-surface trajectories and a controller coordinates wrist and finger commands to improve contact alignment. CIMI combines contact-grounded observations, retargeting, and action representations, improving mean final-stage success by at least 50 percentage points over baseline policies across six hand-task pairs on LEAP and Wuji. Hardware designs and software are publicly available.

</details>


### [102] [Veloxity: Towards Memory-Safe UAV Embedded Firmware using the Rust Programming Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41053)
*Derek Ward,Brian Bascom,Tyler Larsen,James Usevitch,Phil Tokumaru,Tim McLain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Veloxity：用内存安全语言 Rust 编写的开源嵌入式 UAV 自动驾驶仪，提供最小化、模块化的自主飞行基础，并在仿真、硬件测试台与室外航点飞行中与 C++ 同级自动驾驶仪对比，验证功能对等且性能更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内存安全语言可消除大量历史需高昂代价分析的软件缺陷，但在航空航天、尤其无人机（UAV）领域采纳仍零散；现有 Rust UAV 项目或仅仿真、或闭源、或未与成熟自主生态和现有 C/C++ 固件集成测试，缺少可对比的完整基线。

Method: 开源实现基于 Rust 的嵌入式自动驾驶仪，设计为最小化、模块化架构以兼容现代自主生态；与等效 C++ 自动驾驶仪在仿真环境、硬件测试台、以及室外航点跟随飞行三种场景下进行对照评估。

Result: 在三类测试中实现与 C++ 实现的功能对等，并在性能上有所提升；表明内存安全语言可用于可靠且高性能的自主航空航天固件。

Conclusion: 现代内存安全语言是自主航空航天系统固件的可行选择；Veloxity 降低了在现有生态内采用内存安全语言的门槛，但论文偏系统/语言安全论证，对具身导航与感知方法贡献有限。

Abstract: Recent years have witnessed increased momentum towards memory-safe programming languages for safety-critical systems. Memory-safe languages can eliminate entire classes of common software errors that have historically required extensive analysis and expenditure to avoid. Nevertheless, memory-safe language adoption in aerospace remains fragmented and incomplete, particularly for unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs). Recent Rust UAV projects either run only in simulation, are closed source, or have not been integrated with an established autonomy ecosystem and tested against existing C/C++ firmware. To address this gap, we present Veloxity: an open-source embedded UAV autopilot written in the memory-safe Rust programming language. Veloxity lowers the barrier to using memory-safe languages within modern ecosystems by providing a minimal, modular foundation for autonomous UAVs. We compare Veloxity to equivalent autopilots written in C++ in simulation, with hardware testbenches, and via outdoor waypoint-following flight tests, demonstrating functional parity with improved performance. These results indicate that modern memory-safe languages are a viable option for reliable and performant firmware for autonomous aerospace systems.

</details>


### [103] [Mechanistic Interpretability for End-to-End Self-Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41185)
*Manav Gagvani,Benjamin Namikas,Sivamurugan Velmurugan,Nysa Kumar,Shrey Sharma,Thomas Peterson,Pratyush Mathur,Hyunseo Chang,Rohan Paleja*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Training SAEs on a single-camera end-to-end planner's activations yields interpretable, visually grounded latents that steer trajectory outputs and improve selection via a lightweight adapter.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: E2E driving planners are performant but opaque; need observability/trust.

Method: Sparse Autoencoders on internal activations of a single-camera E2E path planner; unsupervised clustering; paired counterfactual image edits for grounding; latent steering; SAE-latent adapter classifier.

Result: Latents align with route intent (98.3% unsupervised accuracy), speed, braking; respond to traffic light/stop sign; monotonic steering of lateral offset, curvature, braking; adapter cuts trajectory selection error 3.44%.

Conclusion: SAE-based mechanistic interpretability is feasible and useful for autonomous driving planners, giving actionable representations.

Abstract: End-to-end (E2E) autonomous driving models achieve strong planning performance but remain largely opaque, limiting observability and trust. We apply mechanistic interpretability to E2E driving by training Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) on the internal activations of a simple, single-camera end-to-end path planning model. Unsupervised clustering of SAE latents reveals interpretable structure aligned with route intent, vehicle speed, and braking behavior, recovering route intent with 98.3% accuracy without supervision. Through paired counterfactual image edits, we further show that specific SAE features are visually grounded and systematically respond to scene changes such as traffic light state and stop sign presence. Steering individual SAE latents produces predictable, monotonic changes in planner outputs including lateral displacement, curvature, and braking. Finally, we demonstrate a practical application of these representations: we build a lightweight adapter trained on SAE latents that reduces trajectory selection error by 3.44%, outperforming an equivalent adapter that omits SAE latents, confirming that the sparse representation captures useful information for interpretability. Code and visualizations are available at this https URL

</details>


### [104] [A Capability-Aware Backup-Recoverability Filter for Heterogeneous Multi-Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41188)
*Joonkyung Kim,Yanze Zhang,Wenhao Luo,Yiwei Lyu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Capability-Aware Backup-Recoverability Filter (CA-BRF)：在共享笛卡尔操作空间中构造能力相关的 backup-recoverable 集合，通过候选动作与备份动作之间的可行动作插值，实现异构多机器人团队的单步可执行、成对安全控制，并证明递归可行性与安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构多机器人团队面临两个耦合难点：(1) 各机器人制动与转向能力不同，导致规避响应能力受限；(2) 同时多体交互要求单条控制满足多个避碰约束。二者叠加易使局部安全约束与可行控制集不相容，去中心化安全控制器可能无可行解。

Method: 1) 在共享笛卡尔工作空间中，评估每个机器人按其运动学与驱动约束设计的 backup 机动能否在停稳前维持成对安全，从而构造 backup-recoverable 集合，使恢复证书具备能力依赖性；2) CA-BRF 对各机器人候选控制进行过滤，选择候选动作与 backup 动作之间的可行插值，并在同时交互间协同选择，在最大化保留候选动作的同时保证成对恢复条件由单一可执行控制满足。

Result: 理论上给出离散时间闭环系统的递归可行性与安全性保证，即使名义安全约束优化本身不可行仍成立；仿真验证至 30 台异构机器人，实机实验覆盖五类不同运动学模型。

Conclusion: 以"保留认证恢复机动可达性"为核心的安全过滤框架，可有效解决异构多机器人去中心化避碰中的可行性与安全性耦合问题，兼具理论保证与多规模、多机型的实验支撑。

Abstract: Safe navigation in heterogeneous multi-robot teams faces two coupled challenges: differences in robots' braking and turning capabilities limit how each robot can respond to an approaching neighbor, while simultaneous interactions require a single control action to satisfy multiple collision-avoidance constraints. Together, these challenges can make local safety constraints incompatible with a robot's admissible controls, leaving a decentralized safety controller without a feasible action. We propose a Capability-Aware Backup-Recoverability Filter (CA-BRF), based on the insight that navigation actions should preserve access to a certified recovery maneuver. First, we construct a backup-recoverable set in a shared Cartesian operational space by evaluating whether prescribed backup maneuvers maintain pairwise safety until the robots come to rest.
