We propose to let a VLM build a sketch model of the task, i.e., a few geometric primitives and two mate frames, which a VLM agent measures with geometric tools in multi-view depth and which a standard planner and compliant controller execute. Because a sketch describes the parts independently of their placement, the robot needs to build it only once per task. A sketch memory stores each agent run and transfers the stored sketch to later placements by point-cloud registration, so that the VLM is called only for unfamiliar parts. After grasping the part, the robot inspects both mating faces at close range and corrects the sketch by shape matching. On 69 simulated insertion episodes, our method inserts 88% of the parts at the assembled pose in a strict kinematic test, while ReKep and MOKA with the same VLM insert none. On new placements of eight tasks, reusing one stored run inserts as many parts as calling the agent in each episode, and it produces the sketch in 3.4 s instead of about 90 s.

</details>


### [140] [When the Simulator Fails, Not the Policy: Auditing Contact Artifacts in Simulated Robot Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41857)
*Alex Zhou,Junhao Wu,Xueyang Zhou,Yixiao Huo,Hongcheng Gao,Di Zhu,Zhenghao Zhang,Yongchao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文指出仿真benchmark中的失败常源于物理引擎而非策略：PhysX的碰撞检测距离算法丢弃面积阈值以下的三角形，导致厘米级几何被误报约2cm穿透、物体被弹飞数米每秒。作者提出SimuGuard审计插件（覆盖LIBERO/RoboTwin/RoboCasa/RoboDojo），逐物理步记录状态、标记异常速度、逐比特重放并单参数干预，定位根因后仅改该常数，异常回合从12/100降至0，五个容器任务成功率89%→98%。建议报告在异常回合全过/全败时的成功率区间（如LingBot-VA 83.1%对应75.8%–98.5%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真benchmark以成功率评价策略，但失败被一概归咎于策略；作者怀疑部分失败由仿真器自身（碰撞检测/求解器）造成，需要可审计手段区分"策略错"与"仿真器错"，并修正引擎以提升benchmark可信度。

Method: 提出SimuGuard审计插件，不改变评测形式：①每个物理步记录状态，若物体在接触时开始运动且速度超出机器人接触与重力的合理解释，则标记异常；②逐比特重放已记录回合；③单参数改动重跑以做因果定位。据此将RoboTwin上的异常回合追溯到PhysX碰撞检测中的固定三角形面积阈值常量，并做最小改动验证。

Result: 在RoboTwin上，1–2cm间距几何被引擎报约2cm穿透，求解器以数m/s弹出物体；benchmark自带脚本演示也存在同样失败。仅修改该面积阈值常量、不改其他设置：异常回合12/100→0，五个容器任务成功率89%→98%。对LingBot-VA，报告83.1%对应的区间为75.8%–98.5%。

Conclusion: 仿真成功率混入了仿真器造成的失败，benchmark结果存在系统性偏差；PhysX≤5.3.1的benchmark应在修复引擎上重跑，无法修改引擎时应同时报告含异常回合全败/全过边界的成功率区间。方法学贡献是提供了可复用的审计、重放与单参数因果归因流程。

Abstract: Simulation benchmarks score robot policies by success rate, and every failed episode counts against the policy. Many of these failures are the simulator's fault, not the policy's; we find the cause and fix it. Testing a vision language action model and a world action model in simulation, we find that the policy often places the object and the simulator then ejects it, and that the benchmark's own scripted demonstrations fail the same way. We present SimuGuard, an auditing plugin for LIBERO, RoboTwin, RoboCasa and RoboDojo. It leaves the evaluation unchanged. At every physics step, it records the state and flags an object that starts moving at a contact faster than the robot's contact or gravity account for. It replays a recorded episode bit for bit, and it reruns the episode with one simulation parameter changed. With it we trace these episodes on RoboTwin to the collision detection of the physics engine: for geometry one to two centimeters apart, the engine reports a penetration of about two centimeters, and the solver throws the object out at several meters per second. The cause is one constant: the distance algorithm discards triangles below a fixed area threshold, which valid triangles fall below at the centimeter scale. With that constant changed and nothing else, episodes with an artifact fall from 12 in 100 to none, and the success rate on five container tasks rises from 89% to 98%. Success rates in simulation thus count failures that the simulator causes. Benchmarks on PhysX 5.3.1 or earlier should rerun in a repaired engine. Where the engine cannot change, they should report the success rate together with the range it spans when every flagged episode fails or passes: 75.8% to 98.5% around a reported 83.1% for LingBot-VA.

</details>


### [141] [META-Robot: Self-Cognition and Capability Scaling in Agentic Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41868)
*Xuanzhi Feng,Zeyu Liu,Weiheng Gao,Xinchen Li,Jiankun Tang,Fangqi Zhu,Quanxin Shou,Hao Chen,Zhengyang Yan,Hualei Zhang,Junhao Li,Zhiyao Wu,Jie Zhang,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: META-Robot：用动作级谓词（action-level predicates）把执行结果显式化供 LLM 推理，形成“自我认知—准则扩展—技能结构化扩展”闭环，并引入本体感知反馈，在仿真与真机上提升任务表现与泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 机器人智能体虽能生成控制程序，但泛化依赖繁重的脚手架（scaffolding）；单纯扩充技能库既无法评估新能力，也难以识别能力边界。

Method: 提出 META-Robot 框架：以动作级谓词显式化执行结果，供 LLM 评估；智能体据此定位自身局限、扩展评估准则，并结构化地扩展技能集；辅以本体感知（proprioceptive）反馈把过程锚定到机器人物理状态。

Result: 仿真与真实机器人实验显示：整体任务性能更强、对未见任务泛化更好、技能与评估准则可持续扩展，且能有效利用非视觉本体感知信号。

Conclusion: 将“评估准则的扩展”与“技能扩展”耦合的自我认知机制，可作为提升 LLM 机器人智能体泛化性的有效路径。

Abstract: LLM-based robotic agent systems can generate programs for robot control, yet their generalization to new tasks often depends on elaborate scaffolding. Expanding their skill repertoires alone offers limited guidance on how to assess new capabilities or identify what remains beyond their reach. We introduce META-Robot, a framework that couples self cognition with capability scaling by expanding the criteria through which an agent evaluates and develops its skills. At its core, action-level predicates make execution outcomes explicit and available for LLM reasoning. The agent uses these assessments to identify its own limitations, extend relevant evaluation criteria, and guide the structured expansion of its skills. Proprioceptive feedback further grounds this process in the robot's physical state, enriching the evidence available for self-cognition. Experiments in simulation and on real robots demonstrate stronger overall task performance, generalization to new tasks, continued expansion of skills and evaluation criteria, and effective use of non-visual proprioceptive signals.

</details>


### [142] [Movens-Zero: Scaling Humanoid Motion Foundation Models with Large-Scale Human Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41877)
*Wendong Bu,Tinghong Ye,Qizhou Wang,Jiacheng Li,Zhiqi Ge,Yuze Lin,Zhongqi Yue,Yi Su,Jingyu Wang,Kai Shen,Jingdong Liu,Chengyuan Deng,Xiaoxuan Chen,Xin Tan,Jiaming Ji,Wenqiao Zhang,Chenghong Jiang,Tuoyu Li,Siliang Tang,Jun Xiao,Tat-Seng Chua,Yueting Zhuang,Xingxing Wang,Juncheng Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Movens-5M/Movens-Zero/Movens-Intent：从互联网视频构建1500万人类动作与500万可物理执行的人形动作序列（1万+小时，文本/RGB/语音/音乐对齐），并提出可扩展的理解-生成-执行架构与实时闭环执行方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形动作基础模型受限于多模态、机器人可执行数据的稀缺，以及异构多模态联合学习与实时闭环控制难以兼得。

Method: 数据侧采用片段生成、质量过滤、语义标注、动作恢复、物理精修（人体到人形重定向+执行验证）流水线；模型侧 Movens-Zero 采用任务混合 flow-matching 训练单一动作专家覆盖七类生成任务，并用 Multimodal Real-Time Chunking 在持续更新的指令与机器人状态下衔接重叠动作块实现闭环；配套四轨基准 Movens-Intent（8192 对专家校验条件-动作对）。

Result: 执行验证数据从1%扩到100%、动作专家从64M扩到911M参数时，四种模态（文本/语音/音乐/视频）均取得一致增益；数据质量与跨模态监督同时提升生成与执行表现。

Conclusion: 规模化"经执行验证"的数据与统一动作专家容量是提升人形运动基础模型跨模态生成与闭环执行的关键路径，但证据以离线指标与数据缩放曲线为主。

Abstract: Scaling humanoid motion foundation models is constrained by the scarcity of diverse, robot-executable data and by the difficulty of jointly learning from heterogeneous multimodal inputs while supporting real-time closed-loop control. We present Movens-5M, constructed from raw Internet videos through clip generation, quality filtering, semantic annotation, motion recovery, and physical refinement with humanoid retargeting and execution verification. It contains 15 million human-motion sequences and 5 million physically executable humanoid-motion sequences spanning over 10,000 hours, aligned with text, RGB video, speech, and music. Building on this data, we introduce Movens-Zero, a scalable understanding-generation-execution architecture that supports joint learning across modalities and real-time closed-loop execution. Task-mixed flow-matching training jointly optimizes a single action expert across seven generation tasks, enabling direct motion generation from text, speech, music, and video. Multimodal Real-Time Chunking connects overlapping chunks under continuously updated instructions and robot states for continuous closed-loop execution. We further introduce Movens-Intent, a four-track benchmark with 8,192 expert-verified condition-motion pairs across text, speech, music, and video. Experiments show consistent gains across all four modalities when scaling execution-verified data from 1% to 100% and action-expert capacity from 64M to 911M parameters, while demonstrating that data quality and cross-modal supervision improve generation and execution.

</details>


### [143] [Online Learning of Heterogeneous Locomotion Dynamics under Reinforcement Learning Policies for Precise Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41892)
*Tianlin Zhang,Weipeng Xia,Lingwei Zhang,Yiyang Shao,Zhongyu Li,Yun-Hui Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在冻结的预训练运动策略下在线学习闭环浮基动力学，用"污染高斯"模型区分可预测/不可预测响应以消除隐条件导致的异质偏差，结合RFF高斯过程做在线学习，配合MPC实现精确移动操作（末端误差约0.01 m）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复用预训练运动策略做精确loco-manipulation仍属开放问题：冻结策略的隐条件使同一观测状态产生异质响应，直接学习会引入偏差与波动，破坏浮基运动预测与末端跟踪精度。

Method: 以冻结运动策略为对象在线辨识其闭环浮基动力学；用双分量污染高斯近似单一观测下"可预测/不可预测"两类响应，并将该模型嵌入基于随机傅里叶特征（RFF）的高斯过程在线学习器，以估计样本属于可预测分量的概率作为加权进行在线更新；学得动力学后接入MPC做浮基运动预测与精确操作；并给出有限样本误差界以证一致收敛。

Result: 理论：建立在线学习的有限样本一致收敛误差界。实验：仿真与实机表明在多种机械臂构型与任务下末端执行器跟踪一致，误差约0.01 m。

Conclusion: 把策略隐条件造成的观测异质性显式建模为污染高斯混合并做概率加权，可得到一致、无偏的在线动力学估计，是实现预训练运动策略复用与高精度loco-manipulation的有效途径。

Abstract: How to reuse a pretrained locomotion policy for precise loco-manipulation is still an open problem. We propose an online method to learn the closed-loop dynamics under a frozen locomotion policy. The learned model predicts floating-base motion and improves loco-manipulation accuracy. However, unobserved latent conditions of the policy introduce heterogeneous responses, meaning that the same observed state will lead to different motions. Direct learning without accounting for this heterogeneity introduces bias and fluctuations into dynamics estimates, degrading floating-base prediction and end-effector tracking. To address this problem, a contaminated Gaussian model is proposed to approximate the effects of latent conditions. The model uses two Gaussian components to approximate predictable and unpredictable responses under the same observed state. Then, the contaminated Gaussian model is combined with a Gaussian process to construct an online learner using random Fourier features (RFFs). The learner estimates each response's probability of belonging to the predictable component and uses it to weight online updates, capturing predictable responses while reducing bias and fluctuations caused by heterogeneous observations. With consistent and unbiased dynamics estimates, model predictive control (MPC) predicts floating-base motion accurately and enables precise loco-manipulation. A finite-sample error bound is established to ensure uniform convergence in online learning. Simulation and hardware experiments demonstrate consistent end-effector tracking across different manipulator configurations and tasks, with errors of 0.01 m.

</details>


### [144] [Adaptive Coverage Algorithms for Mobile Ad Hoc Networks in Dynamic Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41936)
*Hugo Gandhi,Edwin Meriaux,Antonio Loría*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: I extended a multi-agent exploration/coverage framework to dynamic settings with agent loss and world change (DPOCGAGP), upgrading CADENCE/DADENCE centralized and decentralized algorithms and outperforming a state-of-the-art baseline over 4500+ test cases.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The original Partially Observable Cooperative Guard Art Gallery Problem (POCGAGP) assumes a static world, ignoring real-world unpredictability such as agent failures and environmental change during deployment.

Method: Formulates the Dynamic POCGAGP with two perturbation settings—Agent Loss and World Change—and adapts two state-of-the-art algorithms, centralized CADENCE and its decentralized analogue DADENCE, to handle these dynamic conditions.

Result: Adapted CADENCE/DADENCE outperform another state-of-the-art benchmark algorithm across a test set of over 4500 cases with varied size and structure.

Conclusion: Coordinated multi-agent coverage of unknown spaces can be made robust to dynamic instability; however, evaluation is simulation-only with no physical robot deployment or code release mentioned.

Abstract: This paper extends an existing framework for the coordination of mobile agents in unknown worlds, by considering the possibility of dynamic instability. In the original problem, known as the Partially Observable Cooperative Guard Art Gallery Problem (POCGAGP) ( arXiv:2512.09495 ), the multi-agent system must establish a communication network which completely covers an initially unknown constrained space. However, the POCGAGP fails to account for the unpredictability often seen in real-world deployment. To address this, the dynamic case (DPOCGAGP) introduces two settings which can influence the mobile network during deployment: Agent Loss and World Change, which represent dynamic changes to the world. Within this study, we present advancements to two state-of-the-art algorithms, the Centralized Agent-Directed Exploration for Network Coverage (CADENCE), and its decentralized analogue, DADENCE, to provide solutions in these cases. We then compare their performance against another state-of-the-art benchmark algorithm, and show that the adapted algorithms outperform across our benchmark test set, which comprises over 4500 test cases of varied size and structure.

</details>


### [145] [Language-Conditioned Behavioral Cloning for Autonomous Endoscope Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41978)
*Ariel Rodriguez,Lorenzo Mazza,Ortrun Hellig,Alejandra Toscano,Rayan Younis,Stefanie Speidel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出语言条件行为克隆方法来学习外科医生请求的内窥镜相机引导行为，定义了6种临床相关引导行为并采集235个演示，在体模实验中实现聚焦/全景/停止100%、搜索与跟随80%、水平校正50%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微创手术中自动内窥镜引导可减少持续手动相机控制负担，但期望的相机运动取决于外科医生的即时意图，无法仅从视觉手术场景推断，因此需要可被语言指令条件化的策略。

Method: 语言条件行为克隆（language-conditioned behavioral cloning）：定义6种临床相关的引导行为，采集235条子任务演示数据，训练单一视觉运动策略（visuomotor policy）根据语言指令输出相机运动。

Result: 体模初步实验中：focus、overview、stop成功率100%；search-tip与follow-tip为80%；horizon leveling仅50%。失败模式表明高难度引导任务需要时序上下文信息。

Conclusion: 单一视觉运动策略可学习异构的外科医生请求相机行为，验证了可行性；但高难度任务仍需引入时序上下文，是后续改进方向。

Abstract: Automated endoscope guidance reduces the need for continuous manual camera control during minimally invasive surgery. However, the desired camera motion depends on the surgeon's immediate intentions and cannot always be inferred from the visual operative scene alone. We propose to automate the task using language-conditioned behavioral cloning; we define 6 clinically relevant guidance behaviors and collect 235 subtask demonstrations. In preliminary phantom experiments, our policy achieves 100% success on focus, overview, and stop, 80% on search-tip and follow-tip behaviors, and 50% on horizon leveling. These results demonstrate the feasibility of learning heterogeneous surgeon-requested camera behaviors with a single visuomotor policy, while the observed failure modes motivate future work on adding temporal context for more demanding guidance tasks.

</details>


### [146] [Design and Validation of a Robotic Knee Exoskeleton with Musculoskeletal Simulation-Derived Biological Torque Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41981)
*Rajiv V. Joshi,Jimin An,Ilseung Park,Dawit Lee,Inseung Kang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种全开源、基于现货零件的膝关节外骨骼，其生物力矩控制器完全由仿真（肌肉驱动的模仿策略复现深蹲动作＋力矩蒸馏为仅用板载传感器的估计器）训练得到，无需在目标设备上采集数据，7名受试者深蹲时峰值生物膝关节伸展力矩降低21.5%、股内侧肌激活降低26.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动生物力矩控制器（如比例估计用户自身关节力矩）的估计器通常在目标设备采集的数据上训练，换硬件即需重采数据；现有开源工作分散发布硬件、软件或控制器，仍需在装配后的设备上重复采集，阻碍控制器在共享硬件上的可比较研究。

Method: 硬件：现货组件＋通用制造工具构建的开源膝关节外骨骼。算法：用公开生物力学数据集训练的肌肉驱动模仿策略生成深蹲运动，产出膝关节力矩，再将其蒸馏为仅依赖板载传感器的估计器；全流程在仿真中完成，不在实体设备上采集训练数据。硬件与训练管线一并开源。

Result: 7名健全受试者深蹲实验中，相对无外骨骼条件，峰值生物膝关节伸展力矩降低21.5%，股内侧肌峰值激活降低26.7%（p<0.05）。

Conclusion: 仿真导出的生物力矩控制器可迁移到物理硬件而无需设备专属数据采集；硬件与训练管线同步发布，使研究组可在共享硬件上训练与对比控制器、免除额外数据采集开销。

Abstract: Data-driven controllers have enabled lower-limb robotic exoskeletons to adapt assistance to the user across many tasks. A leading example is biological torque control, which estimates the user's own joint moment from onboard sensors and assists in proportion to that estimate. However, its estimators are trained on data recorded with the target device, so deploying the controller on new hardware requires collecting new data. Existing open-source efforts release hardware, software, or controllers separately, so this data collection must still be repeated on the assembled device. Here, we present an open-source knee exoskeleton, built from off-the-shelf components and widely available fabrication tools, with a biological torque controller derived entirely from simulation. A muscle-driven imitation policy reproduces squatting motions from an open biomechanics dataset, and the resulting knee moments are distilled into an estimator that uses only onboard sensors, so no training data are recorded on the device. In seven able-bodied participants, assistance reduced peak biological knee extension moment by 21.5% and peak vastus medialis activation by 26.7% during squatting, relative to squatting without the exoskeleton (p<0.05). Our results demonstrate that a biological torque controller derived from simulation can transfer to physical hardware without device-specific data collection. Because the hardware and training pipeline are released together, research groups can train and compare knee exoskeleton controllers on shared hardware without additional data collection overhead.

</details>


### [147] [Suspended-Fabric Robotic Manipulation Surfaces: Modelling, Design Space, and Multimodular Extensio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41982)
*Pratik Ingle,Jørn Lambertsen,Kasper Støy,Andres Faiña*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出悬索（separable catenary）模型描述由稀疏竖直直线执行器悬挂的柔性织物操作面，给出正解（执行器高度→面形）与闭式逆解（目标局部坡度→执行器高度），用PID在面上定位物体；并扩展为共享执行器的多模块平台，在2×2原型（9个执行器、1 m²工作面）上实现厘米级物体跨模块传输与0.03 m定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Suspended-fabric机器人操作面（RMS）以稀疏竖直执行器网格降低驱动密度，但缺少两方面的设计方法：(1) 如何设计使物体在整面任意位置均可控；(2) 如何扩展到多模块并使物体在模块间传递、实现并行操作。

Method: 1) 将悬垂织物建模为可分离悬索（catenary）近似，计算高效；2) 推导闭式逆运动学：由期望局部坡度直接解出执行器高度，配合简单PID控制器实现物体位姿/位置控制；3) 由织物长度、模块跨度、执行器行程解析推导可达工作空间，给出最小织物长度下界与设计图表；4) 多模块扩展：相邻模块共享执行器，构成2×2原型（9个执行器）。

Result: 在2×2原型、1 m²工作空间、仅9个执行器条件下，厘米级物体可在模块间传递并定位误差约0.03 m；支持多物体操作，为并行操作奠定基础。

Conclusion: 提供了一个可解析、可逆、便于控制器设计的RMS建模框架与设计准则，并以共享执行器的模块化硬件验证了物体跨模块传输与低驱动密度下的操作能力，是柔性面操作（surface manipulation）方向有工程落地价值的进展。

Abstract: Robotic Manipulation Surfaces (RMS) reposition objects by deforming the surface on which they rest. A recent class of RMS suspends a continuous fabric sheet from a sparse grid of vertical linear actuators lowering the actuator density of such surfaces but how to design such surfaces so that objects can be controlled everywhere on it, and how to extend it beyond a single module so that objects can pass between modules and achieve parallel, remain open. This paper presents a separable catenary model of the suspended sheet that is fast to compute and provides both the surface shape for given actuator heights and the actuator heights for a desired local slope. The closed form inverse lets a simple PID controller position objects on the sheet, and the model relates fabric length, module span and actuator stroke to the reachable workspace, yielding a minimum fabric length bound and design charts for these surfaces. The approach is then extended to a multimodular platform in which neighbouring modules share actuators. On a $2 \times 2$ prototype, we demonstrate that centimetre-scale objects are transferred between modules and positioned within 0.03 m over a $1 m^2$ workspace with only nine actuators, enabling multi-object manipulation and opening the way to parallel manipulation.

</details>


### [148] [Nexus-VLA: Bridging Human and Robot Experiences via a Shared Hierarchical Semantic Latent Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42021)
*Biaoliang Guan,Tingyu Yuan,Wen Ye,Ziming Ji,Xu Yang,Yan Huang,Jihua Zhu,Chaoyang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Nexus-VLA：端到端人-机器人联合训练的混合策略学习框架，通过分层跨具身语义对齐与共享操作隐表示实现人类视频到机器人策略的知识迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模第一人称人类视频含丰富操作经验，但视觉外观、运动学结构与动作空间的具身差异阻碍迁移；现有方法将跨具身知识对齐与机器人策略学习解耦，导致人类知识无法直接、充分利用。

Method: 提出混合策略学习框架：(1) 分层跨具身语义对齐范式，从任务意图到细粒度交互逐步建立共享操作语义；(2) 在该层级结构上设计共享操作隐表示，将多层级共享知识联合编码为可直接用于连续动作生成的内部表征，实现端到端人-机器人联合训练。

Result: 在 RoboCasa-GR1 上达到 80.5% 任务成功率，并在 OOD 设置与真实机器人实验中展现优势，在人类数据利用率、学习效率与跨场景泛化方面表现突出。

Conclusion: 端到端联合训练优于解耦式跨具身对齐，分层语义对齐加共享隐表示是有效的人到机器人策略迁移路径，且具真实机器人验证。

Abstract: Large-scale egocentric human videos provide rich and scalable manipulation experience for robot policy learning, yet substantial embodiment gaps in visual appearance, kinematic structure, and action spaces hinder effective knowledge transfer. Existing methods often decouple cross-embodiment knowledge alignment from robot policy learning, making it difficult for human manipulation knowledge to directly and fully contribute to robot action learning. To address this issue, we propose Nexus-VLA, a hybrid policy learning framework that enables cross-embodiment knowledge transfer through end-to-end human-robot joint training, allowing human manipulation experience to directly contribute to robot policy learning. We first introduce a hierarchical cross-embodiment semantic alignment paradigm that progressively establishes shared manipulation semantics from task intent to fine-grained interactions, enabling more complete use of the manipulation knowledge contained in human videos. Building on this hierarchy, we design a shared manipulation latent representation that jointly encodes multi-level shared knowledge into an internal representation directly used for continuous action generation, improving policy learning efficiency while effectively leveraging human knowledge. Across RoboCasa-GR1, OOD settings, and real-world robot experiments, Nexus-VLA achieves an 80.5% task success rate on RoboCasa-GR1 and demonstrates strong advantages in human-data utilization, learning efficiency, and cross-scenario generalization.

</details>


### [149] [Proof-Carrying Evolution: Black-Box Search, White-Box Flight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42043)
*Amit Jain,Sergio Sanchez-Hurtado,Victor Rodriguez-Fernandez,Richard Linares*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究语言模型能否辅助设计行为可被形式化验证的控制器：提出"证明携带演化"(PCE)框架——搜索器同时提出控制器及其证书，证书检查充当选择信号，仅返回通过验证的候选；部署物为控制律+证书，LLM仅在设计阶段使用。在带速度约束的对接、机动目标追踪、SO(3)姿态控制三任务上与经典搜索/综合对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键控制需要形式化保证，而LLM生成控制器的可靠性无法自动确认；作者希望把LLM的提议能力与可机器检验的证书机制结合，使"仅被验证的产物才可交付"，并检验LLM相较于经典搜索/综合/优化是否真有增益。

Method: PCE：控制器与证书的数学形式固定，提议者只联合选择数值参数；证书检查引导选择，最终输出经认证的控制律。比较4个LLM vs 经典搜索与综合，配对运行、共享提议预算。线性问题在搜索中用精确有理数算术验证；SO(3)上先用浮点检查，再对每个报告的最优个体做精确复核；各方法最佳运行另附Lean 4证明。消融实验保持目标与变异算子固定，检验"保留已认证候选"规则的必要性。

Result: 线性问题上最强模型每轮均胜过标准经典基线，但为其选择同一目标证书的经典优化器可弥补大部分差距；追踪任务的冠军排序在Euler→零阶保持动力学下不保持。SO(3)上无模型能可检测地超过与经典变异混合的CMA-ES；纯LLM提议时一个模型超过CMA-ES、另一个劣于其混合版本。所有搜索中位数仍低于手工调参控制律。对接任务消融中，保留认证候选每次运行都产生新认证设计，去掉该规则则无一例。

Conclusion: LLM的价值主要体现在受证书约束的提议与筛选流程中，而非单纯超越经典优化；大部分优势可被更强的经典优化基线吸收，且优势对动力学建模变化敏感。真正稳健的收益来自"仅保留认证候选"这一演化规则，它保证输出可验证且能持续产出新认证设计。

Abstract: We study whether a language model can help design controllers whose behavior can be formally checked. In proof-carrying evolution (PCE), a search proposes controllers together with their certificates. Their mathematical forms are fixed; a proposer chooses their numerical parameters together. Certificate checks guide selection, and only a certified candidate can be returned. The deployed artifact is a control law and its certificate; the language model is used only during design. We compare four language models with classical search and synthesis on docking under a speed limit, pursuit of a maneuvering target, and attitude control on SO(3), using paired runs and shared proposal allowances. Certificates are checked in exact rational arithmetic during search on the linear problems. On SO(3), the search uses floating-point checks, followed by exact re-verification of every reported champion. The best run of each method also receives Lean 4 proofs of its stated control properties. On the linear problems, the strongest models beat the standard classical baselines on every run, but a classical optimizer that chooses certificates for the same objective closes most of that lead. Among the cited pursuit champions, the ranking does not survive a change from Euler to zero-order-hold dynamics. On SO(3), no model detectably outperforms CMA-ES when mixed with classical mutation. When models write every proposal, one tested model improves on CMA-ES and the other performs worse than its mixed variant. All search medians remain below the hand-tuned law. In a docking control with the objective and mutation operator held fixed, retaining certified candidates yields new certified designs in every run; removing this rule yields none.

</details>


### [150] [A Hybrid Path Planning Framework for Assistive Wheelchair Navigation in Dynamic Pedestrian Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42049)
*Damla Leblebicioğlu,Rachel Barume,Taşkın Padır*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TSP全局任务排序 + 势场(PF)传递全局路径 + RRT/A*启发式局部规划器的混合多目标导航框架，用于动态人群中自主轮椅导航，蒙特卡洛仿真显示TSP-PF-RRT碰撞更少、行程更短。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主轮椅需在含动态障碍与不可预测行人的环境中完成多点停靠，现有多目标导航在动态人类环境下可靠性不足，需兼顾全局任务顺序与局部避障。

Method: 分层混合框架：TSP决定中间站点访问顺序；势场法将全局路径信息编码为引导场；局部规划器（RRT、A*）生成可行路径跟随全局轨迹并重规划避障。实现两种管线TSP-PF-RRT与TSP-PF-A*，在拥挤/极拥挤异构动静态障碍场景做蒙特卡洛仿真对比。

Result: 仿真中两算法均可实现多目标无碰撞导航；TSP-PF-RRT在碰撞次数（更少或无碰撞）与行程距离上优于TSP-PF-A*。未提供真实轮椅平台或真实行人场景实验。

Conclusion: TSP+PF+采样/图搜索局部规划器的组合是低成本、可取的多点导航工程方案，但其增益仅由仿真支持，缺乏真机部署、动态行人预测建模与用户交互验证，属于相邻应用型工作。

Abstract: Autonomous wheelchairs can improve the quality of life for individuals with mobility impairments by providing greater independence. However, reliable navigation especially in human environments is a major challenge due to factors such as dynamic obstacles, unpredictable pedestrian behavior, and the users' need to complete multiple stops along the way before reaching their final destination. This paper presents a hybrid path planning framework for multi-goal navigation in dynamic environments. The framework integrates the Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP) for global task ordering with the Potential Field (PF) method and heuristic local planners such as Rapidly-exploring Random Tree (RRT) and A-star (A*) to achieve collision-free navigation. Within this hybrid framework, TSP determines the visiting order of intermediate stations, PF transfers global path information, and local planners avoid obstacles while guiding the robot to the stations. The local planners generate feasible paths that allow the robot to follow the global trajectory and reroute to avoid static and dynamic obstacles. For this purpose, two algorithms have been developed: (i) TSP-PF-RRT and (ii) TSP-PF-A*. The performance of the proposed algorithms are evaluated using Monte Carlo simulations in crowded and extra-crowded scenarios that contain heterogeneous dynamic and static obstacles. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed hybrid framework enables efficient multi-goal navigation while maintaining safe operation in dynamic environments with humans, with TSP-PF-RRT achieving fewer or no collisions and shorter travel distances.

</details>


### [151] [REA: A Routed Embodied Agent Runtime for Asynchronous Brain-Cerebellum Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42053)
*Pengfei Zhou,Yixing Ma,Zhengfeiyang Zhang,Haoyuan Chen,Zhenglin Wan,Hexin Wang,Wangbo Zhao,Yang You*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: REA是一个统一运行时，让System 2基础模型、System 1具身路由器和System 0反应式策略在“运行策略绝不等待大脑”原则下协同；通过预存成功率的路由记忆、边界触发判官门与共享预算升级阶梯实现有界恢复，在LIBERO-Pro 60任务3000回合达95.9%成功率并减少25%控制步、17%控制时间，并迁移到RoboTwin 2.0、BEHAVIOR-1K与真实Unitree G1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型高层推理强但太慢太贵，不适合做细粒度控制器；VLA与全身控制器快却泛化差。把“慢大脑”和“快小脑”拼在一起看似自然，但现有方法建立在不兼容的栈上、异步协调困难：agent循环里调用VLA会阻塞快执行，VLA在出错时回调agent又会反复触发回退或漏掉静默失败。

Method: 提出REA统一运行时，三层协同：System 2审议式基础模型、System 1具身路由器、System 0反应式策略；核心规则是任何运行中的策略都不等待大脑。具体由三点支撑：(1) 路由记忆，预先存储各策略的实测成功率以替代每次失败时查询大脑；(2) 边界触发判官门，仅在策略执行出现边界/异常时咨询大脑；(3) 共享预算升级阶梯，实现有界、不无限回退的恢复。同一运行时内同时容纳学习型VLA策略与程序化/代码策略。

Result: LIBERO-Pro 60任务、3000测试回合：95.9%成功率，超过自身策略池内最佳单agent基线94.7%，控制步减少25%、控制时间减少17%。RoboTwin 2.0 100回合：VLA策略84%、VLA+code 86%，强于最强单VLA基线MEDex的80%。进一步迁移到BEHAVIOR-1K与真实Unitree G1人形机器人，在对比方法中取得最高观测成功率。

Conclusion: 分层“大脑-路由器-小脑”异步运行时是可行且有收益的具身控制范式；学习型策略与程序化策略可在同一运行时共存，且性能提升主要来自路由与有界恢复机制而非更大模型。真实人形平台结果支持其可迁移性，但对比基线范围与真实场景评测规模有限。

Abstract: Foundation models are becoming remarkably reliable at high-level reasoning, yet they remain too slow and costly to serve as fine-grained controllers. Conversely, fast policies like VLAs and whole-body controllers offer more efficient reflexes, but struggle to generalize. While pairing a deliberative brain (foundation models) with a reactive cerebellum (fast policies) seems natural for embodied systems, two obstacles remain: methods are built and scored on incompatible stacks, and their asynchronous coordination is difficult. For example, an agent that calls the VLA in the agentic loop can stall the fast execution, while a VLA that calls the agent on errors tends to re-trigger fallbacks repeatedly or completely miss silent failures. To address this, we propose REA (Routed Embodied Agent), a unified runtime where a deliberative System 2, an embodied router System 1 and reactive System 0 policies cooperate under a single golden rule - no running policy waits on the brain. Instead of querying the brain at each failure, REA employs a route memory that stores each policy's measured success ahead of time, a boundary-triggered judge gate that consults the brain on policy execution, and a shared-budget escalation ladder for bounded recovery. Evaluated on 3,000 test episodes across 60 LIBERO-Pro tasks, REA reaches 95.9% success and beats the best single-agent baseline in its own policy pool (94.7%) while reducing control steps by 25% and control time by 17%. In a separate 100-episode RoboTwin 2.0 study, REA achieves 84% success with VLA policies and 86% with VLA+code, compared with 80% for the strongest single VLA baseline MEDex. These results demonstrate learned and programmatic policies operating within the same runtime. REA also transfers to BEHAVIOR-1K and a real Unitree G1 humanoid, achieving the highest observed success among the compared methods.

</details>


### [152] [ROOF: Learning Humanoid Fall Recovery under Overhead Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42097)
*Yidan Lu,Jiayuan Yu,Zongrui Cai,Jingwen Sun,Jinrui Han,Jing Wang,Ping Luo,Peng Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出带接触支撑几何逃逸引导的强化学习方法，使摔倒的人形机器人在头顶障碍物下自主让出空间并稳定起身；仅用本体感受，仿真中"仅可抬起"板下成功率88.5%（无引导仅18.9%），Unitree G1 真实场景150次中恢复95次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人跌倒于低矮障碍（如家具下方）时存在耦合难题：需先创造净空再组织支撑起身，过早起身反而加剧与障碍的重叠；纯RL在稀疏奖励下难以发现这种"先让空、后起身"的时序策略。

Method: 在RL中引入接触支撑的几何逃逸引导：以"机器人-障碍重叠体积的增量减少"作为奖励信号，但不规定具体逃逸轨迹；结合学习到的运动先验，以及随姿态变化的支撑代价与关节堵转代价，塑造单一策略，隐式决定何时清障、如何建立支撑、何时完成起身；全部基于本体感受，无外部感知。

Result: 仿真中"仅可抬起"板场景恢复率88.5% vs 无几何引导18.9%；随机尺寸/质量/摆放的自由板场景98.6%；Unitree G1 上七个真实场景（含训练未见的家具与多物体组合）150次尝试恢复95次。

Conclusion: 以几何重叠缩减为稠密引导、配合支撑与堵转代价塑形，可在无外部感知下学到清障—支撑—起身的统一策略，并具备跨场景sim-to-real泛化能力。

Abstract: A fallen humanoid beneath an overhead obstacle faces a coupled recovery problem. It must create clearance while organizing support for a stable rise, since attempting to stand before making room can further increase obstruction. We introduce a reinforcement learning method with contact-supported geometric escape guidance, which rewards incremental reduction of robot-obstacle overlap without prescribing an escape path. To achieve this, a learned motion prior, together with posture-dependent support and joint-stall costs, is proposed to shape a single policy that learns when to clear the obstacle, how to establish support, and when to complete the rise, all using proprioception alone. In simulation, it recovers in 88.5% of trials beneath plates that can only be lifted, compared with 18.9% without geometric guidance, and in 98.6% of trials beneath free plates with randomized size, mass and placement. On a Unitree G1, the deployed policy recovered in 95 of 150 attempts across seven real-world obstacle scenes, including furniture and multi-object arrangements absent from training. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [153] [RoboArchitect: Automating the Design of Robotic Agents with Persistent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42141)
*Shengguang Wu,Suning Huang,Cherie Ho,Ria Doshi,Hao Zhu,Serena Yeung-Levy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboArchitect 让编码模型观察冻结机器人策略的 rollout、诊断失败并迭代改写 agent harness 控制代码，在不动任何权重的情况下把未见场景成功率从 9.7% 提升到 43.3%，未见任务从 12.7% 提升到 40.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 agent harness（决定何时查询策略、调用视觉工具或直接控制机械臂的控制代码）对冻结策略增益显著，但完全靠人工搭建且每个机器人/benchmark 都要重新设计；作者希望证明 harness 设计本身可被自动优化，作为替代微调的强方案。

Method: 以编码大模型为优化器：在若干 episode 上观察冻结策略的 rollout，诊断失败模式，迭代修订 harness 控制代码；harness 在固定 episode 集上优化、在留出场景与任务上评估；全程不更新模型权重。harness 还可演化出持久记忆（如有效抓取深度）并被后续决策复用。

Result: 跨多个 benchmark 与策略，未见场景平均成功率 9.7%→43.3%（4.5x），未见任务 12.7%→40.2%（3.2x）；分布偏移越大增益越明显，包裹通用策略后常超过微调版本；自动涌现出“抬臂解卡”“遇上方障碍改水平伸入”等控制策略。

Conclusion: 机器人操作的主要提升空间可能不在策略权重而在 agent harness，且其设计可由编码模型自动完成，无需权重更新；harness 记忆也能持续累积经验。

Abstract: Recent work shows that a frozen robot policy performs far better inside an agent harness, where control code decides when to query the policy, consult vision tools, or command the arm directly. Yet today these harnesses are built by hand and redesigned for each new robot or benchmark. We show that harness design can be automated, offering a strong alternative to finetuning on a novel setup. In RoboArchitect, a coding model observes a robot's rollouts, diagnoses failures, and iteratively revises the harness around the frozen policy. The harness evolves on a set of episodes for its optimization and is evaluated on held-out scenes and tasks. Without updating any model weights, RoboArchitect raises average success from 9.7% to 43.3% on unseen scenes (4.5x) and from 12.7% to 40.2% on unseen tasks (3.2x), across multiple benchmarks and policies. The gains are largest where the benchmark is far from the policy's training data: wrapping a general-purpose policy, the evolved harness yields a specialist that often outperforms even the finetuned version. Intuitive control strategies are automatically designed, such as lifting a jammed arm clear or reaching in horizontally when an obstacle blocks the way from above. The harness also evolves its own persistent memory, carrying learned experience, such as effective grasp depths, into later decisions. These results suggest that substantial headroom in robotic manipulation lies in the agent harness, and its design can be effectively automated and optimized without weight updates.

</details>


### [154] [Hierarchical World Models with Implicit Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42146)
*Gaoyue Zhou,Yvonne Wu,Zichen Jeff Cui,Nicolas Ballas,Mido Assran,Lerrel Pinto,Yann LeCun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ID-WM 提出分层世界模型，将状态空间可达性与底层控制解耦：高层模型在状态流形上采样可行转移，低层世界模型作为逆动力学求解器把转移落地为精确动作，从而以短片段合成新颖的长程规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程任务中世界模型规划受累积误差与搜索复杂度制约；现有分层方法采用刚性宏动作或潜技能，导致表征脆弱、缺乏细粒度控制。

Method: 分层世界模型：高层采样状态流形上的可行子目标转移（不受底层执行细节约束），低层世界模型充当逆动力学求解器，将状态转移接地为精确动作；优先考虑局部物理可行性而非全局行为模式。

Result: 在三个导航与操作测试集上较 SOTA 策略与扩散式规划器提升 38.5%；相比单层世界模型 MPC，在复杂未见过配置的长程任务上提升 135%。

Conclusion: 解耦可达性与局部控制可有效缓解复合误差并降低搜索复杂度，从短片段组合出超越训练行为模式的长程规划。

Abstract: Planning with world models enables test-time adaptation for embodied agents, offering superior generalization over direct policy learning. However, long-horizon tasks remain hindered by cumulative errors and search complexity. Existing hierarchical methods often use rigid macro-actions or latent skills, leading to brittle representations and limited fine-grained control. We introduce ID-WM (Implicit Dynamics World Models), a hierarchical world model that decouples state-space reachability from local control. Our framework utilizes a high-level model to sample feasible transitions across the state manifold and a low-level world model as an inverse dynamics solver to ground these transitions into precise actions. This allows the planner to identify reachable subgoals without being constrained by low-level execution details. By prioritizing local physical feasibility over global behavioral patterns, ID-WM synthesizes novel long-horizon plans from short segments, transcending the specific behavioral modes seen during training. We demonstrate its effectiveness across three navigation and manipulation suites, achieving a 38.5% improvement over state-of-the-art policy and diffusion-based planners. Crucially, by leveraging this hierarchical structure to mitigate compounding errors, our approach yields a 135% performance gain over single-level world model MPC, particularly in complex, unseen configurations for long-horizon tasks. Videos can be viewed at this https URL

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [155] [One Verdict Is Not Enough: Regression Testing Learned World Models and Their Test Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41130)
*Junyu Huo,Zihao Wan,Ziqi Mao,Gouri Ginde*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 TetraState 回归测试协议：对成对的 possible/impossible 视频测试，在四种模型原生配置下运行，按正确判定数分四类结果，单独报告不确定与执行失败，并以逐测试结果转移比较版本迭代，同时将评测框架（harness）纳入被测系统版本管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已学习世界模型以软件组件形式发布，其模型版本、预处理与测试框架在版本间变化；现有负翻转（negative flip）统计只看准确率掩盖的退化样本，仍存在两个盲点：(1) 同一测试在不同配置下结论不一致；(2) harness 故障可在不崩溃的情况下产生错误分数与错误结论。

Method: 成对 possible/impossible 视频测试协议；每个测试在四个固定的模型原生配置下执行，依据正确判定数目划为四类结果；不确定测试与执行失败单独报告，并与判定率并列；版本间以逐测试的结果状态转移进行对比；harness 与被测系统一同版本化。

Result: 在 IntPhys 2 Main 全部 506 个测试及两个异构系统（Cosmos 3 Nano、V-JEPA 2）上：两个 V-JEPA 2 harness 修订仅使众数准确率变化 0.2 与 2.6 个百分点，却分别改变 11 与 214 个测试标签；第二种情况下准确率上升但被正确判定的测试数下降，且 43 个回归仍保持众数判定，故仅检查众数翻转会漏检这些回归。

Conclusion: 准确率与众数翻转检查对配置差异与 harness 故障均不敏感；需建立面向学习型组件的配置级回归判定准则（research agenda）。

Abstract: Learned world models ship as software components whose model versions, preprocessing, and test harnesses change between releases. Work on model updates already counts per-sample regressions, or negative flips, that an accuracy check hides. Two problems remain: a test can pass under one configuration and fail under another, and a harness fault can produce erroneous scores and wrong verdicts without a crash. We present TetraState, a regression-testing protocol for paired possible/impossible video tests. Each test runs under four fixed model-native configurations; its number of correct verdicts assigns one of four outcome classes. Inconclusive tests and failed executions are reported separately alongside the verdict rate. Revisions are compared by per-test outcome transitions, and the harness is versioned as part of the system under test. On all 506 IntPhys 2 Main tests and two heterogeneous systems, Cosmos 3 Nano and V-JEPA 2, two V-JEPA 2 harness revisions move modal accuracy by only 0.2 and 2.6 points yet change 11 and 214 test labels; in the second, accuracy rises although fewer tests are judged correctly, and 43 regressions keep the modal verdict, so a modal-verdict flip check misses them. These results outline a research agenda on configuration-level regression oracles for learned components.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.QM'></div>

# q-bio.QM [[Back]](#toc)

### [156] [A small HER2 reader for routine H&E slides: real-world deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40662)
*Thang Tran,Lan Dang*

Main category: q-bio.QM

TL;DR: 构建22M参数ViT-S/16组织切片编码器（自监督+从H-optimus-0蒸馏+两轮JEPA精炼），配5折注意力切片分类器，做常规H&E上的HER2状态读取器，作为病理医生的预读基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HER2状态决定靶向治疗资格，IHC 2+ 疑难病例需等待ISH确认，成本高、周期长；希望用常规H&E切片端到端预测HER2状态，为病理医生提供预读并帮助对疑难病例优先排序ISH。

Method: 22M参数ViT-S/16 tile编码器，在公开TCGA切片上自监督训练，并从H-optimus-0蒸馏、两轮JEPA精炼；5个交叉验证的注意力式WSI分类器概率平均；在训练集上固定操作阈值；统一配对十种子协议比较；复现PathBench编码器基准；外部医院验证含per-laboratory分数归一化。

Result: 外部验证TCGA-BRCA→Yale-HER2 AUC 0.812，7个编码器中排名第一，与4倍和50倍规模编码器持平；复现PathBench排序，本编码器在TCGA-BRCA 0.689居首、Yale-HER2 0.897与H-optimus-0/Phikon持平；部署在Yale-HER2 0.82、HEROHE 0.75、BCNB穿刺活检0.63；HEROHE 85例IHC 2+中区分ISH扩增AUC 0.76；单张切片无GPU需5-6秒。

Conclusion: 小规模专用病理编码器可在HER2预测上媲美大模型，可作为研究用途的预读工具嵌入病理平台，辅助疑难IHC 2+病例的ISH优先级排序；权重、逐切片预测与图表数据均公开。

Abstract: HER2 status decides who receives HER2-targeted therapy. Pathologists score HER2 protein by immunohistochemistry (IHC); equivocal IHC 2+ tumours, their hardest calls, wait for in situ hybridisation (ISH). We built a HER2 reader for routine H&E slides, owned end to end, as a baseline for pathologists. A 22M-parameter ViT-S/16 tile encoder was trained on public TCGA slides by self-supervision, distillation from H-optimus-0 and two rounds of JEPA refinement; five cross-validated attention-based slide classifiers average their probabilities; and an operating point is fixed on training data. Compared under one paired ten-seed protocol, readers on our encoder rank first of 7 on external validation from TCGA-BRCA to Yale-HER2 (AUC 0.812), level with readers on encoders 4 and 50 times larger. We also rebuilt PathBench's encoder benchmark on public cohorts; our replica reproduces its published order of reference encoders, and under it our encoder ranks first of 7 on TCGA-BRCA (0.689) and sits level with H-optimus-0 and Phikon on Yale-HER2 (0.897). On three external hospitals our deployed reader reaches AUC 0.82 on Yale-HER2 (about 0.81 with training slides balanced for tissue size), 0.75 on HEROHE and 0.63 on BCNB biopsies, and among HEROHE's 85 IHC 2+ cases it ranks ISH-amplified above non-amplified tumours at 0.76, best among our TCGA-trained readers. Per-laboratory score normalisation and a laboratory's own slides improve it further on a new site. Without a GPU our reader needs 5-6 s per slide. It serves as a research-use pre-read in a pathology platform, where it proposes a probability and a call beside IHC and helps prioritise ISH for equivocal cases. Weights, per-slide predictions and the data behind every figure are public at this https URL .

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [157] [Defining Page-Centric Visual Document Retrieval and Evaluating Existing Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41360)
*Chongzhi Zhang,Yuqi Zuo,Aixin Sun*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出以页面为索引单元的“以页为中心的视觉文档检索”(VDR)形式化框架，并系统梳理评估了42个基准，指出现有评测集中在页级独立检索、缺乏真实用户需求查询与分级相关性标注，给出基准选择指引与未来设计优先级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代文档（Word、PDF、幻灯片）是视觉丰富、分页的结构化序列，而纯文本检索系统要么忽略这种结构，要么通过任意切块破坏结构；同时缺乏对VDR任务形式与评测协议的系统性对齐分析。

Method: 概念形式化 + 基准调研分析：将VDR定义为以页面为基本索引单元、可利用文档上下文判断页相关性的检索任务，区分页级/文档级两种粒度与独立项、证据集、迭代三种检索范式；随后按语料特性、查询构造、相关性标注、排序支持四方面共八项标准评估42个基准。

Result: 发现两点主要问题：(1) 评测集中于页级独立项检索，文档级、证据集与迭代设置资源稀缺；(2) 独立于目标语料、源自真实用户信息需求的查询与分级相关性标注少见，相关性标注的覆盖度与可验证性差异显著，暴露了应用能力需求与现有评测协议可确证能力之间的差距。

Conclusion: 提供基准选择指南，并提出未来基准设计的优先方向，强调任务需求、相关性语义与评测协议三者之间的对齐。

Abstract: In modern information retrieval (IR), documents are increasingly encountered as structured sequences of visually rich, paginated units, rather than as flat text. Example documents encountered in our daily life include Word files, PDFs, and slide decks. Free text-centric retrieval systems either ignore this structure entirely or destroy it through arbitrary passage chunking. In this paper, we formulate page-centric Visual Document Retrieval (VDR) with pages as the fundamental indexing units and document context available for assessing page relevance. The formulation distinguishes two retrieval granularities, page and document, and three retrieval paradigms: independent-item, evidence-set, and iterative retrieval. We then assess 42 benchmarks using eight criteria covering corpus characteristics, query construction, relevance annotation, and ranking support. Our analysis shows two main findings on current VDR research. First, evaluation remains concentrated on page-level independent-item retrieval, with fewer resources supporting document-level, evidence-set, and iterative settings. Second, queries originating from real user information needs independently of target corpus content and graded relevance judgments are uncommon, while the coverage and verification of relevance judgments vary substantially. Both findings reveal gaps between the capabilities applications require and those existing evaluation protocols can establish. We provide benchmark selection guidance and identify priorities for future benchmark design, emphasizing alignment between task requirements, relevance semantics, and evaluation protocols.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [158] [Consensus Within, Competition Between: Decentralized LLM Teams for Perimeter Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41044)
*Khawaja Abaid Ullah,Ananya Acharya,Mohammad Javad Khojasteh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在简化周界防御仿真中比较集中式LLM指挥官、基于拍卖共识的多智能体团队与独立智能体在五种通信条件下的表现，并考察视觉伪影混淆与提示预警对LLM决策的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM协调多智能体团队时，任务分配应集中式还是分布式、通信不可靠时如何取舍，缺乏实证依据。

Method: 玩具级周界防御仿真（对手亦由LLM控制），对比中央指挥官、拍卖式共识团队、独立智能体三种架构在五种网络条件下（含有限无线电覆盖）的突破率；替换低成本LLM参与投标计算；向文本状态中额外注入鸟类/民用无人机照片，并测试伪装为标本鸟无人机的入侵者及一句提示前警语的效果。

Result: 突破率：共识团队15%、指挥官32%、独立智能体29%；仅有限无线电范围会改变团队突破率（使指挥官更差）；低成本LLM参与投标计算使突破率上升38个百分点。仅前沿级模型能区分鸟类/民用无人机与入侵者；伪装入侵者在近前沿模型中被放行比例达59–61%（原为1–16%），加一句前警语后降至0–25%。

Conclusion: 配备专用外部冲突解决协议的LLM团队突破率最低；LLM对未见视觉分布的鲁棒性依赖提示先验。

Abstract: The use of large language models (LLMs) to coordinate multi-agent teams raises a fundamental architectural question: whether task allocation should be centralized in a single LLM controller or distributed across autonomous LLM agents, particularly when communication is unreliable. In a deliberately simplified perimeter-defense simulation against LLM-controlled intruders, we compared a central commander, an auction-based consensus team and independent agents under five network conditions. Breach rates were 15% for the consensus team, 32% for the commander and 29% for independent agents, and of the different network conditions, only limited radio range changed a team's breach rate, raising the commander's. In consensus team, giving a low-cost LLM more authority over the bid computation raised breaches by 38 percentage points. With birds and civilian drones in airspace, we added a photograph for each to the input text state. Only the near-frontier model treated them apart from intruders. Intruders disguised as existing taxidermy bird drones were mistook as birds by both low-cost and near-frontier LLMs, going unpursued in 59-61% of near-frontier decisions (1-16% before). However, a one-sentence forewarning in the prompt lowered it to 0-25%. We find that LLM team with a dedicated external conflict resolution protocol results in fewest breaches. Code: this https URL .

</details>


### [159] [ReuScope: Auditing Data Reuse and Evaluation Sensitivity in Capsule Vision 2024](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41835)
*Eunseob Choi,Kyeonghun Kim,Hyuk-Jae Lee,Nam-Joon Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ReuScope协议，用于审计数据复用与评测敏感性。以Capsule Vision 2024为案例：发现公开训练/验证划分间存在字节完全相同的文件，Kvasir-Capsule全部41段源录像同时出现在两个子集中；移除训练中Kvasir-Capsule相关行后，验证平衡准确率从82.5%降至61.2%，但在其他来源验证行上反而提升1.38个百分点，说明该下降衡量的是"对源移除的敏感性"而非泄漏导致的虚高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据泄漏审计通常只报告"重叠存在"或单一得分下降，缺乏区分"观测到的数据复用"与"条件性能敏感性"的规范流程，也缺少对评测population、类别支撑集与不确定性单位的明确界定，导致结论易被误读为泄漏引发的性能虚高。

Method: 构建ReuScope协议，结合数据关系度量（文件哈希、源录像级跨划分重叠、患者标识）与两组对照比较：固定预测改变评测population、固定评测改变训练数据。做去除实验：在10个配对随机种子上，剔除所有归于Kvasir-Capsule的训练行及其他来源中5个近匹配行；在原始验证行与其他来源验证行上分别评测；加入匹配对照排除样本量与类别计数解释；另做探索性实验，移除验证帧的近期训练邻居（或等量远端行），以双重差分估计对真实类概率的影响并给出配对t区间。

Result: 公开验证集平衡准确率由82.5%降至61.2%；同一批模型在其他来源验证行上，去除训练数据后平衡准确率反而提升1.38个百分点，比较方向被反转。匹配对照显示样本量与类别计数不能解释差异。探索性双重差分估计相对匹配未标记帧为-0.355个百分点真实类概率变化，准确率的配对t区间跨越零。

Conclusion: ReuScope将"观测到的数据复用"与"条件性能敏感性"分离，并为每条结论显式指定population、类别支撑集与不确定性单位；公开验证集下降应解读为对源移除的敏感性，而非泄漏引起虚高的量值。

Abstract: We develop ReuScope, a protocol for auditing data reuse and evaluation sensitivity, using Capsule Vision 2024 as a case study. It combines measurements of data relationships with two comparisons: changing the evaluation population while holding predictions fixed, and changing training data while holding evaluation fixed. Byte-identical files cross the public training-validation boundary, and all 41 Kvasir-Capsule recordings that contribute frames occur in both partitions. Shared source recordings establish cross-split overlap; missing cross-recording patient identifiers prevent counting unique shared patients. Across ten paired seeds, removing all training rows attributed to Kvasir-Capsule, plus five rows from other sources flagged as near-matches to it, lowers public-validation balanced accuracy from 82.5% to 61.2%. Evaluating the same fitted models on the validation rows of the other sources reverses the comparison: removal improves balanced accuracy by 1.38 percentage points. The public-validation drop measures sensitivity to source removal, not an amount of leakage-induced inflation. Matched controls show that sample size and class counts alone do not explain the observed score differences. An exploratory experiment removes the training neighbors of validation frames that have close training matches, or equally many distant training rows, and yields a difference-in-differences estimate, relative to matched unflagged frames, of -0.355 percentage points in true-class probability; the paired-t interval for accuracy spans zero. The resulting procedure distinguishes observed reuse from conditional performance sensitivity and specifies the population, class support and uncertainty unit for each conclusion.

</details>


### [160] [Sensorium: An Open-Source Multimodal Data Collection and Curation Library for Embodied Surgical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41946)
*Lorenzo Mazza,Ariel Rodriguez,Stefanie Speidel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Sensorium：基于ROS/ROS2的开源Python库，用于多模态手术数据的采集、处理、语义标注与episodic数据集构建，在1516次体模/离体/活体猪实验上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身手术AI依赖可靠多模态数据，但手术数据采集成本高、难复现、强依赖硬件配置，缺乏统一的采集-处理-标注-整编工具链。

Method: 开源Python库，构建于ROS与ROS2中间件之上，支持可配置的机器人与传感器流；集成面向操作者的数据采集、可配置处理管线、语义标注与episode级数据集生成四个模块。

Result: 在1516次记录（体模、离体、活体猪，多任务多传感配置）上完成验证，并给出初步可用性反馈。

Conclusion: 提供了面向具身手术AI的工程化多模态数据基础设施，可作为相邻领域数据采集工具参考，但缺乏算法层面的方法贡献与量化下游收益。

Abstract: Embodied surgical AI requires reliable multimodal data, yet surgical data collection remains costly, difficult to repeat, and strongly dependent on the hardware setup. We present Sensorium, an open-source Python library for recording, processing, annotating, and curating multimodal surgical data from configurable robot and sensor streams using the Robot Operating System (ROS) and ROS2 middleware. The library combines operator-facing data acquisition, configurable processing, semantic annotation, and episodic dataset generation. We evaluate Sensorium across 1516 recordings of phantom, ex vivo, and in vivo porcine experiments involving multiple surgical tasks and sensing configurations, and report preliminary usability feedback.

</details>


### [161] [CausalWAM: Learning the Causes of Actions in World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42096)
*Juntao Cheng,Jinzhou Tang,Fan Feng,Henrik I. Christensen,Yujia Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CWAM（Causal WAM）：在预训练世界动作模型的动作头上加一条稀疏路由路径，使生成动作所依赖的隐变量仅为"动作相关隐变量"的可逆函数，理论证明在稀疏约束下结构可识别（至多差一个置换），且在合成数据与真实机器人上验证并提升闭环成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAM）从观测中学习隐变量，既要预测整场景演化，又要生成动作；但只有部分场景因素真正导致动作。拟合演示数据的WAM无法保证仅从动作相关隐因生成动作，可能把动作相关隐变量与冗余变量纠缠在一起，从而产生虚假相关并损害泛化与闭环可靠性。

Method: 将"导致动作的因素"与"预测所依赖的因素"分离，在稀疏性约束下建模动作生成路径；理论上证明此时用于生成动作的隐变量是动作相关隐变量的可逆函数，真实结构可识别到隐变量置换等价，且无需充分的时间依赖性假设、无需对未观测regime演化方式作假设，并允许动作相关隐变量与其余变量统计相关。实现上不改动世界建模目标，仅在预训练WAM的动作头加入稀疏路由（sparse routing）路径，作为轻量后接约束。

Result: 合成数据实验验证了可识别性理论；真实机器人实验进一步佐证，并显示闭环任务成功率与效率均有提升。

Conclusion: WAM的动作生成通道需要显式因果/稀疏约束才能避免隐变量纠缠；以最小侵入方式将稀疏路由加在动作头上即可获得可识别的动作相关表征，并带来实际闭环收益。可作为世界模型用于策略学习的通用正则化手段，后续可关注其在不同WAM骨干、长时序任务与真实部署中的稳定性。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) learn latent variables from observations and use these to both predict the evolution of the scene and to generate actions. The prediction has to be of the whole scene, whereas only a portion of the scene causes actions. WAMs fitted to demonstrations do not, in general, guarantee that they will generate actions solely from the latent causes of the demonstrated actions (the action-relevant latent variables), as the model could just as well learn to imitate in this way whilst entangling the action-relevant latent variables with the nuisance variables. We therefore separate out what causes action from what prediction depends on. We prove that under a sparsity constraint the latent variables used to generate actions are an invertible function of the action-relevant latent variables alone and the true structure is identified up to a permutation of the latent variables. This requires neither sufficient temporal dependence nor any assumptions on how the unobserved regimes evolve and holds even when the action-relevant latent variables are statistically dependent on the rest. We therefore propose CWAM (Causal WAM), which incorporates the constraint into a pretrained WAM by adding a sparse routing path to its action head, with the world modeling objective remaining unchanged. Experiments on both synthetic data and real-world robots validate the theory as well as showcase the improved efficiency in closed-loop success.

</details>
